prathamaḥ sargaḥ

avyātsa vo yasya nisargavakraḥ spṛśatyadhijyasmaracāpalīlām  |
jaṭāpinaddhoragarājaratna marīcilīḍhobhayakoṭirinduḥ  || 1.1 ||

jaṭāhiratnadyutipāṭalo'vyātsa vaḥ śaśī śaṅkaramauliratnam  |
śrutāvaśokāṅkurakautukena yaṃ kartumicchatyacalendrakanyā  || 1.2 ||

kumbhasthalī rakṣatu vo vikīrṇa sindūrareṇurdviradānanasya  |
praśāntaye vighnatamaśchaṭānāṃ niṣṭhyūtabālātapapallaveva  || 1.3 ||

cakṣustadunmeṣi sadā mukhe vaḥ sārasvataṃ śāśvatamāvirastu  |
paśyanti yenāvahitāḥ kavīndrāstriviṣṭapābhyantaravarti vastu  || 1.4 ||


__________________________________________

prācīnakavivarṇanam

tattvaspṛśaste kavayaḥ purāṇāḥ śrībhartṛmeṇṭhapramukhā jayanti  |
nistṛṃśadhārāsadṛśena yeṣāṃ vaidarbhamārgeṇa giraḥ pravṛttāḥ  || 1.5 ||

pūrṇendubimbādapi sundarāṇi teṣāmadūre purato yaśāṃsi  |
ye bhartṛmeṇṭhādikavīndrasūkti vyaktopadiṣṭena pathā prayānti  || 1.6 ||

sarasvatīkalpalataikakandaṃ vandāmahe vākpatirājadevam  |
yasya prasādādvayamapyananya kavīndracīrṇe pathi sañcarāmaḥ  || 1.7 ||


divaṃ yiyāsurmama vāci mudrāmadatta yāṃ vākpatirājadevaḥ  |
tasyānujanmā kavibāndhavasya bhinatti tāṃ saṃprati sindhurājaḥ  || 1.8 ||


__________________________________________

kaveḥ śālīnatādi

naite kavīndrāḥ kati kāvyabandhe tadeṣa rājñā kimahaṃ niyuktaḥ  |
kiṃ vālukāpavartake dhareyamāropyate satsu kulācaleṣu  || 1.9 ||

aho mahatsāhasametadeva yadvarṇaṇīyo navasāhasāṅkaḥ  |
dūre paricchedakathā hi satyametadguṇānāmudadherapāṃ ca  || 1.10 ||

bhaktyāthavāsyaiva mama prabandhe sūkṣmo'yamunmīlati śaktileśaḥ  |
ullaṅghito yatkapinā payodhiḥ sevānubhavaḥ sa raghūdvahasya  || 1.11 ||

samatsare cetasi durjanānāṃ na jātucitsūktiguṇo guṇāya  |
nisargakṛṣṇendravadhūkapole nirarthakaḥ kuṅkumapattrabhaṅgaḥ  || 1.12 ||

kimanyadasyāścaritairnṛpasya muktāvadātaiḥ kṛtamaṇḍanāyāḥ  |
madīyasūktermukulībhavantu svabhāvaśuddhāni satāṃ manāṃsi  || 1.13 ||

namo'stu sāhityarasāya tasmai niṣiktamantaḥ pṛṣatāpi yasya  |
suvarṇatāṃ vaktramupaiti sadhordurvarṇatāṃ yāti ca durjanasya  || 1.14 ||

śrīsāhasāṅkojjvalakīrtigarbhā mamāthavā kaṃ na haranti vācaḥ  |
kasyātra lobhāya na śuktayastā muktā hi yāsāmudare sphuranti  || 1.15 ||


__________________________________________

pratijñā

etānyavantīśvarapārijāta jātāni tārāpatipāṇḍurāṇi  |
sampratyahaṃ paśyata! digvadhūnāṃ yaśaḥprasūnānyavataṃsayāmi  || 1.16 ||


__________________________________________

atha ujjayinīvarṇanam

asti kṣitaūjjayinīti nāmnā purī vihāyasyamarāvatīva  |
babandha yasyāṃ padamindrakalpaḥ śrīvikramāditya iti kṣitīśaḥ  || 1.17 ||

āmañjuguñjatkalahaṃsapaṅkti vikasvarāmbhojarajaḥpiśaṅgā  |
ābhāti yasyāḥ parikhā nitambe saśabdajambūnadamekhaleva  || 1.18 ||

prākāravapracchalataḥ śarīramāvartya līlāśayanaṃ murāreḥ  |
yatrāntarasthāyinidhānarakṣāṃ vidhātumunmagna ivoragendraḥ  || 1.19 ||

pade pade sāndrasudhojjvalāni gṛhāṇi yā nākasadāṃ bibharti  |
abhyudgatānīva phaṇīndralokamāpūrya tadbhūmibhṛtāṃ yaśāṃsi  || 1.20 ||

himacchaṭāhāribhiraṃśujālaiḥ prālambimuktāphalajālakāni  |
vilāsinīvibhramamandirāṇi yasyāṃ hasantīva parasparasya  || 1.21 ||

gṛhāṇi yasyāṃ savarāṅganāni varāṅganā rūpapuraskṛtāṅgyaḥ  |
rūpaṃ samunmīlitasadvilāsamastraṃ vilāsāḥ kusumāyudhasya  || 1.22 ||
dṛṣṭavyam‌ . kāvyaprakāśa 10.131

yatrānanaireṇādṛśāmabhikhyāṃ sitāśmavātāyanapaṅktireti  |
ambhoruhairujjvalahemakḷptairākāśagaṅgājalaveṇikeva  || 1.23 ||

vidhūyamānāḥ pavanena yasyāṃ nīlāśmaveśmāruṇavaijayantyaḥ  |
bhinnāñjanaśyāmaghanodgatānāṃ taḍillatānāṃ dyutimāvahanti  || 1.24 ||

udeti kāntāmaṇimekhalānāṃ gṛhe gṛhe yatra muhurninādaḥ  |
āyāti yo'naṅgajayadvipasya madāvatārotsavaḍiṇḍimatvam  || 1.25 ||

mukhendubhiḥ pauravilāsinīnāṃ kapolakāntyā kṛtasaṃvibhāgaḥ  |
na yāti kārśyaṃ bahule'pi yatra vātāyanāsannataraḥ śaśāṅkaḥ  || 1.26 ||

na pakṣapātena vadāmi satyamuṣassu yasyāṃ bhavanāṅganebhyaḥ  |
sammarjanībhiḥ parataḥ kriyante visūtritaikāvalimauktikāni  || 1.27 ||

yasyāmasaṅkṣiptadṛśāṃ stanāṅke kastūrikāpattralatā cakāsti  |
śarāsanābhyāsavidhau samāpte mukteva godhā makaradhvajena  || 1.28 ||

vilāsinīsadmalasatpatākā paṭāñcale kāñcanakiṅkiṇīnām  |
nirantarairyā raṇitairajasramājñāmivodghoṣayati smarasya  || 1.29 ||

pratikṣaṇaṃ yā galitāṃśukānāmanaṅgalīlākalahotsaveṣu  |
analpakṛṣṇāgurudhūmabhaṅgyā vāmabhruvāmarpayatīva vāsaḥ  || 1.30 ||

yatrāṣṭamīcandramupeyivāṃsamālambya saudheṣvasamagrakāntim  |
keśāhṛtaiḥ ketakagarbhabarhairāpūrayantyardhamarālakeśyaḥ  || 1.31 ||

līlākaṭākṣe madirekṣaṇānāṃ sammohanāstrasphuritaṃ niveśya  |
ratyā saha kṛīḍati puṣpadhanvā yasyāmaśokadrumavīthikāsu  || 1.32 ||

jāne jaganmohanakautukena vidhāya kūjāmiṣamanyapuṣṭaiḥ  |
aharniśaṃ cūtavaneṣu yasyāmadhīyate mānmathamastravedam  || 1.33 ||

dikcakrasaṃcārimarīcidaṇḍa= cchalena cāmīkaratoraṇānām  |
avaimi dikpālapurīrvijitya yā hemavetragrahaṇe niyuṅkte  || 1.34 ||

ullāsiṣu svarṇagavākṣapaṅkteryā raśmidaṇḍeṣu vighūrṇamānaiḥ  |
bhātyagravedisphaṭikāṃśujālairdodhūyamānojjvalacāmareva  || 1.35 ||

yasyāṃ gṛhaprāṅganapadmarāga raśmicchaṭāpāṭalamantarikṣam  |
āliṅgitaṃ kiṃśukaśoṇabhāsā sandhyātapeneva sadā vibhāti  || 1.36 ||

avāpya yasyāṃ gṛhadīrghikāccha vaiḍūryasopānamayūkhasakhyam  |
hārītaśaṅkāṃ kalahaṃsaśāvā vāmabhruvāṃ pratyahamarpayanti  || 1.37 ||

nikāmamacchaiḥ pramadākapolairyatrendubimbākṛtibhiḥ kriyante  |
svavaktrasaundaryavilokaneṣu vilāsino darpaṇanirvyapekṣāḥ  || 1.38 ||

parāṅmukhīnāmapi ratnabhittau prasādavattadvadanaṃ vilokya  |
yasyāṃ yuvāno hariṇekṣaṇānāmalīkakopaṃ sahasā vidanti  || 1.39 ||

kurvanti yasyāṃ kusumeṣukeli śramonmiṣatsvedalavāstaruṇyaḥ  |
kapolakālāgurupattravallī kalmāṣamambho gṛhadīrghikāsu  || 1.40 ||

yasyāṃ samunmīlati sundarīṇāṃ sā kāpi saubhāgyaviśeṣalakṣmīḥ  |
vilāsamuktāguṇavadyadāsāṃ sadā priyastiṣṭhati kaṇṭhalagnaḥ  || 1.41 ||

avaimi gītena hṛte kuraṅge purandhribhiḥ saudhatalasthitābhiḥ  |
śyāmāsu yasyāṃ labhate tadaccha= kapolabimbānukṛtiṃ mṛgāṅkaḥ  || 1.42 ||

durgeti sarvatra gatā prasiddhiṃ nagendrakanyeva sanīlakaṇṭhā  |
yā lagnakāñcīviṣayeṇa kāntiṃ siṃhāsanenātitarāṃ bibharti  || 1.43 ||

vṛntādapāstairmarutā vikīrṇaiḥ sugandhibhistīrataruprasūnaiḥ  |
śiprāsaritkūlatamālanīlā vibhāti yasyāḥ kabarīlateva  || 1.44 ||

dhūmena yā naikamukhogatena saṃveṣṭyamāṇā paritaścakāsti  |
madāttaratneti samatsareṇa kṛtoparodheva mahārṇaveṇa  || 1.45 ||

vilaṅghayanti śrutivartma yasyāṃ līlāvatīnāṃ nayanotpalāni  |
bibharti yasyāmapi vakrimāṇameko mahākālajaṭārdhacandraḥ  || 1.46 ||

dhvajāgralagnena vilambatā khamanekaratnāṃśukadambakena  |
yasyāṃ sa caṇḍīpatimaṇḍapo'pi bibharti māyūramivātapattram  || 1.47 ||

purā kila brahmakamaṇḍaloryatāpūritaṃ puṇyatamābhiradbhiḥ  |
dhatte'tra yā tattripurāntakasya taḍāgamādarśamivāṅkadeśe  || 1.48 ||

yasyāmanekāmaraveśmarājirmaṇidhvajāgrocchalitairmayūkhaiḥ  |
likhatyamartyapramadākuceṣu vicitravarṇā iva patralekhāḥ  || 1.49 ||

yasyāṃ visūtrojjhitamekhalāni tathā śukāvartitasītkṛtāni  |
śaṃsanti saṃketamuṣassu yūnāṃ śiprātaṭodyānalatāgṛhāṇi  || 1.50 ||

manoharaiḥ kāmijanasya yasyāṃ nīrandhraniryanmṛganābhigandhaiḥ  |
sacandanaiḥ kāñcanakeliśailaiḥ kucairivodyānabhuvo vibhānti  || 1.51 ||

gatāsu tīraṃ timighaṭṭanena sasaṃbhramaṃ pauravilāsinīṣu  |
yatrollasatphenataticchalena muktārdrahāseva vibhāti śiprā  || 1.52 ||

saṃsargamāsādya vilāsinīnāṃ vilāsaveśmāgurudhūpadhūmaiḥ  |
baddhāspadāḥ saudhaśikhāsu yasyāṃ sugandhi toyaṃ jaladā vamanti  || 1.53 ||

satpuṣkaroddyotitaraṅgaśobhi nyamandamārabdhamṛdaṅgavādye  |
udyānavāpīpayasīva yasyāmeṇīdṛśo lāsyagṛhe ramante  || 1.54 ||

māṇikyavātāyanakāntijāla viluptarathyātimirotkarāsu  |
śyāmāsu yasyāṃ pramadāḥ kathaṃ citsaṅketamutkampikucāḥ prayānti  || 1.55 ||

navāmbuvāhapratibimbavatyāṃ yatroccaharmyāruṇaratnabhūmau  |
vyaktiṃ labhante surasundarīṇāṃ sālaktakāḥ prāvṛṣi pādamudrāḥ  || 1.56 ||

kṛtāvadhānātiśayena manye yā vedhasā madhyamalokaratnam  |
svaśilpavijñānaparaprakarṣa prakāśanāyātra vinirmiteva  || 1.57 ||



__________________________________________

atha nāyakavarṇanam

rājāsti tasyāṃ sa kulācalendra nikuñjaviśrāntayaśastaraṅgaḥ  |
bhāsvān grahāṇāmiva bhūpatīnāmavāptasaṃkhyo dhuri sindhurājaḥ  || 1.58 ||

nirvyūḍhanānādbhutasāhasaṃ ca raṇe vṛtaṃ ca svayameva lakṣmyā  |
nāmnā yameke navasāhasāṅkaṃ kumāranārāyaṇamāhuranye  || 1.59 ||

sahelamabhyuddharatā dharitrīṃ magnāṃ dviṣadvārinidhāvagādhe  |
yenātra nītā pṛthuvikrameṇa vyaktiṃ jagatyādivarāhalīlā  || 1.60 ||

uddāmadugdhābdhitaraṅgahāse yasyārikāntākucamaṇḍalāni  |
hārāḥ patatsāñjanabāṣpapaṅka kalaṅkabhītyeva parityajanti  || 1.61 ||

sadyaḥ karasparśamavāpya citraṃ raṇe raṇe yasya kṛpāṇalekhā  |
tamālanīlā śaradindupāṇḍu yaśastrilokābharaṇaṃ prasūte  || 1.62 ||

parāṅmukhenāpi sadā parasve patyā bhuvaḥ sāgaramekhalāyāḥ  |
aho yaśaḥ pūrvamahīpatīnāmanāvilaṃ yena balādviluptam  || 1.63 ||

cittaṃ prasādaśca manasvitā ca bhujaṃ pratāpaśca vasundharā ca  |
adhyāsate yasya mukhāravindaṃ dve eva satyaṃ ca sarasvatī ca  || 1.64 ||

yasyāpsarobhiḥ parigīyamānamākarṇya bāhvorvijayaprapañcam  |
śacīkucasparśamivāpya dhatte romodgamādhyāsitamaṅgamindraḥ  || 1.65 ||

prasādhitā yena ca bālya eva caturbhirutsāhavatā catasraḥ  |
śrutena buddhiḥ prabhutā nayena tyāgena lakṣmīrvasudhā balena  || 1.66 ||

raṇe raṇe muktakṛpaḥ kṛpāṇaṃ yaḥ śātadhāraṃ kṛtavān kṛtāstraḥ  |
anekarājanyaghaṭākirīṭa māṇikyaśāṇopalapaṭṭikāsu  || 1.67 ||

bhareṇa bhūmeḥ sphuṭamā namantyāḥ pāṃsucchaṭāḥ śeṣaphaṇāmaṇīnām  |
nyamīlayan yadvijayaprayāṇe nāgāṅganānāṃ nayanotpalāni  || 1.68 ||

anyonyasaṃśleṣaviśīrṇahāra cyutena sevāvasare nṛpāṇām  |
kīrṇāsu muktānikareṇa yasya kakṣyāsu vārapramadāḥ skhalanti  || 1.69 ||

ākṣipya hārānnijavikramāgni sphuliṅgaśaṅkāmanusandadhanti  |
yenārikāntākucamaṇḍaleṣu guñjāphalānyābharaṇīkṛtāni  || 1.70 ||

kṛpāṇapātairdalatāmarāti karīndrakumbhasthalamauktikānām  |
dhūlicchaṭā māṃsalayanti yasya samudgatānyājimukhe yaśāṃsi  || 1.71 ||

indudyutiḥ kundasitān dadhānā guṇānanaṅgotsavavaijayantī  |
yena dviṣāṃ dūramanāyi kaṇṭhādekāvalī vāmavilocanā ca  || 1.72 ||

yasmin vahatyambudhinemimurvīṃ maurvīkiṇaśyāmaladīrghadoṣṇi  |
vibhāvyate pauravarāṅganānāṃ madhyaṃ paraṃ dhāma daridratāyāḥ  || 1.73 ||

ākrāntadiṅmaṇḍalakuntalendra sāndrāndhakārāntaritaṃ raṇe yaḥ  |
svarājyamastrāruṇamaṇḍalāgro gṛhītavān dīdhitimānivāhaḥ  || 1.74 ||

ākampitānāṃ maruteva yasya doṣṇājibhūmāvatidakṣiṇena  |
ajāyatāripramadālatānāṃ bāṣpodabindūtkarapuṣpamokṣaḥ  || 1.75 ||

vipakṣahṛdbhaṅgakṛtā nitāntaṃ bhrūlekhayākuñcitayollasantyā  |
nākāramātreṇa parantapasya yasyānvakāri kriyayāpi cāpam  || 1.76 ||

doścandanānokahamāpya yasya samullasatsāndrayaśaḥprasūnā  |
gatātivṛddhiṃ lavalīlateva nibaddhamūlā paramāralakṣmīḥ  || 1.77 ||

kṛtānatibhyaḥ sahasā dadāti yaḥ sāmparāyeṣvabhayaṃ ripubhyaḥ  |
yaśaśca gṛhṇāti tuṣārahāra mṛṇālakarpūraparāgapāṇḍu  || 1.78 ||

prakāśitāśaṃ paritaḥ prajānāṃ yasyodayaṃ dhāmanidhervadanti  |
muktāñjanadhvāntaparigrahāṇi netrāṇi śatrupramadājanasya  || 1.79 ||

yasya prayāṇe pṛtanābhareṇa pariskhalatsaptasamudramudrā  |
parasparakṣodasamākulāsu dolāyate bhūḥ phaṇabhṛtphaṇāsu  || 1.80 ||

vibhinnamānaṃ kamalekṣaṇānāṃ vyaktānubhāvaṃ bhuvanatraye'pi  |
āhurjanā dīrghaguṇābhirāmaṃ yamekacāpaṃ kusumāyudhasya  || 1.81 ||

asaṃśayaṃ prāgasṛjadvidhātā yamekameva trijagadvadānyam  |
kalpadrumādīnatha taistadīya nirmāṇaśeṣaiḥ paramāṇuleśaiḥ  || 1.82 ||

akṛtrimatyāgasamudgatāni vipakvatālīdalapāṇḍurāṇi  |
āśālatānāṃ valayeṣu bharturyaśāṃsi yasya stabakībhavanti  || 1.83 ||

yatra pratāporjitarājacakra kirīṭaratnadyuticumbitāṅghrau  |
yathārthatāṃ yāti yayātipāṇḍu dilīpatulyaujasi rājaśabdaḥ  || 1.84 ||

ucchindataḥ kṣmāsarasīṃ vigāhya dharmakriyāpaṅkajinīvanāni  |
svairapracāraḥ kalikuñjarasya yenāṅkuśeneva balānniruddhaḥ  || 1.85 ||

ciraṃ vibhinnāḥ kumudendukunda bhāsaḥ samagrā api yatra te te  |
anyonyamekatra nivāsasaukhya kutūhaleneva guṇā ghaṭante  || 1.86 ||

kāntyānuliptāni vilocanānāmā pāṭalānāmatirodanena  |
sakuṅkumānīva puno bhavanti yasyārinārīkucamaṇḍalāni  || 1.87 ||

śriyi pratāpe yaśasi kṣamāyāṃ tyāge vilāse vinaye mahimni  |
kimanyadārohati yasya sāmyaṃ na rantidevo na pṛthurna pārthaḥ  || 1.88 ||


__________________________________________

sacivavarṇanam

sāmrājyabhārodvahanapragalbho yaśobhaṭākhyaḥ sacivo'sti yasya  |
svasūkticaryāsvapareṇa nāmnā ramāṅgadaṃ yaṃ kavayo vadanti  || 1.89 ||


__________________________________________

kularājadhānīvarṇanam

vijitya laṅkāmapi vartate yā yasyāśca nāyātyalakāpi sāmyam  |
jetuḥ purī sāpyaparāsti tasya dhāreti nāmnā kularājadhānī  || 1.90 ||

tasyāṃ sa sāhasajitāvanipālamauli ratnāṃśupallavitakāñcanapādapīṭhaḥ  |
devaḥ kṣamāvalayametadudañcitaika līlātapatrasulabhābharaṇaṃ bhunakti  || 1.91 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye nagarīnarendravarṇano nāma prathamaḥ sargaḥ




************************************************


dvitīyaḥ sargaḥ

kadā cillocanātithyamāptenālekhyaveśmani  |
sa mṛgavyāvinodena paspṛśe pārthivo bhṛśam  || 2.1 ||

udagraturagārūḍhaḥ sa samaṃ rājasūnubhiḥ  |
gireragacchadvipinaṃ vindhyasyāvandhyaśasanaḥ  || 2.2 ||

hāreṇāmalakasthūla muktenāmuktakuntalaḥ  |
phaṇīndrabaddhajūṭasya śriyamāpa sa dhūrjaṭeḥ  || 2.3 ||

devaḥ sa vārabāṇena nalinīpatrabandhunā  |
śyāmajīmūtasannaddhaḥ kāñcanādririvābabhau  || 2.4 ||

tasyopari vibho nīlamātapatraṃ vyarājata  |
vāridheriva pītāmbhaśśyāmalaṃ meghamaṇḍalam  || 2.5 ||

vyādhūtimuktamarutā vyarucaccāmareṇa saḥ  |
bandigṛhānniḥśvasatā yaśasevādibhūbhujām  || 2.6 ||

vyadhādivodgatairdūraṃ cūḍāratnaraśmibhiḥ  |
niśākarakuraṅgasya pāśamākāśavartmani  || 2.7 ||

tasyāṃsayornṛsiṃhasya hārakāntisaṭābharaḥ  |
uvāha kaṇṭhalagnaśrī vilāsahasitaśriyam  || 2.8 ||

puraḥ pade pade tasya nānāratnāṅgadatviṣaḥ  |
racayanti sma sañcāri cāpaṃ prācīnabarhiṣaḥ  || 2.9 ||

bhāti sma kaṇṭhābharaṇa padmarāgaprabhāvṛtaḥ  |
rājanyāsrasaraḥsnātaḥ sa bhārgava ivāparaḥ  || 2.10 ||

ruruce sa purastvaṅgatsitacchatraparamparaḥ  |
velānilasamuddhūta pheṇaḥ patirivārṇasām  || 2.11 ||

upāyanīkṛtonnidra padmakiñjalkasaurabhaḥ  |
tamasevata samrājaṃ vindhyadūta ivānilaḥ  || 2.12 ||

tadaśvīyakhurotkhātaiḥ pāṃsukūṭairajāyata  |
punaḥ prasabhavardhiṣṇu vindhyaśaṅkākulaṃ jagat || 2.13 ||

sahemaśṛṅkhalāḥ śvānaḥ śvetāstasyāgrato yayuḥ  |
vahantaḥ sataḍiddāma śāradāmbudharaśriyam  || 2.14 ||

tatasturagaheṣābhiḥ pattikolāhalena ca  |
ajāyanta bhayodbhrānta śvāpadā vindhyabhūmayaḥ  || 2.15 ||

rabhasākṛṣṭakodaṇḍaṃ karṇapūrīkṛtekṣaṇāḥ  |
tamanaṅgamivāpaśyan vanānte vanadevatāḥ  || 2.16 ||

mayi goptari coro'yamabalālocanaśriyaḥ  |
itīva mumuce tena kṛṣṇasāre śilīmukhaḥ  || 2.17 ||

sa citravarṇavicchitti hāriṇoravanīpatiḥ  |
śrīharṣa iva saṃghaṭṭaṃ cakre bāṇamayūrayoḥ  || 2.18 ||

camarīṇāṃ śarotkṛttaiḥ sa vāladhibhirujjvalam  |
parito vyadhitāraṇyaṃ svayaśaḥstabakairiva  || 2.19 ||


āhuta iva sāṭopaṃ lāṅgūlasphoṭaniḥsvanaiḥ  |
abhyadhāvadabhivyāghrānāgrataḥ kautukena sa  || 2.20 ||

vidhitsurātmanaḥ śauryamasāmānyamivādadhe  |
sa śarān puṇḍarīkeṣu puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇaḥ  || 2.21 ||

taistasya bāhuvīryeṇa dṛṣṭena vrīḍitairiva  |
tatyaje vikramaspardhā puraḥ paścāttu jīvitam  || 2.22 ||

kṣiterekātapatrāyāḥ sa patirmatsarādiva  |
udagrapuṇḍarīkatvaṃ na sehe vindhyabhūbhṛtaḥ  || 2.23 ||

araṇyamahiṣairdūraṃ tasmātsāyakavarṣiṇaḥ  |
apasasre vikīrṇāṃśostamobhiriva bhāsvataḥ  || 2.24 ||

mahāmahiṣaniṣpeṣakeliḥ pāramagāddvayoḥ  |
rājñastasyāticaṇḍasya caṇḍikācaraṇasya ca  || 2.25 ||

śaradīva prasarpantyāṃ tasya kodaṇḍaṭāṅkṛtau  |
vinidrajṛmbhitaharirvindhyodadhirajāyata  || 2.26 ||

aśeṣabhuvanakhyāta vikrame'vyaktavikramaiḥ  |
darpānmumucire tasmin tiryakkesaribhirdṛśaḥ  || 2.27 ||

sa teṣāṃ sahajodagra śauryasañcāravīthiṣu  |
mattebhamauktikottaṃsānna sehe nakhaśuktiṣu  || 2.28 ||

yūthe mahāvarāhāṇāṃ gate tadbāṇagocaram  |
cirātpalvalamustānāṃ santānaḥ svastimānabhūt || 2.29 ||

samadakroḍadaṃṣṭrābhiḥ kṛttābhiḥ kautukena sa  |
sthalīrvyadhita vindhyasya vikīrṇendukalā iva  || 2.30 ||

asairibhamasāraṅgamavārāhamakesari  |
kṣaṇādvanamaśārdūlamāttacāpaścakāra sa  || 2.31 ||

alaṃ prahṛtya bhūpāla sattveṣvanaparādhiṣu  |
itīva saḥ khagārāvairnyaṣedhi vanarājibhiḥ  || 2.32 ||

asevanta samīrāstamanastamṛgayāśramam  |
surataśrāntaśabarī kabarīmālyacumbinaḥ  || 2.33 ||


__________________________________________

sacivavarṇanam

athendracāpalalitaṃ sañcarantamitastataḥ  |
amandamṛgayāsaṅgaḥ saḥ kuraṅgamalokata  || 2.34 ||

so'pi taṃ valitagrīvaḥ kṣaṇaṃ sthitvā dadarśa ca  |
nirantaralatāpuñjaṃ vindhyakuñjaṃ viveśa ca  || 2.35 ||

tatasturagamutsṛjya visṛjyānuplavānapi  |
tamanviyāya sāraṅgaṃ sāraṅgāyatalocanaḥ  || 2.36 ||

devo ramāṅgadenātha saḥ śriyevānvagamyata  |
chāyā nivartate jātu na tu tasyaiṣa bhūpateḥ  || 2.37 ||

mṛgānusārī vicarannāttacāpo vane vane  |
līlāṃ kirātaveṣasya saḥ prapede pinākinaḥ  || 2.38 ||

dūrādeva sa tenātha śaravyatvamanīyata  |
svanāmadheyasya cihnasya hemapuṅkhasya patriṇaḥ  || 2.39 ||

śilābhedakṣamenāpi kimapi ślathamuṣṭinā  |
abhūdviddhaḥ saḥ sāraṅgastena tvaci ca marmaṇi  || 2.40 ||
[var2.40  {śilābheda॰|lem |eḓ  sirābheda |em?}]

saḥ śarāpātabhītena manaso'pyatiraṃhasā  |
atidūraṃ kuraṅgeṇa ninye rāma ivāparaḥ  || 2.41 ||

tena vindhyāṭavīmadhye dhāvannīrandhravīrudhi  |
utpatannutpatanneva sa kevalamalakṣyata  || 2.42 ||

dṛśā vanasthalīḥ kurvan vikīrṇendīvarā iva  |
javāddūramatikrāntaṃ taṃ kṣitīśastadaikṣata  || 2.43 ||

tatastirohite tasminnasamāptakutūhalaḥ  |
svabāṇa iva sa prāpa pṛthivīṃ durvilakṣatām  || 2.44 ||

daśanajyotsnayā kurvan latāḥ stabakitā iva  |
iti pārśvagataṃ smitvā sa jagāda ramāṅgadam  || 2.45 ||
[var2.45  {॰jyotsnayā|lem |em̭ ॰jotsnayā |ed}]



__________________________________________

ramāṅgadāya mṛgavarṇanam

ayaṃ tulitapaulomī kāntakāmukavigrahaḥ  |
mṛgo dṛggocaraṃ kaccitgatastava ramāṅgada!  || 2.46 ||

tadvadhūsvakaranyasta citrapatralatāṅkitaḥ  |
asau vihārahariṇaḥ kiṃ syādanalasāratheḥ  || 2.47 ||

api dṛṣṭā tvayaitasya kaṇṭhe kanakaśṛṅkhalā?  |
churitasyendracāpena meghasyeva taḍillatā  || 2.48 ||

mṛgajātirapūrveyaṃ sarvathā vasudhātale  |
sambhavatyamarādrau vā bhuvane vā phaṇābhṛtām  || 2.49 ||

asyākhaṇḍalakodaṇḍa kānti carmātipāvanam  |
gajapṛṣṭhe nidhāsyāmi mahāsamaraparvasu  || 2.50 ||

yannimajjati maccetaḥ kutūhalarasormiṣu  |
mārgamanveṣṭumetasya tadehi prayatāvahe  || 2.51 ||

ityuktvā virate tasmin paramāramahībhṛti  |
ūce ramāṅgadenaivamavāpyāvasaraṃ vacaḥ  || 2.52 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadakṛto mṛgānusaraṇaniṣedhaḥ

krudhevādhijyacāpena varṇasaṅkaradarśinā  |
tvayaiṣa citrasāraṅgo deva dūramanudruta  || 2.53 s||

aśūnyāḥ suragandharva siddhavidyādharoragaiḥ  |
imā navanavāścarya nidhayo vindhyabhūmayaḥ  || 2.54 ||

viramātyādaraḥ ko'yaṃ kuraṅgānveṣaṇe tava  |
na dhāvantyarthariktāsu kriyāsu tvādṛśāṃ dhiyaḥ  || 2.55 ||

mṛgayāsaktacittasya tavātra vicariṣyataḥ  |
pathi locanayoreṣa puno'pyāpatiṣyati  || 2.56 ||

śaraḥ saṃhriyatāmeṣa dhanurapyavatāryatām  |
śeṣā ca svastimatyastu deva śvāpadasantatiḥ  || 2.57 ||

daśā dinasya tīvreyaṃ yadayaṃ bhagavān raviḥ  |
kṛṣṇasyorasi puṣṇāti nabhasaḥ kaustubhaśriyam  || 2.58 ||

nihateṣu tvayā deva sattveṣu vyathitā iva  |
etā vahanti santāpamatīvāraṇyabhūmayaḥ  || 2.59 ||

amībhirbālavānīra viṭapeṣvagataklamaiḥ  |
kapiñjalairitaḥ paśya sahasaiva nilīyate  || 2.60 ||

arkāṃśuglapitairebhirito'pyālikhitairiva  |
rājajambūnikuñjeṣu paśya puṃskokilaiḥ sthitam  || 2.61 ||

āhlādahetuḥ snigdheyamito vanyena dantinā  |
paśya nīpatarośchāyā savaśena niṣevyate  || 2.62 ||

navāmbudharanīlo'yaṃ dāvadhūmalatodgamaḥ  |
nīlakaṇṭhairitastarṣātsotkaṇṭhairavalokyate  || 2.63 ||

anayā vidrumastamba bhaṅgapiṅgalayā dṛśā  |
itaḥ palvalapaṅkānto vyaktimabhyeti sairibhaḥ  || 2.64 ||
[var2.64 {॰paṅka॰|lem |em̭paṅkta॰ |ed}]

kaṭhorātapataptasya rājahaṃsasya samprati  |
narendra nalinīpatramātapatrībhavatyadaḥ  || 2.65 ||

mukhamaśvarajachanna kapolaphalakadyuti  |
deva danturayantyete tavāpi svedabindavaḥ  || 2.66 ||

tadatra kusumasmere niḥsvanatsamadālini  |
vinīyatāṃ latākuñje tvayaiṣa mṛgayāśramaḥ  || 2.67 ||

api svacchajalā deva kalahaṃsāṅkasaikatā  |
varāhotkhātamṛtsneyaṃ puraḥ puṣkariṇī tava  || 2.68 ||

tvāmivārkakaraklāntamākārayitumetayā  |
ayamādhūtakahlāra kalikaḥ preṣito'nilaḥ  || 2.69 ||

latāpuṣpotkaraiḥ kīrṇo mārgo'yamavagāhyatām  |
ito vanyebhamuktābhirimāḥ śarkarilā bhuvaḥ  || 2.70 ||

ityukte masṛṇaṃ tena nṛpasya padamādadhe  |
smitaṃ sarasvatīratna paryaṅke dantavāsasi  || 2.71 ||

yaśobhaṭopadiṣṭena gatvā kiñcidivādhvanā  |
prāpa puṣkariṇītīramavantitilako'tha saḥ  || 2.72 ||

karādanucarastasya sāndrasvedajalāṅguleḥ  |
madhurmanobhavasyeva saśaraṃ cāpamādade  || 2.73 ||


__________________________________________

atha snānādivarṇanam

tataḥ snānecchayā spṛṣṭo visṛṣṭaśyāmakañcukaḥ  |
sa reje meghanirmuktaḥ paryāpta iva candramaḥ  || 2.74 ||

pramṛṣtamṛgayāreṇu tanmukhaṃ pārśvavartinā  |
ruruce mārutākṣipta parāgamiva paṅkajam  || 2.75 ||

nisargalalitā tasya vimuktālaṅkṛtistanuḥ  |
lateva pārijātasya paryastastabakābhavat || 2.76 ||

svedanunnāṅgavarṇasya sarasīmavagāhataḥ  |
vanyasyevābhavattasya śrīḥ paryantavisarpiṇī  || 2.77 ||
[onlyin k̭ ed. readssvedabhinnāṅgarāgaḥ sa sarasīṃ tāmagāhata | madasiktataṭāghātadhūlirvanya iva dvipaḥ}]

sa tasyāṃ dūravikṣipta vihagaśreṇimekhalaḥ  |
vijahāra yathākāmaṃ vilāsakusumāyudhaḥ  || 2.78 ||

uvāha visphurannāla kaṇṭakacchadmanāpi sā  |
tadaṅgayaṣṭisparśena romañcamiva padminī  || 2.79 ||

tatastaraṅganirdhautamadhyāsya sa śilātalam  |
yaśaḥsnapitadiksīmā devaḥ sasnau yathāvidhi  || 2.80 ||

tasyāviralamattāli niḥsvanacchadmanā vane  |
agīyateva devasya latābhiḥ snānakautukam  || 2.81 ||
[var2.81 {॰kautukam|lem |k̭ ॰maṅgalam |ed}]

so dūrodastaparyasta sapuṣpasalilāñjaliḥ  |
jagattamo'pahaṃ jyotistrayīmayamupasthitaḥ  || 2.82 ||

tamānarca sa rājendurmaulau yasyendulekhayā  |
kriyate svardhunībāla mṛṇālaśakalabhramaḥ  || 2.83 ||

dhanyā hi tā vanalatā yatphalānyajahāra sa  |
kāryataḥ sadṛśī tāsāṃ samudraraśanā mahī  || 2.84 ||

nipīya nikhilavyakta rājacihnena pāṇinā  |
upāspṛśatsa cāmbhoja kiñjalkakapiśaṃ payaḥ  || 2.85 ||

nīlātapatramitreṇa patreṇāmbujinībhuvā  |
nivāritoṣṇaḥ saśrīkaṃ latākuñjaṃ jagāma sa  || 2.86 ||

saparāge viśaśrāma kusumaprastare ca sa  |
lakṣmīkucāṅgarāgeṇa bhinne śeṣa ivācyutaḥ  || 2.87 ||

ramāṅgado'pi nirvartya tvarayā kiṃ cidāhnikam  |
avāptasevāvasaraḥ paryupāsta viśāmpatim  || 2.88 ||

so bhṛṅgadhvaninā supto vipañcīnādabandhunā  |
tamālapallavaistena kiṃ citkiṃ cidavījyata  || 2.89 ||

nidrāgṛhītanirmukta locano'tha jahāra sa  |
ghanachāyāvṛtavyakta bhāsvato nabhasaḥ śriyam  || 2.90 ||

pīnāṃsataṭasaṃśliṣṭa puṣpakesaraśobhinā  |
uṣaḥsākalpakeneva śayanīyamamucyate  || 2.91 ||


__________________________________________

punarmṛgayāvihāraḥ

cakāra ca padaṃ citraḥ sa mṛgastasya cetasi  |
lagnaṃ hi kimapi kvāpi kṛcchrādākṛṣyate manaḥ  || 2.92 ||

prasādahṛdyālaṅkāraistena mūrtirabhūṣyata  |
atyujjvalaiḥ kavīndreṇa kālidāsena vāgiva  || 2.93 ||

jagāhe sa mahāraṇyamaṃsāsaktadhanurlataḥ  |
upoḍhaśaśabhṛllekhaḥ sāyamabdhimivāryamā  || 2.94 ||

tasmin kusumakirmīra tale ca vicacāra sa  |
sphurannakṣatraśabale nabhasīva niśākaraḥ  || 2.95 ||

mṛgānugamanirbandho na jagāmāsya mandatām  |
maithilīramaṇasyeva vipine pṛthivīpateḥ  || 2.96 ||


__________________________________________

niśātikramaṇam

tataḥ papāta jaladhau virocanaphaṇāmaṇiḥ  |
dināhernīyamānasya balātkāragarutmatā  || 2.97 ||

śanaiḥ śanairatha vyomni mṛgāṅkaḥ padamādadhe  |
saśaṅka iva bhūpālānmṛgayāsaktacetasaḥ  || 2.98 ||

ramāṅgadāstṛtasnigdha pallavaprastare tataḥ  |
vane rājendunā ninye tenendutilakā niśā  || 2.99 ||

atha mukharakhagāpanītanidraḥ kvacidapi padmasarasyupāsya sandhyām  |
puno'pi tamavekṣituṃ niśānte nṛpatiriyeṣṭa mṛgaṃ mṛgendrakalpaḥ  || 2.100 ||
[var2.100 {niśāntlem |k̭  vanānte |ed}]

pañcaikena smara iva śarān pāṇinā hemapuṅkhānanyenorvīvijayi ca dhanuḥ sāhasāṅkaṃ dadhānaḥ  |
devaḥ sa atha vyavaharadariṣu nyastapādaḥ pikālī nīḍanyañcanniculanicayaśyāmalāsu sthalīṣu  || 2.101 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye citramṛgāvalokano nāma dvitīyaḥ sargaḥ





************************************************


tritīyaḥ sargaḥ

atha bahu carato'sya cāpapāṇeścakitavalanmṛgayūthavīkṣitasya  |
vanabhuvi sulabhaḥ pariśramo'bhūnna tu hariṇaḥ sa hṛtendracāpaśobhaḥ  || 3.1 ||

dhanuṣi tanubharaṃ nidhāya kiṃcittaruṇatamālavane vinītakhedaḥ  |
mṛgarudhirakalaṅkitena devaḥ samaviṣameṇa pathā puraḥ pratasthe  || 3.2 ||
[var3.2 {॰kalaṅkiten lem |k |b̭ ॰lavāṅkitena |ed}]


__________________________________________

vindhyakandarapraveśaḥ

atha sa caṭulaṣaṭpadopagītaṃ vanagajadānasugandhigandhavāham  |
parisaramabhinṛttanīlakaṇṭhaṃ nyaviśata vindhyanagendrakandarasya  || 3.3 ||

caṭulakṛtakacagrahaḥ sa gacchan vanalatayā parihāsalolayeva  |
narapatiravaśaḥ kṛtaḥ sa kāmaṃ viyati mukhendumudañcayāṃ cakāra  || 3.4 ||
[var3.4  {caṭula॰|lem |eḓ  catura॰ |ed}]

namadavanipatiḥ patiḥ prajānāmayi! capale nu vikṛṣyate kaceṣu  |
virama muhuritīva manyuguñjaddhvanibhirasāvalibhirlatābhyadhāyi  || 3.5 ||
[var3.5 {manyu॰|lem |conj̭  mañju॰ |ed}|var{guñja lem |k̭ mukta |ed}]

katicidalinipītadhūpagandhānanativilambiparārdhyamauliratnān  |
tvaritamatha ramāṅgado'sya keśān vipinalatāviṭapāntarāccakarṣa  || 3.6 ||


__________________________________________

haṃsadarśanam

nṛpatiratha tadonmukhaścarantaṃ jhaṭiti sitacchadamambare dadarśa  |
dadhatamadhigatāṃ kuto'pi cañcvā bisalatikāmiva tārahāralekhām  || 3.7 ||

vikasitakumudacchadāvadāte tatanibhṛte patatāṃ tatī dadhānam  |
viracayitumupāyanaṃ nṛpendornavaghaṭite iva dantapatralekhe  || 3.8 ||

citamatanu visarpatā samantātkiraṇalatānikareṇa hārayaṣṭeḥ  |
sitamaṇimayasūcinirmitasya sthitamiva jaṅgamapañjarasya madhye  || 3.9 ||

taralamaṇirucāvṛtaṃ prakṛtyā vidalitavidrumakandakāñcituṇḍam  |
praṇayanihitapādayāvakāṅkaṃ kamalavanasthitilolayeva lakṣmyā  || 3.10 ||

caraṇayugatale vibhātakāla sphuṭitajapākusumābhitāmrabhāsi  |
svayamiva nalinīvanaprasūteḥ paricayataḥ kṛtakāntisaṃvibhāgam  || 3.11 ||

pratipadamatidīrghahārabhārātavanamadunnamayantamuttamāṅgam  |
śirasi nipatato nikāmamuṣṇānahimarucaḥ kiraṇānivotkṣipantam  || 3.12 ||

vivṛtamukhadhṛtasya niṣpatadbhistaralamaṇeraruṇasya kāntileśaiḥ  |
aviratamaravindavṛndapītānmadhupṛṣatāniva bhūyasodvamantam  || 3.13 ||

parikhacitamayatnapūritāśairaviralamauktikadāmaraśmijālaiḥ  |
yaśa iva parabhūtabhṛtāṃ niruddhya prasabhamupāhṛtamātmano yaśobhiḥ  || 3.14 ||

abhinavabisaśaṅkayāpahṛtya sphaṭikamayīmasamakṣamakṣamālām  |
vihagamiva vimānahaṃsapaṅktervighaṭitamekataraṃ caturmukhasya  || 3.15 ||

cyutamiva sitacāmaraṃ maghoneḥ śramajaḍavāravilāsinīkarāgrāt |
apahṛtamiva lolapatrajālaṃ surasaritaḥ pavanena puṇḍarīkam  || 3.16 ||

harahasitasitaṃ divāpi kānti stabakamivāpatitaṃ sudhākarasya  |
api patitamivāntarikṣapīlormaghavadibhasya vilāsakarṇaśaṅkham  || 3.17 ||
[syntax kulakam]

sa ca pariṇatalodhradhūliśuklastaralamavantipateścakāra cetaḥ  |
kisalayamiva bālacandanasya stimitagatirmalayācalendravātaḥ  || 3.18 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavarṇanam

avadadatha vibuddhapuṇḍarīka pratimamupāntacare nidhāya cakṣuḥ  |
daśanamaṇimayūkhabhinnavarṇāṃ girimiti mālavarājapūrṇacandraḥ  || 3.19 ||

surabhikusumacumbināvanamrāmalipaṭalena latāmimāṃ vinā me  |
khagamamumupadarśayetka evaṃ gaganaramāpatipāñcajanyamanyaḥ  || 3.20 ||

smaravarakarihastaśīkarāṇāṃ vidadhati rucayo navahāramauktikānām  |
vidadhati rucayo'sya canncukoṭau kavalitabālamṛṇālasūtralīlām  || 3.21 ||

ayi! kathaya sitacchadaḥ kva cāyaṃ vananalinīpulināntabaddhavāsaḥ  |
avanipatikalatrakaṇṭhayogyaḥ kva ca śaśibandhuranargha eṣa hāraḥ  || 3.22 ||

vanabhuvipatitaḥ kuto'yamasyāṃ kathamayamasya mukhātithitvamāptaḥ?  |
guruvibhavapadasya kasya vā syādayamiti me na paricchinatti cetaḥ  || 3.23 ||

ayamucitataraḥ phaṇistriyo vā kucakalaśāntaramartyayoṣito vā ?  |
niyatamudadhimudramīdṛśāni kṣititalamābharaṇāni na spṛśanti  || 3.24 ||

kimaparamanugamya eṣa haṃsaḥ śramajaḍapakṣatirāvayorvanānte  |
ayi! yadayamavāmanasya bhūmirmukhadhṛtahāralataḥ kutūhalasy || 3.25 a ||

__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavākyam

iti viratavacasyudīrya tasmin kṛtini nṛpe paramāravaṃśaketau  |
sphuradadharavikīrṇadantakānti prasaramidaṃ jagade ramāṅgadena  || 3.26 ||

jaḍarucirapi rocate na kasmai kathamapi dīrghaguṇena labdhasaṅgaḥ  |
naravara yadanena hāradāmnā tava patagaḥ spṛhaṇīya eṣa jātaḥ  || 3.27 ||

upavana iva sambhavaḥ kadācinnṛpa ghaṭate vipine'pi hārayaṣṭeḥ  |
yadasurasuranāgarājakanyā iha viharanti nagendrakandareṣu  || 3.28 ||

śakunirayamito digantalagnairanupamamauktikanirgatairmayūkhaiḥ  |
tava viracayatīva sūtrapātaṃ sukṛtanidhāna! bhaviṣyataḥ śubhasya  || 3.29 ||

drutamayamanugamyatāmidānīmanugamanena yato'sya hāralābhaḥ  |
phalamadhikamato'pi naḥ kadācitkimapi bhavedayamasya hetuḥ  || 3.30 ||

taruviṭapalatāntareṇa gacchan bhuvamabhajan bahumuktameghavartmā  |
ayamatiguruhārabhārajātāṃ śramajaḍatāmalamātmano vyanakti  || 3.31 ||

nayanapathamayaṃ yathā tavārāttvamapi tathāsya sitacchadasya yātaḥ  |
avanitalamṛgāṅka! yadvanāntaścakitamivāyamitastataḥ prayāti  || 3.32 ||

niculavanamatītya vartate'yaṃ purata imāmayamabjinīmupetaḥ  |
navajaladharaśaṅkayeva śaṅke taruṇatamālavanādito nivṛttaḥ  || 3.33 ||

kisalayakalitāñjaliṃ tvarāvānayamupasarpati nīlasinduvāram  |
kṣaṇamayamiha bālacūtamaulau vicakilamālyavilāsamādadhāti  || 3.34 ||

ayamabhinavakarṇikārayaṣṭiṃ jhaṭiti ghanastabakastanīmupaiti  |
ayamaticapalo nisargaraktāṃ sthalanalinīmavadhīrya deva! yātaḥ  || 3.35 ||

ayamiha hi latāmupaiti kaundīṃ kusumavatīṃ navamādhavīṃ vilaṅghya  |
kvaṇadalivalayāsu nāsu tena skhalitamitaḥ sahakāramañjarīṣu  || 3.36 ||

abhisarati vanasthalīmivaitāṃ madanavatīmayamūḍhakāmilīlaḥ  |
sphuradatanuśilīmukhasya cāgre vicarati karṇe ivāyamarjunasya  || 3.37 ||

śramamapaharatastanūrmivātairayamatithirvanapalvalasya jātaḥ  |
tarutatiṣu tirohito'yametāsvayamaravindavanādivojjihīte  || 3.38 ||

kurabakavanataḥ kadambarājiṃ vrajati tato mucakundakānanāni  |
iti nagamavagāhate sahārastvamiva dhrtaklama eṣa rājahaṃsaḥ  || 3.39 ||

kuru vijayamito mamārpya etaddhanuradhunā sasuvarṇapuṅkhabāṇam  |
yadayamitagatirgato'tidūraṃ jalapatagaḥ saha naḥ kutūhalena  || 3.40 ||


__________________________________________

haṃsānugamanam

iti kathayati cāpamarpayitvā samamiṣubhiḥ sa ramāṅgade narendras |
patagamanu tamāttahāraṃ harati na kaṃ navavastusaṃprayogaḥ  || 3.41 ||

ṛju tamatha vihāyasā vrajantaṃ rabhasavaśādanugacchato nṛpasya  |
samajani bhṛśamāyato'sya panthāstaruviṭapāvaṭavarjanena vakraḥ  || 3.42 ||

nṛpatiranuyayau vane vihaṅgaṃ nṛpatimabhi praṇayī ramāṅgado'pi  |
śrutamiva viśadaṃ śucirvivekaḥ kṛtini vivekamivāntaraḥ prasādaḥ  || 3.43 ||

atha kamalasarastaraṅgadolā calayavilolarathāṅganāmayugmam  |
madakalakalahaṃsanādakṛṣṭaḥ śramaviveśaḥ saḥ sitacchadaḥ prapede  || 3.44 ||

vilulitakabarīkalāpamālyā mṛdunavaśaivalamekhalā vahantyaḥ  |
ratiraṇamavasāya yatra nityaṃ saha ramaṇairamarāṅganā ramante  || 3.45 ||

salilagatadhiyātha tena dūrātsa gururamucyata niḥsahena hāraḥ  |
jaḍahṛtahṛdayāḥ kiyacciraṃ vā guṇamahatāmiha bhāramudvahanti  || 3.46 ||

sa ca vitatamarīcicañculekho vigalitahāralatāmiṣeṇa haṃsaḥ  |
pariṇatabisakāṇḍabhaṅgapītaṃ paya iva vistṛtadhāramujjagāra  || 3.47 ||

atha kanakamṛṇālikāyugasya dyutinicayena citaṃ visarpatādhaḥ  |
aśiśiramahaso visāriṇā khe valayitamaṃśulatākadambakena  || 3.48 ||

taṭabhuvi tamapaśyadāpatantaṃ patiravane ravataṃsitāyatākṣaḥ  |
sitamabhinavahemadaṇḍaśobhi sphaṭikaśalākamivātapatramaindram  || 3.49 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]

sarasi dhavalite tataḥ samantādamṛtamarīciruceva tasya kāntyā  |
vyadhita balavatī viyogapīḍā padamapade hṛdayeṣu cakranāmnām  || 3.50 ||

ativitataguṇaikadhāmni tasmin vidhuramadhaḥpatite viśuddhibhāji  |
kathamapi vasudhādhipaḥ pramodaṃ jhaṭiti jagāma guṇiṣvamatsaro hi  || 3.51 ||

atha nabhasi piśaṅgasāndhyarāga cchurita ivāmbaranimnagātaraṅgam  |
kiyadapi sarasastaṭe sa gatvā kamalarajaḥkapiśe dadarśa hāram  || 3.52 ||

sa ca sapadi ramāṅgadopanītaṃ kanakasaroruhakāntinā kareṇa  |
nijayaśa iva mūrtamādade taṃ bhuvanatalābhayadānadīkṣitena  || 3.53 ||

sujanamiva guṇairupoḍhaśobhaṃ śucitarabālamṛṇālasūtradīrghaiḥ  |
ahamahamikayā kṛtapraveśaṃ taraṇikaraglapitairivendupādaiḥ  || 3.54 ||

dadhatamaruṇamaṅgarāgaśeṣaṃ kvacidapi yaṣṭiṣu tāramauktikāsu  |
ghaṭitamiva navātapena kiṃcidbahunavayā śaśalakṣmaṇastviṣā ca  || 3.55 ||

ativitataruciṃ vahantamantastaralamaṇiṃ taruṇendragopaś¿bham  |
aviralamasakṛnnivāsalagnaṃ lalitavadhūhṛdayādivānurāgam  || 3.56 ||

katicidapi latāntare dadhānaṃ mṛgamadaliptatalāni mauktikāni  |
śabalajalalavairivāttajanmānyayamaranadīyamunātitherghanasya  || 3.57 ||

anuguṇapadavīvinirgatāsu pratilatamāyatamauktikaprabhāsu  |
rajanikaramarīcisūcidīrghairbahubhiriva grathitaṃ mṛṇālasūtraiḥ  || 3.58 ||

atidṛḍhamanuraktayā vitīrṇaṃ mukharamahodadhimekhalāṃ vahantyā  |
urasi nihitabandhujīvamurvyā vicakilamālyamiva svayaṃvarāya  || 3.59 ||
[syntax kulakam]

arucadatha kare sa tasya bibhradyuvatiradacchadakāntimadhyaratnam  |
kimapi manasijena śāsanāṅkaḥ prahitanijāyudhacitrapuṣpamudraḥ  || 3.60 ||

śaśikararucā sa tena reje mṛdukarapuṣkaravartinā narendraḥ  |
amarapuradhunīsamuddhṛtena tridaśakarīva mṛṇālakandalena  || 3.61 ||

urasi narapateḥ pataṃścakāśe kanakaśilāvipule tadaṃśupūraḥ  |
akhilabhuvanakośarājalakṣmyā nihita ivādhikamutkyā kaṭākṣaḥ  || 3.62 ||
[var3.62 {kaṭākṣaḥ|lem |em̭ kadākṣaḥ |ed}]

vikṛtirudadhiśuktieṣu cyutānāṃ dhruvamiyamindukalāsudhālavānām  |
jaladajalakaṇodbhaveṣu kāmaṃ nivasati kāntiriyaṃ na mauktikeṣu  || 3.63 ||

na kimayamuḍumaṇḍalāpavādaḥ kumudavanāni puro'sya na trapante  |
kathamayamavadhirna muktibhājāmiti tamavekṣya sa cintayāṃ cakāra  || 3.64 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]

sphuradudaraniveśitendranīlāṃ madajalarājimiva smaradvipasya  |
avanipatirapaśyadakṣarāṇāṃ tatimatha hāramṛṇālikāntarāle  || 3.65 ||

abhinavalikhitāmiva praśatiṃ madanamahānṛpateḥ sa pārthivendraḥ  |
iti nibiḍakutūhalākulastāṃ lalitapadābharaṇāmavācayacca  || 3.66 ||

’’manasijavaravīravaijantyāstribhuvanadurlabhavibhramaikabhūmeḥ  |
kucamukulavicitrapatravallī paricita eṣa sadā śaśiprabhāyāḥ" || 3.67 ||

kimu vipulamimaṃ manuṣyalokaṃ puramuta parvatapakṣaśātanasya  |
kimu yuvatiriyaṃ bhujaṅgabharturbhuvanamalaṅkurute śaśiprabheti  || 3.68 ||

vadati śaśimukhīmitasna dūre taruṇavilepanabhinna eṣa hāraḥ  |
saritamiva vanāntare samīraḥ sphutitasaroruhareṇunā piśaṅgaḥ  || 3.69 ||

kusumaśarasakhasya kasya citkiṃ samajani nākatalādhidevateyam  |
uta mukulitamanmathāvatāre pathi vicare'dhinākakanyakānām  || 3.70 ||

api kṛtanayanotsavena tanvī taruṇasudhāmadhureṇa darśanena  |
mudamupajanedvane kimeṣā ? mama śaradindukaleva kairavasya  || 3.71 ||

iti kiyadapi yāvadeva cintā vaśamagamatsa manuṣyalokapālaḥ  |
dhanurapi niculaṃ vidhāya tāvatkusumaśaro'sya babhūva pārśvavartī  || 3.72 ||

atha sambhramādanucareṇa nihitatamālapallave  |
āsta tarukusumasaṃvalite sarasaḥ saḥ saikataśilātale nṛpaḥ  || 3.73 ||

ālakṣya stanasakhyalakṣmaṇi tatastasmin kuraṅgīdṛśo muktādāmni karodarapraṇayitāmāpte tuṣāratviṣi  |
vismṛtyā sahasā hṛtaṃ caturayā kroḍīkṛtaṃ cintayā cetaḥ śrīnavasāhasāṅkanṛpaterutkaṇṭhayākṛṣyata  || 3.74 ||


iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye śaśiprabhāhāralābho nāma tṛtīyaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


caturthaḥ sargaḥ

tataḥ saścetasyavanīpatirdadhe śaśiprabhālokamahotsavaspṛhām  |
upoḍharāgāmudadhistaṭodare navodgatāṃ vidrumakandalīmiva  || 4.1 ||

śaśiprabhāśānalinīmṛṇālatāmupāgate mauktikadāmni sādaraḥ  |
tadāgate dūta iva nyaveśayatsa darśitapremalave vilocane  || 4.2 ||

punaḥ punaḥ ṣaṭpadarājimecakāṃ tadindranīlākṣarapaṅktimaikṣata  |
sa tatkṣanānmanmathajātavedasaṃ tanīyasīṃ dhūmalatāmivodgatām  || 4.3 ||

sugandhihārādanulepanaṃ kare samunmiṣatsvedalave vilumpati  |
asaṅgatāyā api dīrghacakṣuṣaḥ payodharaspṛśamivāsasāda sa  || 4.4 ||

tadīyanāmāṅkalipiṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ salīlamāvartayituṃ pracakrame  |
parisphuratpallavapāṭalādharo rahasyavidyāmiva manmathasya sa  || 4.5 ||

anekarūpālikhanapragalbhayā sutīkṣṇayā vartikayeva cintayā  |
sastāmanāptekṣaṇasaṃstavāṃ purā lilekha citte muhuranyathānyathā  || 4.6 ||

anaṅgacaṇḍātapataptayostadā śaśiprabhāvibhramadarśanaṃ prati  |
dvayorabhūdutsukatā vanāntare vilāsinastasya ca kairavasya ca  || 4.7 ||

udagradigvāraṇahastahāriṇā sa dakṣiṇena sphuratā ca bahunā  |
sthirīkṛtāśo manāsāpi durlabhāmadurlabhāmindumukhīmamanyata  || 4.8 ||

puro vimuñcannayane yadṛcchayā nṛpastamāladrumakānanodare  |
apaśyadatravāsare vilāsinīṃ payodhamadhye śaśinaḥ kalāmiva  || 4.9 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

athaiṣa dīrghā darśanārciṣaḥ kiranmukhāmṛtāṃśoḥ kiraṇacchaṭā iva  |
nirīkṣya tāmunmadahaṃsagāminīṃ ramāṅgadaṃ sasmitamityavocat || 4.10 ||
[var4.10 {sasmitam|leṃ k |tr |i̭ saspṛham |ed}]

śanaiścarantī vipine tava sthitā nitambinī kaccidiyaṃ dṛśaḥ pathi?  |
adhīratāṃ dakṣiṇamātariśvanā lateva nītā masṛṇena mādhavī  || 4.11 ||

yutā sitābhaiḥ sumanobhiretayā pariślatheyaṃ kabarī niyamyate  |
udastabhāsvatkarakāntayā śriyā dinasya tārāśabaleva śarvarī  || 4.12 ||

asatkavervāgiva vītasauṣṭhavaṃ niveśayantī padamavyavasthayā  |
asāvanekaskhalitaiḥ samākulā vimucya mārgaṃ kimitaḥ pratiṣṭhate  || 4.13 ||

haṭhena netuṃ vaśatāmivātmano mano'bhirāmāsu vilāsabhaṅgiṣu  |
dhṛtāṃśukā tābhiriyaṃ pade pade latābhirambhojamukhī niruddhyate  || 4.14 ||

vicinvatī kiṃ cidiveyamādarādapakṣamapātastimite vilocane  |
gate'vataṃsotpalapatrabandhutāmitastataḥ padmavane vimuñcati  || 4.15 ||

mṛdu prayāntīyamanimnanimnayoḥ sitāṃśukā kāmapi kāntimaśnute  |
jale kalā iva pratibimbitaindavī vanānilodañcadavāñcadūrmiṇi  || 4.16 ||

prasādamasmākamaraṇyadurlabhairvidhehi sālaktakapādatāḍanaiḥ  |
asāvaśokaiḥ kṣaṇamāśritaiḥ śramātitīva mattālirutena yācyate  || 4.17 ||

abhedamūḍhastabakābhirāvṛtā latābhirīṣallulitālipaṅktibhiḥ  |
iyaṃ puro mārutanartitālakā na lakṣyate vyaktamavāmanastanī  || 4.18 ||

ṛju kvacitkvāpi anṛju pravartate kvacitskhalatyuccaśilātale pathi  |
iyaṃ śanaiḥ śailanadīva ca kvacitvinamravānīratalena gacchati  || 4.19 ||

sthite pariṣvajya sarojinīmimāṃ vane ghane'smin kusumonmadālini  |
prayāti sāsūyamiyaṃ kathaṃcana svahastasīmantitamārgavīrudhi  || 4.20 ||

kvacitkvacitsvedalavodgamo mukhe samāhṛteyaṃ kabarī yathā tathā  |
sayaṃ ca kampaḥ kucayorvadhūriyaṃ ratiśramavyākuliteva lakṣyate  || 4.21 ||

anena rūpātiśayena līlayā viviktanepathyaparigraheṇa ca  |
araṇyasañcāraparā iyamekikā kutūhalaṃ me hṛdaye niṣiñcati  || 4.22 ||

asau parādhīnatayāspadīkṛtā na bālikā na pratibhāsate na mama  |
ayaṃ sphuratkāñcanapadmasodaraḥ sacāmaro'syāḥ kathamanyathā karaḥ  || 4.23 ||

iyaṃ kimu syādvanadevatāgatā ? gatā dharāṃ vyomavadhūriyaṃ kimu ?  |
avekṣituṃ hāramiheyamāgatā śaśiprabhāvāravilāsinī kimu ?  || 4.24 ||

itaḥ saścitrākṛtirīkṣato mṛgaḥ sitacchadādāptamito vibhūṣaṇam  |
itaśca dṛṣṭeyamiti sprasūyate prasaktamāścaryamiyaṃ vanasthalī  || 4.25 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalāyā nāyakadarśanam

iti prakṛtyā madhuroktiruktavān tayāyatākṣyā dadṛśe viśāmpatiḥ  |
tamālapatrāpihite śilātale kumudvatīkānta ivāmbare sthitaḥ  || 4.26 ||


tatastadālokanakautukena sā sthitā nimeṣojjhitapakṣmalekṣaṇam  |
vinidrasatkesarapaṅkajā babhau vane nivātastimiteva padminī  || 4.27 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalāyāḥ svagatam

acintayatseti ca pāṭalādharaḥ sitāravindacchadadīrghalocanaḥ  |
mukhaṃ sudhādīdhitisundaraṃ dadhan vane gataḥ ko'yamanaṅgavibhramaḥ  || 4.28 ||

vyanakti kalyāṇamayīyamākṛtirmahīyasīmasya mahānubhavatām  |
asatyametāsu rucā vitanvatī latāsu kārtasvarapallavodgamam  || 4.29 ||

bhujena citrāṅgadaratnaśobhinā satārahāreṇa bhujāntareṇa ca  |
vadatyayaṃ madhyamalokapālatāṃ parārdhyacūḍāmaṇinā ca maulinā  || 4.30 ||

anātapatro'yamatra lakṣyate sitātapatrairiva sarvato vṛtaḥ  |
acāmaro'pyeṣa sateva vījyate vilāsavālavyajanena ko'pi ayam  || 4.31 ||

priyā iyamārūḍhaguṇā suvaṃśabhūrna cāntikaṃ cāpalatāsya muñcati  |
ime pṛṣatkā api pārthivaśriyo vilāsakarṇotpalapallavā iva  || 4.32 ||

itaḥ śilotsaṅgatale niṣeduṣā divaścyuteneva kuraṅgalakṣmaṇā  |
adhastaruṇāmamunā vinīyate kṣaṇaṃ mṛgavyopanataḥ pariśramaḥ  || 4.33 ||

ayaṃ sa na syānnavasāhasāṅka ityanaṅgalīlāsu kṛtī bhuvaḥpatiḥ  |
sa yena mukto nijanāmalāñchitaḥ śaśiprabhākelikuraṅgake śaraḥ  || 4.34 ||

ayaṃ sa no hāra ivāsya dṛśyate karodare pallavapāṭalatviṣi  |
itaḥ kimetasya na saikate saḥ kiṃ sitacchado locanagocaraṃ gataḥ  || 4.35 ||

suvarṇapuṅkhe likhitaṃ śilīmukhe tadasya nāmāsti samānamākṛteḥ  |
yadadbhutāmekapade pṛṣatkatāmagādanaṅgasya śaśiprābhāṃ prati  || 4.36 ||

anena cedyogamupaiti daivataḥ phanīndrakanyā śaśineva rohiṇī  |
analpalāvaṇyatiraskṛtopamaṃ vapustadasyāḥ saphalatvameṣyati  || 4.37 ||

vidhātumenāmahameva vā kṣamā mitodarīmaṅkatale'sya ko vidhiḥ  |
mamedṛśe yadviṣaye vimatsarāḥ stuvanti sakhyo masṛṇoktisauṣṭhavam  || 4.38 ||

sahāmunā kiṃcidupāntavartinā vadatyasaūdgatadantadīdhitiḥ  |
kutūhalākṣiptanimeṣalāsyayā vilokayanmāmiva dīrghayā dṛśā  || 4.39 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalāyāḥsamīpagamanam

athādhigantuṃ kila tasya patriṇo gatiṃ vanānte kathamapyalakṣitām  |
tamabhyagātsā nṛpatiṃ sacāmarā saritsaphenā nidhimambhasāmiva  || 4.40 ||

samuccarannūpurasinncitaiḥ padairyathā yathā saṃmukhamājagāma sā  |
tayāyatākṣyeva tathātatheritā dṛgasya paścādapasāramādade  || 4.41 ||

śanaistatastāṃ savidhopasarpaṇīṃ nirīkṣya hāraṃ pidadhe narādhipaḥ  |
nijottarīyeṇa sitena mārutaḥ śaraddhaneneva śaśāṅkamaṇḍalam  || 4.42 ||

payodharotsaṅganivāsalālitaṃ vyadhādimaṃ pannagarājakanyakā  |
iti prarohadbahumānamantharo babhūva tasminnavanīpurandaraḥ  || 4.43 ||

analpalāvaṇyavilāsajanmabhūrvicitraratnadyutibhāsvarormikā  |
tamiddhamuktābharaṇaṃ bhuvaḥpatiṃ payodhiveleva suvelamāpa sā  || 4.44 ||

avāpa devaḥ śriyamantikasthayā tayā sa vālavyajanāṅkahastayā  |
niṣevyamāṇaḥ sphuṭalakṣyadehayā narendralakṣmyeva yaśaḥsametayā  || 4.45 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalākṛtaḥ satkāraḥ

vibhinnacūrṇālakabhakti kurvatī vikīrṇacūḍāmaṇicandrikaṃ śiraḥ  |
athānubhavena nideśiteva sā nanāma māninyavaśā viśāṃpatim  || 4.46 ||

dṛśā narendreṇa nideśite svayaṃ śilātale nātividūravartini  |
upāviśatsā raśanāmaṇitviṣā niṣicyamāne'maracāpaśobhini  || 4.47 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavākyam

tayātidīrghairdaśanānupātibhirvikṛṣyamāṇāmiva bhūṣaṇāṃśubhiḥ  |
iti kṣitīśeṅgitavartmadīpikāmudīrayāmāsa giraṃ ramāṅgadaḥ  || 4.48 ||

anena vindhyādrivihārajanmanā śrameṇa kāmaṃ bhavatī kadarthitā  |
prasuptajūṭāhimukhāniloṣmaṇā jaṭāviṭaṅkendukaleva śūlinaḥ  || 4.49 ||

amī sarojapratime mukhe muhustavātapātāmrakapolabhittini  |
samunmiṣanti śramavāribindavo natāṅgi lāvaṇyasudhālavāsiva  || 4.50 ||

ito'vataṃsotpalalāsyadeśike nirantaraṃ gandhavahe vahatyapi  |
na ghūrṇate khinnalalāṭasaṅginī tavālakaśreṇiriyaṃ manāgapi  || 4.51 ||

anena pīnastanakampadāyinā nirāyatenodvahatā kaduṣṇatām  |
atha pravālādapi pāṭalacchavirna dūyate niḥśvasitena te'dharaḥ  || 4.52 ||

uditya paṅktyā śramavārivipruṣāṃ nirantarādhyāsitarekhayānayā  |
tavaiṣa kaṇṭhaḥ kuṭajāvadātayā vilāsamuktālatatayeva bhūṣyate  || 4.53 ||

idaṃ mahaccitramamānuṣaṃ tvayā vigāhyate yadvanamadvitīyayā  |
imāḥ kva vindhyasya bhuvo'tidurgamāḥ kva rājaveśmābharaṇaṃ bhavādṛśī  || 4.54 ||

navodgatāśokapalāśakāntinā nikāmaniryannakhacandrikeṇa ca  |
bibharṣi kasyedamanena pāṇinā vadāvadhūtendumarīci cāmaram  || 4.55 ||

nṛpasya kasyāpi paricchadāṅganā yadi tvamuccairvibhavo hi ko'pi sa  |
marutpatirmenakayeva tanvi yastvayāpi vālavyajanena vījyate  || 4.56 ||

atha rdhimatyā paravatyasi striyā kayāpi kāsau jagadekasundarī  |
natabhru yasyāḥ smaracāpayaṣṭayo vidheyatāṃ yānti bhavadvidhā api  || 4.57 ||

parasparaspardhi vilāsasampadāṃ trayaṃ bhavatsvāmitayā vikalpyate  |
marutvato vā ramaṇī ramātha vā kalatramardhenduvibhūṣaṇasya vā ?  || 4.58 ||

iyaṃ paribhrāntiragendrakandare sakhīva te śaṃsati kāryagauravam  |
bhavādṛśaḥ śvāpadadūṣite'nyathā carantyaraṇye kimadhīnanītayaḥ  || 4.59 ||

anena khelanmadadantinā vada tvamāgatā caṇḍi kuto duradhvanā  |
vidhāya viśleṣaviṣādamāvayoḥ svakāryaniṣṭhe kathaya kva yāsyasi ?  || 4.60 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

iti sābhihitā mṛgāyatākṣī samupoḍhapraṇayaṃ yaśobhaṭena  |
sahasā na jagāda lajjayā nu śramataḥ kiṃ tu nṛpastu tāmavocat || 4.61 ||

śrāntāsi kautukahṛtena kadarthitāsi praśnairanena vihito na tavopacāraḥ  |
ātithyameṣa kurute paramaṅgalekhā saṃvāhanaikacaturo niculānilaste  || 4.62 ||

evaṃ nisargamadhureṇa sudhārasaika niṣyandinā phaṇivadhūratha sā hasantī  |
candrāṃśunā kumudinīva dinoṣmataptā vītaklamā narapatervacasā babhūva  || 4.63 ||


iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye pāṭalādarśanaṃ nāma caturthaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


pañcamaḥ sargaḥ

atha sā vadane navendukānti smitamaṅke viniveśya cāmaraṃ ca  |
jagatīpatimevamā babhāṣe masṛṇaṃ mūrtimatī vidagdhateva  || 5.1 ||
[var5.1  {॰kānti॰|lem |em̭ ॰kānti॰ |ed}]

tvayi puṇyavaśena dṛṣtimāpte labhate śarma gataśramo mamātmā  |
udite hi virocane nalinyā vitamastāmarasaṃ vikāsameti  || 5.2 ||

atimātramupoḍhasaukumārye vacasi śrotramupeyuṣi tvadīye  |
jhaṭiti pratibhāsate mamāyaṃ kaṭhinaścandanapallavāvataṃsaḥ  || 5.3 ||

kimayaṃ mayi saṃbhramo'yamāstāṃ kimayaṃ na eva janaḥ paricchadaste  |
upacāraviśeṣasaṃvidhāne pratipaccandra iva uditastvameva  || 5.4 ||

bhavadiṅgitavedinaiva pṛṣṭā yadanenāhamupāntavartinā te  |
kimu tatkathayāmi sammataṃ cetkriyatāṃ me svayamājñayā prasādaḥ  || 5.5 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakanideśaḥ

abhidhehi lavaḥ kutūhalasya tvadudantaśravaṇe mamāpi jātaḥ  |
iti sā vasudhāpaternideśādatha pātālavilāsinī jagāda  || 5.6 ||


__________________________________________

bhujaṅgalokavarṇanam

sa tava śrutimāpta eva tāvatsmaralīlābhavanaṃ bhujaṅgalokaḥ  |
upayāti yadekadeśasāmyaṃ na mahī nāpi purī purandarasya  || 5.7 ||

atidurlabhasūryabhāsi yasmiṃstamasāmullasatāṃ tiraskriyāyai  |
phaṇino'ruṇakāntibhiḥ śirasthairmaṇibhirbālamivātapaṃ vahanti  || 5.8 ||

anulimpati rodasī yathenduḥ prabhayā māṃ na tathetyasūyayeva  |
paṭu yaḥ pramādāvilāsahāsa stabakairinduparamparāḥ prasūte  || 5.9 ||

savilāsamudastahastamuktairnikarairdigdviradendraśīkarāṇām  |
parito nicitāḥ sadaiva yasmin kakubhastārikā ivāvabhānti  || 5.10 ||

pratibhāti dadhan phaṇākalāpe pṛthivīṃ yatra saḥ śeṣanāgarājaḥ  |
nabhaso nipatañjavena bhittvā jagatīṃ gāṅga iva cyutaḥ pravāhaḥ  || 5.11 ||


__________________________________________

bhogavatīvarṇanam

viditā khalu vāsukestrilokyāṃ harakaṇṭhābharaṇasya bhogibhartuḥ  |
lalitādbhutabhūmirasti tasminnagarī bhogavatīti rājadhānī  || 5.12 ||

maṇiharmyatalāni ratnadīpāḥ phaṇikāntārdhavilokitāni vīṇāḥ  |
ṛtavo'pyakhilāḥ sametya yatra smarasāmrājyamahādhuraṃ vahanti  || 5.13 ||

anupādhirupāhṛto vikāsaḥ kamalairyatra vilāsadīrghikāsu  |
api yatra kumudvatībhirastaḥ sahajaścandramarīcipakṣapātaḥ  || 5.14 ||

abhikānabhisartumudyataḥ san sapadi vyālavilāsinīsamūhaḥ  |
bhavati svaphaṇāmaṇipradīpe timirotsāriṇi yatra sābhyasūyaḥ  || 5.15 ||

adhirohati yatra vaṃśamuktā paṭalasmerataṭā surasravantī  |
saritaḥ śriyamīrṣyayeva tasyāḥ suvate mauktikameva yatpayāṃsi  || 5.16 ||

atikāntaguṇābhirāmamūrtirmadhureṇa dhvaninā manoharantī  |
vidadhāti sadaiva yatra yūnāṃ padamaṅke vanitā ca vallakī ca  || 5.17 ||

śataśo vilasantyudaṃśuratna stabakāḥ kalpalatā yadaṅgaṇeṣu  |
pratimandriramevameva yasyāmapi cintāmaṇayaḥ pade luṭhanti  || 5.18 ||

api dattakutūhalāḥ surāṇāmapi vāñcchāpadamekapiṅgalasya  |
api nirviṣayā manorathānāmuragān yatra vibhūtayaḥ śrayante  || 5.19 ||

vasati svayameva yatra devaḥ sadā kalpitahāṭakeśvarākhyaḥ  |
smaramūrdhvavilocanārciṣīva tripuraṃ yaḥ śarapāvake juhāva  || 5.20 ||
[var5.20 {smaramūrdhva॰|lem |k̭ smaramūrdha॰ |ed}]


__________________________________________

śaṅkhapālavarṇanam

nijavaṃśaviśeṣako'sti tasyāmuragāṇāmadhipaḥ saḥ śaṅkhapālaḥ  |
sragasāviti yatphaṇāsu dhatte vasudhāṃ vāsukinā samānasāraḥ  || 5.21 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhāvarṇanam

jagadekalalāma tasya kanyā guṇavatyasti śaśiprabhā nāmnā  |
sahasaiva phaṇabhṛtāṃ praviṣṭā bhuvane rāhubhayādivendulekhā  || 5.22 ||

na kayāpyatiśayyate'tiśīghraṃ yadiyaṃ kandukakeliṣu bhramantī  |
aparaṃ kṛtamarthavattadasyāḥ sutarāṃ nāma sakhībhirāśugeti  || 5.23 ||

sphuradadbhutarūpasampadāṃ tāmanukartuṃ kalayāpi dhārṣṭyameti  |
na ratirna śacī na citralekhā na ghṛtācī na tilottamā na rambhā  || 5.24 ||

surakinnarasiddhakanyakābhiḥ svakalābhyāsavatībhirāptasakhyā  |
nikhilāsu gatā paraṃ prakarṣaṃ śitadhīḥ śaiśavaseva yā kalāsu  || 5.25 ||

anivāritakelikautukā sā sutanuḥ snehavaṃśavadena pitrā  |
viharatyapacīyamānabālyā haraśaile malaye himālaye ca  || 5.26 ||

adhunā puno'tra vindhyapade viharantyāḥ kusumāvacūḍanāmni  |
kvacidapyagamatpalāyya tasyāścapalaḥ kelimṛgo mṛgāyatākṣyāḥ  || 5.27 ||

ativatsalayā samaṃ sakhībhirvipine taṃ paritastayā vicitya  |
puline saritaḥ śaśāṅkasūteḥ śramavatyeyamanīyata triyāmā  || 5.28 ||

kalahaṃsakalasvanairvibuddhā parivārapramadānideśitaṃ sā  |
nikaṣā niculapravālaśayyāṃ tamatha prekṣitavatyāptanidram  || 5.29 ||

tapanīyaśilīmukhastadaṅge cakitaṃ citrarucau tayā ca dṛṣṭaḥ  |
jalade lalitendracāpabhaktau ahimāṃśoriva bhāsuro mayūkhaḥ  || 5.30 ||

aravindadalatviṣā kareṇa svayamutpāṭya sakautukaṃ gṛhīte  |
avalokitametayātha tasmin vijayāṅke navasāhasāṅkanāma  || 5.31 ||

aviśannaranātha nāma pūrvaṃ hṛdaye'syāḥ sahasātha puṣpaketuḥ  |
amṛtāṃśumarīciluptanidre labhate yatkumude'ntaraṃ dvirephaḥ  || 5.32 ||

navameghamalīmasādyugānte vasudhāmuddharato rathāṅgapāṇeḥ  |
paritaḥ śramavāribindavo ye nirapīyanta payodhaśuktiyūthaiḥ  || 5.33 ||

prakṛtiḥ kila yasya te parītā pariṇāmena tadantare'tra haṃsaḥ  |
śayanāntagataṃ mṛṇālaśaṅki shṛtavān hāramadhīralocanāyāḥ  || 5.34 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]

udaḍīyata khe mukhena bibhradbhujagendrapratimaṃ tamañjasā sa  |
apadārpitavaitaneyaśaṅkaḥ phaṇikanyāsu muhūrtaviklavāsu  || 5.35 ||


sa ca vindhyavanāntarājimetāmaviśanmārutapītapadmagandhām  |
atidūravikṛṣṭanāgakanyā cakitodañcitadīrghanetramālaḥ  || 5.36 ||

ahirājasutānideśato'sminasamāpteṣuvilokanotsavo'pi  |
atha haṃsamitastato vicetuṃ vijane nāgavadhūjanaḥ pravṛttaḥ  || 5.37 ||

śabalāsviha kṛṣṇasārayūthaiḥ samadakroḍasanāthapalvalāsu  |
gahanāsvapi tadgaveṣaṇāyai vanalekhāsu mama tvayaṃ prayatnaḥ  || 5.38 ||

sa mayā na taṭeṣu nirjharāṇāṃ sarasāṃ nāpsu na puṇḍarīkaṣaṇḍe  |
sarasīṣvapi nāyatormilekhā ntarapāriplavasārasāsu dṛṣṭaḥ  || 5.39 ||

sitacāmaradhāraṇe niyuktāṃ duhitastena bhujaṅgamādhipena  |
avadhāraya pāṭaleti nāmnā tanayāṃ māmuragasya hemanāmnaḥ  || 5.40 ||

acale vicayaḥ patatriṇo'yaṃ phalitaḥ sādhu mamāhitaśramo'pi  |
yaditaḥ puruṣottamo'si dṛṣṭo vanamadhye nibiḍaśriyopagūḍhaḥ  || 5.41 ||

uparodhamimaṃ na manyase cedyadi vāsmāsu tavāsti pakṣapātaḥ  |
vada taccaratā kvacidvane'smin saḥ sahāro vihagastvayā nu dṛṣṭaḥ  || 5.42 ||

na sa dṛṣṭimitastavāpi manye tadahaṃ tadvicayādito nivarte  |
nanu tāmyati kāmamāttacintā cirayantyāṃ mayi sāhirājakanyā  || 5.43 ||

atha vā mṛgabhaṅginopanīte vidhinā sā navasāhasāṅkabāṇe  |
praṇayārpitalocanā sanāmanyadhunāpyālikhiteva nūnamāste  || 5.44 ||


__________________________________________

puno'pi pāṭaloktiḥ

iti sā samudīrya tatpṛṣatkānavalokyaiva punaścamatkṛtā iva  |
sphuṭakelimṛgopanītabāṇa smaraṇasmeramukhī puno jagāda  || 5.45 ||

so nṛlokaśaśī tvameva manye mṛgayābaddharuciḥ sa yatpṛṣatkaḥ  |
bhuvanābhayadāyināmamīṣāṃ bhavataḥ saṃvadatīva sāyakānām  || 5.46 ||

sthitametadayomukheṣvamīṣu sphutavarṇaṃ tava nāmadheyalakṣma  |
avanītilaka tvayi prarūḍhaṃ mama sandehalavaṃ balātpramārṣṭi  || 5.47 ||

samupaiti sanāthatāṃ kimanyattritayena tritayaṃ narendracandra  |
tridivaṃ namucidviṣā tvayeyaṃ vasudhā vāsukinā rasātalaṃ ca  || 5.48 ||

sanaye nṛpatāvakhaṇḍitājñe tvayi śāsatyavanīṃ dilīpakalpe  |
vidadhīta padaṃ sudurnaye kastamṛte hāramalimlucaṃ vihaṅgam  || 5.49 ||

asatāmasuhṛnna lajjate'yaṃ kimiti jyākiṇalāñchito bhujaste  |
avanau yadanena rakṣitāyāṃ vayamasyāṃ mūṣitāḥ patatriṇāpi  || 5.50 ||

apayātu khagaḥ sastena kṛtyaṃ na hi me hāramiha tvameva yācyaḥ  |
na diśanti kimatra coraluptaṃ bata ṣaṣṭāṃśabhujo vasundharāyāḥ  || 5.51 ||

pathi cedavatiṣṭhase praṇīte manunā nātha tadarpyatāṃ sa hāraḥ  |
na narendra bhavādṛśāḥ kadācitpadavīṃ nyāyavidāṃ vilaṅghayanti  || 5.52 ||

nijamarthayase śilīmukhaṃ cedbhavatā yatnavatāpi kiṃ na labhyaḥ?  |
tvamanāgasi yacchaśiprabhāyāḥ praharan kelimṛge kṛtavyālīkaḥ  || 5.53 ||

atha vā mṛgayiṣyate na hāraṃ viśikhaṃ dāsyati coragendrakanyā  |
tvayi netrapathātithitvamāpte sulabho'syā hi mahājanoparodhaḥ  || 5.54 ||

atidūrato dṛśyate'yaṃ tvamivābhyunnatimānino nagendraḥ  |
vahati praṇayoparuddhakūlā kalahaṃsairamumantareṇa revā  || 5.55 ||

amṛtendukalāsahodarāsyāḥ sutanustīratale'vatiṣṭhate sā  |
urasīva payodhirājakanyā vanamālābharaṇe rathāṅgapāṇeḥ  || 5.56 ||

taditaḥ svayameva deva gatvā pulinaṃ somabhuvastaraṅgavatyāḥ  |
khagaluptavibhūṣaṇaṃ kṣitau te phaṇirājendrasutāṃ vibodhayehi  || 5.57 ||

c
iti tadvacasā smaraikadūta prasavollāsavasantavāsareṇa  |
so nṛpaḥ kamapi pramodamāpatghanarājidhvanineva nīlakaṇṭhaḥ  || 5.58 ||

avadacca vihasya pārthivendraḥ phaṇirājendrasutāvilāsinīṃ tām  |
vacasotkayatā mayūraśāvānnavajīmūtaninādasodareṇa  || 5.59 ||

api nāma mṛdūni vetsi vaktuṃ nipunaṃ nyāyamanujjhatī vacāṃsi  |
prasabhaṃ vihitāstvayā yadete vayamātmaskhalite'pi sāparādhāḥ  || 5.60 ||

idamomiti gṛhyate vacaste saha medhāvini kastvayā vivādaḥ  |
yadimā viśadodgamā giraste pathi siddhāntasamīkṣite caranti  || 5.61 ||

tadanena vinodayāśu tāvanmama hāreṇa manaḥ śaśiprabhāyāḥ  |
iha palvalasaikateṣu yāvattadalaṅkāramavekṣituṃ yatiṣyate  || 5.62 ||


__________________________________________

hārapradānam

atha hāramanādareṇa kaṇṭhātsvayamākṛṣya saḥ kṛṣṭacandraśobham  |
vihasannaravindakośatāmre nidade pāṇitale vilāsavatyāḥ  || 5.63 ||

api kośagṛhodare durāpaṃ phaṇināṃ bharturupoḍhavismayā sā  |
tamudañcitapakṣmaṇā mṛgākṣī likhitenaiva dadarśa locanena  || 5.64 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhāhārapradānam

avakṛṣya salīlamuttarīyātsitameghādiva tārakāvitānam  |
urasā sa babhāra hāramīśaḥ stanaparyaṅkaśayaṃ śaśiprabhāyāḥ  || 5.65 ||

vadati sma hasan ramāṅgadastāmuragastrīmatahbharturiṅgitajñaḥ  |
yadi kautukavatyamatsaraste taditaḥ kiṃ cidito'valokayeti  || 5.66 ||

atha hāralatāvikṛṣṭadṛṣṭiḥ sahasā vārivihaṅgamāvaluptam  |
uragendrasutāvibhūṣaṇaṃ tannṛpatervakṣasi pāṭalā dadarśaḥ  || 5.67 ||


__________________________________________

punaḥ pāṭalāvākyam

daśanacchadamāttabimbaśobhaṃ snapayantī sudhayeva hāsakāntyā  |
avadacca vācāṃsi pārthivaṃ sā caturaivaṃ parihāsapeśalāni  || 5.68 ||

apahartumagāstvameva hāraṃ kimitaḥ kalpitarājahaṃsarūpaḥ  |
vidito'si ghanā tavoragāṇāṃ nagare'styeva hi kāmarūpavārtā  || 5.69 ||

paravañcanapaṇḍitā matiste yadi naivaṃ kathamanyathā narendra  |
idamābharaṇaṃ harasyaraṇye harahāsaikasitaṃ śaśiprabhāyāḥ  || 5.70 ||

avanīvalaye tvamāttadaṇḍaḥ sahase nāvinayāṃśamityavaimi  |
atha ca sthitimātmanā vidhatse viṣaye dasyuniṣevite kimetat || 5.71 ||
72 kṣitipa smayase kimeṣa kelirna bhavatyarpaya hāramasmadīyam  |
nayavartmajuṣo bhavādṛśāḥ kiṃ na parasvagrahaṇādiha trapante  || 5. ||


athavā parato'stu narma nāsyāṃ bhuvi devena samo'sti pārthivo'nyaḥ  |
apaviddhanayā na yasya dṛṣṭerapasarpantyatha khecarāḥ kimanye  || 5.73 ||

anayoḥ kimapi tvayā vilāsin parivarto'yamakāri hārayoryaḥ  |
vacasāmavakāśamīśa datte sa na kasyānṛjucetaso janaḥ?  || 5.74 ||

vihito na hi vakṣasi tvayāyaṃ nṛpa hāraḥ phaṇirājakanyakāyāḥ  |
kimapīpsitamātmano vidhāturvihitaṃ sūtramidaṃ manobhavena  || 5.75 ||

nanu yāmyamunā śaśiprabhāyāstava hāreṇa mano vinodayāmi  |
tvayi sāpyalameva sāndracetaḥ sutanuḥ sneharasārdratāmupaitu  || 5.76 ||

ucitaṃ nijasāyakānurodhādahirājendrasutopasarpaṇaṃ te  |
parato'stu śarastvadarthitā sā kimu na tyāgavaśaṃvadā dadāti  || 5.77 ||

saraso'sya vimuñca tīralekhāmanurevā pulinaṃ gṛhāṇa yātrām  |
uragādhiparūpapakṣapātaṃ śamaya vyālavadhūvilocanānām  || 5.78 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhādarśanārthaṃ gamanam

iti valgu vaco niśamya tasyāḥ sa nṛpaḥ pannagavāmalocanāyāḥ  |
sthitamatra vacasyalaṅghanīye tava kalyāṇi mayeti tāmuvāca  || 5.79 ||

atha sānṛjuvādinī vidhāya kṣitipālaṃ purataścacāla bālā  |
hṛdayena parāmamarśa caivaṃ vilasanmāṃsalavismayo narendraḥ  || 5.80 ||

araṇyānī kveyaṃ dhṛtakanasūtraḥ kva sa mṛgaḥ kva muktāhāro'yaṃ kva ca saḥ patagaḥ kveyamabalā  |
kva tatkanyāratnaṃ lalitamahibhartuḥ kva ca vayaṃ svamākūtaṃ dhātā kimapi nibhṛtaṃ pallavayati  || 5.81 ||

atha madagajalīlākhelagāmī saḥ kṛtvā kanakaviśikhayācñāvyājamavyājakāntaḥ  |
avanihariṇalakṣmā sāhasopārjitaśrīrahiparivṛḍhakanyālokanāya pratasthe  || 5.82 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye śaśiprabhāvalokanārthaprasthānaṃ nāma pañcamaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


ṣaṣṭhaḥ sargaḥ


nāyikāvasthāvarṇanam

tadā phaṇīndrakanyāpi bāṇanāmāṅkasūcite  |
narendratilake tasminnabhilāṣaṃ babandha sā  || 6.1 ||

sahasā hṛdaye tasyā nidadhe manmathaḥ padam  |
aśokayaṣṭistabake sarāga iva ṣaṭpadaḥ  || 6.2 ||

avaśā sābhavaccitraṃ nāmnāpi pṛthivīpateḥ  |
na tadastyatra yannāstraṃ sannaddhasya manobhuvaḥ  || 6.3 ||

smarāgnikaṇameṇākṣyāstasyāḥ samudadīpayat |
makaradhvajasāmrājya sacivo malayānilaḥ  || 6.4 ||

sollasatsmaralīlena navena vayasā babhau  |
unmiṣanmadhulekhena mukuleneva mālatī  || 6.5 ||

sātanuśvasanaspṛṣṭa pāṭalādharapallavam  |
uvāha mukhamujjṛmbhamambhojamiva padminī  || 6.6 ||

nikāmasarale tasmin sā muhustaraledṛśau  |
śare narendracandrasya cikṣepa na sakhījane  || 6.7 ||

narendranāmāṅkalipistāmānandayati sma sā  |
kumudvatīmivodañcatbālendukiraṇacchaṭā  || 6.8 ||

hemapuṅkhāṅkite tasmin śare karamupeyuṣi  |
balādviveśa sā bālā dīkṣāṃ vaivāhikīmiva  || 6.9 ||

tenāvāptatadātāmra pāṇisparśeṇa patriṇā  |
sphuratkānticayavyājādamucyanteva pallavāḥ  || 6.10 ||

navīnasāhasāṅkasya kāmadevākṛterayam  |
mālavaikamṛgāṅkasya sindhurājasya sāyakaḥ  || 6.11 ||

punaḥpuna iti svādu nṛpaternāmalakṣma sā  |
apaṭhaccārubimbauṣṭha luṭhaddaśanacandrikā  || 6.12 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]

adhītya kautukenātha kṛtapañcamamūrcchanāḥ  |
tadagre nṛpanāmāṅkaṃ jaguḥ pātālakanyakāḥ  || 6.13 ||

adṛṣṭe'pyutsukā rājñi tadgītena babhūva sā  |
prabodhe yadanaṅgāgneḥ pañcamaḥ prathāmā samit || 6.14 ||

avepata samunmīlatvilāsakusumānncitā  |
manobhavānilasparśa vaśādvanalateva sā  || 6.15 ||

navānurāgamaṅgena vyaktataccihnacumbinā  |
vināvacanamācakhyau sakhīṣu caturāsu sā  || 6.16 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhāyāḥ praśnaḥ

sāvahitthamathetthaṃ sā sakhījanamabhāṣata  |
sudhāniṣyandajaḍayā śaradindumukhī girā  || 6.17 ||

abhedamindunā nītaḥ sācivyaṃ menaketunā  |
sakhyaḥ kaḥ sindhurājo'yaṃ sāhasāṅkanirūpyate  || 6.18 ||

kimevamavatiṣṭhadhvai maunaṃ muñcata śaṃsata  |
unmīlati yadantarme balātkautukakandalī  || 6.19 ||


__________________________________________

malyavatyā uttaram

atha mālyavatī nāma tatsakhī siddhakanyakā  |
iti smitasudhārdreṇa vacasā tāmavocata  || 6.20 ||

sakhi sāhāsikaḥ sa ayamavantitilako nṛpaḥ  |
gīyate ketakāpāṇḍu yasyoragapure yaśaḥ  || 6.21 ||

taṃ kāśyapīsahasrākṣamadrakṣamahamekadā  |
gatā śrīmanmahākāla parvaṇyujjayinīṃ purīṃ  || 6.22 ||

tanvi tigmāṃśuneva dyaurniśeva śaśalakṣmaṇā  |
sā sanāthā purī tena vajriṇevāmarāvatī  || 6.23 ||

rambhā tvayeva yatsatyaṃ tenāli nalakūbaraḥ  |
tyājito rūpajaṃ garvamurvīmakaraketunā  || 6.24 ||

akalaṅkākṛtestasya catuḥṣaṣṭhikalāvataḥ  |
tulādhirohahevāke kaḥ ṣoḍaśakalaḥ śaśī  || 6.25 ||

tasyāvanipradīpasya te te naisargikā guṇāḥ  |
kva nāma na prakāśante raveriva marīcayaḥ  || 6.26 ||

na nāgeṣu na siddheṣu na nareṣvamareṣvatha  |
avāpi kvāpi saṃvādastadrūpollekhalekhayā  || 6.27 ||
[var6.27 {amareṣvathlem |k̭ amareṣu ca |ed}]

so hi kenāpi kṛtyena gāṃ gataḥ śrūyate punaḥ  |
nihnutaikabhujadvandvo devaḥ śrīvatsalāñchanaḥ  || 6.28 ||

kimanyattava saṃtuṣṭyai paśya citre likhāmyaham  |
cirasya dīrghanayane tavāstu nayanotsavaḥ  || 6.29 ||


__________________________________________

ākṛtilekhanam

atha sā siddhatanayā taṃ lilekha śilātale  |
hṛdi tūragarājendra duhiturmīnaketanaḥ  || 6.30 ||

tadālikhitabhūpālaṃ tayā cintitakāmadam  |
cintāmaṇerapyadhikaṃ śilātalamamāṃsta sā  || 6.31 ||

tataścitragate tasminmahīpālo madālasāḥ  |
samamevāhikanyānāṃ peturnetraparamparāḥ  || 6.32 ||

papau śaśiprabhāpyenaṃ ciramutpakṣmalekhayā  |
punaruktīkṛtonnidra karṇendīvarayā dṛśā  || 6.33 ||

rūpamāsvādayāmāsa tasyālekhyagatasya sā  |
bhramarīvāravindasya sudhāsahacaraṃ madhu  || 6.34 ||

ānīyatākulatvaṃ sā tritīyena tanūdarī  |
vismayenātisāndreṇa madena madanena ca  || 6.35 ||

viveśa hṛdaye tasyāḥ sa citralikhito nṛpaḥ  |
śaratprasanne saritaḥ pratimenduriva ambhasi  || 6.36 ||

stanapatralatāṃ tasyā bibheda pulakodgamaḥ  |
satyaṃ yadantaraṅgeṇa bahiraṅgo nirasyate  || 6.37 ||

tasyāḥ kucayuge kiṃcinniśvāsaḥ kampamādadhe  |
rathāṅganāmamithune sāyantana ivānilaḥ  || 6.38 ||

tasyāḥ svedalavaśreṇi cchadmanā vadanaśriyā  |
viśato'ntaranaṅgasya lājāñjalirivojjhitaḥ  || 6.39 ||

śaṅke śaramṛjūkurvan dadṛśe manmathastayā  |
yatsā mṛdukvaṇatkāñci kiṅkiṇīkamakampata  || 6.40 ||

eṣa dṛṣṭastvayetyuktā sakhyā sā sādhvasākulā  |
gadgadākṣaramavyaktaṃ kṛcchrātprativaco dadau  || 6.41 ||

citravartinyapi nṛpe tattvāveśena cetasi  |
vrīḍārdhavalitaṃ cakre mukhendumavaśaiva sā  || 6.42 ||

dṛṣṭā sakhībhiḥ sākūtaṃ sā bālādharapallave  |
dadhau vailakṣyahasitaṃ prasūnamiva mādhavī  || 6.43 ||

navaṃ prema navotkaṇṭhā navāste te manorathāḥ  |
iti tasyāstathaivābhūdantaraṅgaḥ paricchadaḥ  || 6.44 ||

kiṃcittrapānuviddhena puṇḍarīkadalatviṣā  |
tamānarceva rājendumanimittasmitena sā  || 6.45 ||

sā ca doḥśayitabhuvā nṛpeṇāddhyāsitā hṛdi  |
kṛtāṅgabhaṅgavalanā jhaṭityālasyamāyayau  || 6.46 ||

tatkṣaṇenaiva sā citraṃ tanvī tanmayatāṃ yayau  |
kaṃ na pratārayatyeṣa kitavaḥ kusumāyudhaḥ  || 6.47 ||

stimitevāvatasthe sā sāraṅgāyatalocanā  |
acetaneva śūnyeva suptevālikhiteva ca  || 6.48 ||

smareṇa marmaṇi kvāpi sāvidhyata sumadhyamā  |
anidritāpi yatsābhūtkṣaṇaṃ mukulitekṣaṇā  || 6.49 ||

vyañcitānaṅgalīlena śṛṅgārarasabandhunā  |
tanvī navānurāgeṇa sānyaiva ghaṭitābhavat || 6.50 ||

ṛjunā aikṣata yaccitraṃ yadabhūcca trapāvatī  |
tenātigūḍhabhāvāpi sā sakhībhiralakṣyata  || 6.51 ||

athānaṅgavatī nāma sakhī tāmityavocata  |
daśanajyotsnayāraṇyaṃ sudhayeva niṣiñcatī  || 6.52 ||

kaccidasya pramodāya kumudasyeva cakṣuṣaḥ  |
ayaṃ madhyamalokenduḥ pātālendukale tava  || 6.53 ||

vanānilāhṛtonnidra padmakesaraśālinā  |
romodgama ivānena dhṛtastvadavalokanāt || 6.54 ||

adya naḥ saphalaṃ cakṣuścitre yadavalokitaḥ  |
kandarpādhikakānto'yamavantimṛgalāñchanaḥ  || 6.55 ||

tvāmapyavāñcitāṃ manye yattvayaitasya vakṣasi  |
ete savibhramaṃ nyaste dṛśau muktālate iva  || 6.56 ||

jitametena ko'pyeṣa satyaṃ kāmo'sya kiṅkaraḥ  |
ārohati parāṃ koṭimatra yattava sambhramaḥ  || 6.57 ||

tvamatra baddhabhāveva kimindumukhi lajjase  |
vilaṅghayatyalaṅghyāni smaradurlalitāni kaḥ  || 6.58 ||

ko'nyaḥ sakhi nṛśaṃso'sti kāmaṃ viṣamabāṇataḥ  |
sukumāre tavāpyaṅge yena vyāpāritaḥ śaraḥ  || 6.59 ||

bimbauṣṭhe eva rāgaste tanvi pūrvamadṛśyata  |
adhunā hṛdaye'pyeṣa mṛgaśāvākṣi lakṣyate  || 6.60 ||

ekena rājahaṃsena hṛto hārastanūdari  |
anena tu dvitīyena likhitenāpi te manaḥ  || 6.61 ||

kutastrapā tavālīṣu kiṃcidunnamayānanam  |
aho bata tvametasminnatyāyatakutūhalā  || 6.62 ||

etatkarṇotpalaṃ lolamapāṅgaprativeśitam  |
tvadudantamivaitasya kathayatyalikūjitaiḥ  || 6.63 ||

aho dūrasthitenāpi hṛdi spṛṣṭā nṛpendunā  |
indukāntaśileva tvamārdratāmavagāhase  || 6.64 ||

nipuṇe niḥśvasiṣyevamatigūḍhaṃ yathā yathā  |
tathā tathā tava vyaktamayamucchvasati smaraḥ  || 6.65 ||

smitametadalolākṣi lajjāsaṃvalitaṃ tava  |
idaṃ nirjitabālyasya yauvanasyoditaṃ yaśaḥ  || 6.66 ||

yathā taveyamaratiryathā sutanu vepase  |
tathā kavacitaḥ śaṅke niḥśaṅkaṃ madanastvayi  || 6.67 ||

kimatra karavai gāḍhamākalpakamidaṃ tava  |
iyaṃ ca manmathasyāstraṃ nirgatā cūtamañjarī  || 6.68 ||

surataklāntaśabarī kabarīmālyacumbinaḥ  |
kathamete tvayā tanvi sahyā malayavāyavaḥ  || 6.69 ||

kiṃ tāmyasi tavopāntamānayāmyadhunaiva tam  |
ityāśvāsayatīva tvāṃ kokilo'yaṃ kalasvanaḥ  || 6.70 ||

kūjantī kokilavadhūriyamādhiṃ dhunoti te  |
anaṅganṛpasāmrājya līlāmaṅgalagāyinī  || 6.71 ||

vanāntadevatāvāpta pādanyāsotsavaḥ sphuṭam  |
eṣa stabakito'śokaḥ suhṛtkāmasya kā gatiḥ  || 6.72 ||

atrorvītilake dṛṣṭimasyantīṃ tilakaḥ krudhā  |
ayaṃ tarjayatīva tvāṃ vātādhūtalatāṅguliḥ  || 6.73 ||

pathi smarasya viṣame skhalitāyāmitastvayi  |
smitacchaṭeva niryāti sinduvārasya mañjarī  || 6.74 ||

drāghayatyastabimbauṣṭha ruciniḥśvasitāni te  |
ayaṃ mukulitaḥ kiṃcidbakulo mukulastani  || 6.75 ||

latayā karṇikārasya puraḥ puṣpitayānayā  |
anaṅgasyaikarājye'smin hemavetralatāyitam  || 6.76 ||

aho na kasya bhindanti hṛdayaṃ vīkṣitā api  |
nisargamṛdavo'pyete sahakāranavāṅkurāḥ  || 6.77 ||

caturāṃ kokilāmeṣa kṛtvā kurabako mukhe  |
durlabhaṃ yācatīva tvāṃ līlāliṅganadohadam  || 6.78 ||

aśokaskandhalagneyaṃ kusumairnavamādhavī  |
prārthanīyapriyasparśāṃ hasati tvāmivotsukām  || 6.79 ||

bhūpatāvanuraktāyāstava santāpadīpanam  |
sthalāravindaṃ serṣyeva sūte sakhi vasundharā  || 6.80 ||

amīṣvaṅkasthakandarpa jagadvijagayasiddhiṣu  |
dṛṣṭirudvijate tanvi pāṭalākuḍmaleṣu te  || 6.81 ||

sundari dvitayasyātra kraśimā bhūṣaṇāyate  |
rājanyābaddhabhāvāyāstava rātreśca samprati  || 6.82 ||

eṣa caitrotsavaścitre nṛpo'yaṃ nūtanaṃ vayaḥ  |
prāptāvakāśaḥ kāmo'pi patitāsyatisaṅkaṭe  || 6.83 ||

kimālikhitavatyeṣā citre mālyavatī nṛpam  |
dvārīkṛteyamathavā vāmena vidhinā tava  || 6.84 ||

bahunā kiṃ cakorākṣi chalitāsi manobhuvā  |
sarvathā te kariṣyanti kuśalaṃ kuladevatāḥ  || 6.85 ||

ityādi vyāharantī sā kṛtabhrūbhaṅgayā tayā  |
narendrabāṇapuṅkhena kuce kiṃ cidatudyata  || 6.86 ||


__________________________________________

kalāvatīvacanam

atha kinnararājendra kanyā nāmnā kalāvatī  |
evaṃ vacaḥ smitasudhā niṣiktākṣaramādade  || 6.87 ||

kāmaṃ durlabhamevaitaccaitraścandrāṅkitā niśāḥ  |
preyān vipañcīraṇitaṃ pañcabāṇāṅkitaṃ vayaḥ  || 6.88 ||

ayi tvāṃ mlāpayatyeṣa kālaḥ kamalalocane  |
jagadāhlādajanakaḥ sudhāsūtirivābjinīm  || 6.89 ||

sthāne tavānurāgo'yamanaṅgasyāyamutsavaḥ  |
sakhi snihyati nirvyājamindāveva kumudvatī  || 6.90 ||

nṛpasyāraṇyasañcāraḥ śareṇānena sūcyate  |
marutā dviradasyeva madaniṣyandagandhinā  || 6.91 ||

avaśyaṃ tanvi cinvānā vane haṃsamitastataḥ  |
kvacidvilokayiṣyanti taṃ tvatparijanastriyaḥ  || 6.92 ||

jñātatvadiṅgitaivātra taṃ ceddrakṣyati pāṭalā  |
tato'sya ṣaṭpadasyeva balācceto hariṣyati  || 6.93 ||

sthirā bhava nṛpena tvamiha saṃyogamāpsyasi  |
yathā kaṇvāśrame pūrvaṃ duṣyantena śakuntalā  || 6.94 ||

iti tadvacasaḥ sīmni masṛṇotkampitastanī  |
vyājasācīkṛtamukhaṃ niśaśvāsa śaśiprabhā  || 6.95 ||

vanaśrīratnamañjīro latākuñjodare tataḥ  |
cukūja mañjukaṇṭhastāṃ dakṣiṇena kapiñjalaḥ  || 6.96 ||

harṣāśrulavakīrṇena satpatrāvalicāruṇā  |
vāmena pasphure tasyāścakṣuṣā ca stanena ca  || 6.97 ||

atrāntare samāyāntī dadṛśe dūratastayā  |
narendrasavitustasya puraḥ sandhyeva pāṭalā  || 6.98 ||

tasyāstraye ca tritayamapaśyatphaṇikanyakā  |
mukhe smitaṃ kare hāraṃ sitamaṃse ca cāmaram  || 6.99 ||

sā hārahastā ruruce dhṛtastimitacāmarā  |
sarasī suptahaṃseva phenādhyāsitapaṅkajā  || 6.100 ||

dṛṣṭiḥ phanīndraduhituratikramyaiva pāṭalām  |
bhṛṅgaśreṇirivāśoke papātāvantibhartari  || 6.101 ||

priyaṃ naḥ sa ayamāyāti paśya paśya śaśiprabhe  |
paramārānvayodāra hāramadhyamaṇirnṛpas || 6.102 ||

iti priyasakhīsūkti sudhāniṣyandalekhayā  |
sikte tadābhavattasyāḥ smaraḥ pallavito hṛdi  || 6.103 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]

sudhārasa ivorvībhṛttayā saṃmukhamāpatan  |
dṛśā sphaṭikaśuktyeva vāraṃvāramapīyata  || 6.104 ||

vitene'pyahikanyābhiḥ kautukenātanīyasā  |
tasyāgre rājahaṃsasya netrendīvaravāgurā  || 6.105 ||

avantitilakodantamupasṛtyātha pāṭalā  |
anaṅgadīpanaṃ tasyai jagādeṅgitavedinī  || 6.106 ||

deva paścātsthito'pyagre prāptaḥ paryutsuko bhavān  |
ityabhāṣyata bhūpālaḥ citre caturayā tayā  || 6.107 ||

abhūtparyākulā sā ca muhūrtamasitekṣaṇā  |
paścādalajjatālībhistathā sasmitamīkṣitā  || 6.108 ||

tasyai hāraṃ mahībharturarpayāmāsa pāṭalā  |
ākṛṣya bhartumānītamatiśuddhamiva āśayam  || 6.109 ||

sakhīnāmanurodhena sā kilānaṅgamohitā  |
hṛdi dīrghaguṇaṃ dadhre hāraṃ priyamivāparam  || 6.110 ||

tuṣārapāṇḍunā tena vavṛdhe'syā manobhavaḥ  |
digantavāntajyotsnena mahodadhirivendunā  || 6.111 ||

puṣpodgameneva latā prasādeneva bhāratī  |
sā tena reje hāreṇa yaśaseva narendratā  || 6.112 ||

tataḥ śithilaparyasta vilolakabarīlatā  |
ketuyaṣṭiriva śyāma patākāṅgā hiraṇmayī  || 6.113 ||

vasaṃtakamalollāsi mattabhramaraniḥsvanā  |
dīpamānopadeśeva svarahasye manobhuvaḥ  || 6.114 ||

ullasatkuṭajācchāccha vilāsahasitacchaviḥ  |
jhaṭityuadadhiveleva nirgacchadamṛtacchaṭā  || 6.115 ||

sarojinīva haṃsībhirbhṛṅgībhiriva mālatī  |
śaśilekheva tārābhiḥ sakhībhirabhito vṛtā  || 6.116 ||

dṛṣṭvā narandramāyāntamudasthādutsukātha sā  |
dadhatī nūtanaprema pariṇītāmadhīratām  || 6.117 ||
[syntax kulakam]

atha sutanuralolatārake sā suciramudañcitadīrghapakṣmamāle  |
śaśina iva navodgatasya dūrātavanipateḥ pathi locane mumoca  || 6.118 ||

āsannānucaradhṛtena paṅkajinyāḥ patreṇa sphuṭaghaṭitātapatralīlaḥ  |
saścchāyāmatha mahasā hasan himāṃśoḥ kṣmāpālaḥ pulinamavāpa narmadāyās || 6.119 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye śaśiprabhādarśano nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


saptamaḥ sargaḥ


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhādarśanam

tataḥ sa dūrādahirājakanyakāṃ javaikamitreṇā yutāṃ patatriṇā  |
viśāmpatidīrghaguṇānuṣaṅgiṇīmanaṅgakodaṇḍalatāmivaikṣata  || 7.1 ||

upoḍhalāvaṇyataraṅgabhaṅgayā sarāgabimbauṣṭhagalatpravālayā  |
ahāri lolaṃ nṛpatestayā balātanaṅgaratnākaravelayā manaḥ  || 7.2 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadakṛtaṃ śaśiprabhāvarṇanam

savismayo locanamārgamāptayā tayā saḥ pātālatalendulekhayā  |
athetthamamlānamanorathodgamo ramāṅgadenābhidadhe narādhipaḥ  || 7.3 ||

avaimi saiveyamamoghamāyudhaṃ manobhuvaḥ pannagarājakanyakā  |
udaṃśulekhena mukhena kurvatī jagadvilīnenduvilokanaspṛham  || 7.4 ||

narendra satyaṃ saḥ kutūhalī bhavedalaukikaṃ rūpamavekṣituṃ rateḥ  |
iyaṃ na yasyātithitāṃ gatā dṛśoragādhalāvaṇyasaritsumadhyamā  || 7.5 ||

iyaṃ sudhā mugdhavilāsajanmabhūriyaṃ bhujaṅgālayaratnadīpikā  |
iyaṃ jagannetracakoracandrikā puraḥ patākeyamayugmapatriṇaḥ  || 7.6 ||

iyattayā muktamavaimi naipuṇaṃ prajāpateradbhutaśilpakarmaṇi  |
nṛpopamānāmupamānatāṃ gataṃ vinirmitaṃ yena hi ratnamīdṛśam  || 7.7 ||

iyaṃ tavānena virājatetarāṃ karasthitenāvanipālapatriṇā  |
upeyuṣā kāñcanapadmamaṃśunā himetarāṃśoriva jahnukanyakā  || 7.8 ||

arālakeśīyamanena bhāti te śareṇa kārtasvarapuṅkhaśobhinā  |
nisargagaureṇa narendraraśminā sakajjalā dīpaśikheva śārvarī  || 7.9 ||

iyaṃ vilāsorminimagnaśaiśavaṃ svibhāvyamānastanakuḍmalodgamam  |
madaikavisrambhagṛhaṃ vigāhate vayo vibhaktāṅgamanaṅgadohadam  || 7.10 ||

iyaṃ natāṅgī jagadekasundare nideśite pāṭalayā nṛpa tvayi  |
dṛśau navendīvarapatrapeśale vimuñcati śrīriva śārṅgadhanvani  || 7.11 ||

anaṅgasāmrājyavilāsacāmare vanānilavyākulite'ṃsucumbini  |
pariślathe saṃyamanārthametayā salīlamastaḥ kabarībhare karaḥ  || 7.12 ||

vilokayantī kusumaṃ kacāgrataścyutaṃ kucasparśadhiyeva vakṣasi  |
iyaṃ kṛśāṅgī kusumāstraśaṅkayā kṛtapraṇāmeva tavāvalokyate  || 7.13 ||

vilokayāsyāḥ kṣitipāla bibhratīṃ pradīpaśobhāṃ kabarīṃ niśāmukhe  |
amī muhuḥ kuṅkumakesarāruṇā lasanti sīmantamaṇermarīcayaḥ  || 7.14 ||

virājate'syāstilako'yamañcito vikuñcitabhrūlatikāntare nṛpa  |
vijitya lokadvitayaṃ divaṃ prati smareṇa bāṇo dhanuṣīva saṃhitaḥ  || 7.15 ||

apāṅgasaṃvardhitaśoṇakāntinā sukṛṣṇatāreṇa tuṣārapāṇḍunā  |
iyaṃ pravālāsitaratnamauktikairvinirmiteneva cakāsti cakṣuṣā  || 7.16 ||

iyaṃ puro niryatidūramāyate kapolataḥ kāñcanakuṇḍalārciṣi  |
iṣuṃ ruṣeva pratibhoktumudyatā manobhuve muktaśilīmukhe hṛdi  || 7.17 ||

iyaṃ triyāmāpatikāntipeśalaṃ vibuddhabandhūkadalādhikatviṣā  |
bibharti bimbādharamudrayā mukhaṃ manobhirāmaṃ rajanīva sandhyayā  || 7.18 ||

upoḍhatārāpatitārahārayoriyaṃ yugena stanayorvirājate  |
dvayena deva uṣasi cakravākayoḥ saridgṛhītaikamṛṇālayoriva  || 7.19 ||

surāpagāvīcivipāṇḍureṣa te cireṇa hāraścaritārthatāṃ gataḥ  |
dhṛtaḥ kṣitīśa praṇayārdrayānayā salīlamutkampini yatkucadvaye  || 7.20 ||

iyaṃ mahīpāla vilokitena te vigāhate kāntamidaṃ daśāntaram  |
bhavatyanārūḍhavikāsavibhramā kimudgame candramasaḥ kumudvatī  || 7.21 ||

kimanyaduktaṃ sudhayeva sāndrayā tadanyathā nātha na pāṭalavacaḥ  |
sphuṭeyamasyāḥ kurute yadaṅgake balātpariṣvaṅgamanaṅgavikriyā  || 7.22 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhāsamīpagamanam

itīṅgitajñe vadati priyaṃvade ramāṅgadena smitamudgatasmitaḥ  |
avāpa paryāptaśaśāṅkadarśanaḥ phanīndrakanyāsavidhaṃ narādhipaḥ  || 7.23 ||


__________________________________________

nāyikāceṣṭāḥ

uvāha lajjānatamañcitālakaṃ tato bhujaṅgādhipateḥ sutāmukhaṃ  |
avāñcitaṃ kiṃ cana mātariśvanā saṣaṭpadaṃ padmamivāravindinī  || 7.24 ||

patatyadhaḥ kuṅkumapaṅkapāṭale mayūkhalekhāpaṭale śikhāmaṇeḥ  |
hriyeva raktāṃśukapallavastayā pidhātumālekhyanṛpe nyadhīyata  || 7.25 ||

nṛpasya citre madhureyamākṛtirna bhidyate candramaso yathāmbhasi  |
sakhījanastāmiti narmapeśalaḥ śanaistrapānamramukhīmabhāṣata  || 7.26 ||

yathāsmi vaktāsi tathāsya bhūpateryadā punaḥ karṇasamīpamāpsyasi  |
itīva nirvyājamudīrya tatyaje tayāyatākṣyā sa narendrasāyakaḥ  || 7.27 ||

alaṃ hriyaivaṃ samupekṣya gṛhyatāmayaṃ hi pāṇigrahaṇocitastava  |
itīrayantyāḥ kuṭilaṃ vacaḥ karādanaṅgavatyāḥ kamalaṃ jahāra sā  || 7.28 ||

atha svabimbādharapāṭalacchadaṃ kareṇa tattatsadṛśena bibhratī  |
niṣevituṃ bhūpatimabhyupāgatā rameva rājīvamukhī rarāja sā  || 7.29 ||

dṛśastatastatparivārayoṣitāṃ netrāñjanaśyāmalapakṣmarājayaḥ  |
śaśiprabhe bhūmipatau sakautukāḥ samāpatan kunda ivālipaṅktayaḥ  || 7.30 ||


__________________________________________

nṛpārhaṇam

nipīyamānasya tayā śanaiḥśanairapāṅgasañcāritadīrghanetrayā  |
upāttapuṣpaḥ kṣitibharturarhaṇāṃ cakāra tasyāścaturaḥ sakhījanaḥ  || 7.31 ||

puraḥ śirasyāhitamanmathājñayā tayā kaṭākṣaiḥ kuṭajairivārcitaḥ  |
nṛpaḥ saparyāṃ punaruktasaṃvidāṃ sastadvayasyānihitāvamanyata  || 7.32 ||

sarāgavatyutkalikābhirākule mṛdunyalaṃ pāṭalayā samarpite  |
atha nyaṣīdannavapallavāsane phanīndrakanyāmanasīva pārthivaḥ  || 7.33 ||

anu kṣitīśaṃ nalinīdalāsane kayā cidāste parivārayoṣitā  |
ramāṅgado'pyāsanabandhamādade vasundharāyāṃ vinayaikabandhuḥ  || 7.34 ||

śramānurodhādupaviśyatāmitaḥ kṣaṇaṃ kṛśāṅgīti sakhībhirarthitā  |
upāviśadvepitavāmanastanī tataḥ samaṃ tābhirahīndrakanyakā  || 7.35 ||


__________________________________________

nāyikāvilasitāni

vivartayantī vadanendumaṇḍalaṃ viśālanetrāntaniṣaktatārakam  |
dvayena sā kṛṣyata sundarī samaṃ hriyā nṛpālokanakautukena ca  || 7.36 ||

athāvataṃsīkṛtalocanotpalaṃ kapoladolāyitaratnakuṇḍalam  |
ciraṃ papau saḥ stimitena cakṣuṣā tadānanaṃ mālavamīnaketanaḥ  || 7.37 ||

kṛtī dṛśāsyāḥ sudṛśaḥ pibatyayaṃ kapolalāvaṇyasudhāṃ narādhipaḥ  |
mithaḥ sakhīnāmiti sasmitaṃ vaco niśamya sābhūdadhikādhikatrapā  || 7.38 ||

manāgivāṃsādapavartitānanā nirīkṣya taṃ bhūpatimarpitekṣaṇam  |
vihasya lajjāmukule cakarṣa sā balādapāṅgaprasṛte vilocane  || 7.39 ||

yaśobhaṭe rūpamavantiśasituḥ kṛtasmite citragataṃ praśaṃsati  |
karasthitaṃ sā jhaṭiti nyadhāttataḥ śilātale tāmarasaṃ trapāvatī  || 7.40 ||

nikāmamuktaṃ sukumāramaṅganā vilāsinastasya jahāra sā manaḥ  |
smaraikadūtīsahakāraśākhino lasanmadhāvanyabhṛteva pallavam  || 7.41 ||

tadīyamuddāmarasorminirbharaṃ sa rājahaṃso'pi viveśa mānasam  |
kṛtapraveśaśca salīlamacchinanmanāṅmṛṇālīmiva dhīratāmataḥ  || 7.42 ||

kṣaṇādapāṅgastimitāyatākṣayoḥ sakampayoḥ kaṇṭakitāṅgalekhayoḥ  |
avāpadanyonyanibaddhabhāvayostayoḥ prarohaṃ hṛdi bālamanmathaḥ  || 7.43 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakasya nāyikāṃ pratyuktiḥ

asūyayevātha vimuñcatī dṛśaṃ sakhīṣu sā sūtritanarmasūktiṣu  |
iti smitakṣālitadantavāsasā nṛpeṇa nāgendrasutābhyadhīyata  || 7.44 ||

vadānavadyāṅgi sakhījanādṛtaḥ kimeṣa nāma vyatiricyate janaḥ  |
vihāya visrambhaviśeṣametayā yadaṅgamanto viśatīva lajjayā  || 7.45 ||

sadā sadācārapareti vārtayā vayaṃ hṛtāḥ pannagarājaputri te  |
ataḥ kimevaṃ pratipattimūḍhatāṃ vigāhase'smāsu vimucyatāmiyam  || 7.46 ||

anena te sundari darśanena vā kṛtopacāro'smi kiyatkadarthyase  |
na vīkṣate valgu na mañju bhāṣate gatā kvacillocanavartmamālatī  || 7.47 ||

adhaḥkṛtāḥ satyamadhīralocane rasātalena tridivasya bhūmayaḥ  |
anaṅgadurvāraśarādhidaivataṃ bhavadvidhaṃ ratnamavāpyate'tra yat || 7.48 ||

kutūhalādhyāsitamadhyalokayā tvayā muhūrtaṃ phaṇilokakaumudi  |
avaimi pātālamavāptasandhinā vilaṅghyate santamasena samprati  || 7.49 ||

idaṃ mṛṇāladapi komalaṃ vapustavaiṣa dūrādaravindinīpatiḥ  |
punaḥ punaḥ saṃspṛśatīva kautukāttamālagulmāntarapātibhiḥ karaiḥ  || 7.50 ||

anena te saśramavāribindunā navīnapīyūṣatuṣāradanturaḥ  |
śirīṣamṛdvaṅgi tuṣārapāṇḍunā kapolabimbena viḍambyate śāśī  || 7.51 ||

idaṃ vadāśikṣata kaitavaṃ kutastavaiṣa mugdhe saralāṅguliḥ karaḥ  |
ihaitadālikhya śilātale śanairanena līlākamalaṃ yadujjhitam  || 7.52 ||

na citramevaṃ kva cidasti bhūtale mamātra tenāsti kutūhalaṃ mahat |
ataḥ kimetatpihitaṃ prakāśyatāmahetukastanvi ka eṣa matsaraḥ  || 7.53 ||

adṛśyametadyadi manyase tataḥ kimarthyase kiṃ tviyadeva śaṃso naḥ  |
kṛtī tvayāyaṃ bhujagāmbaraukasāmalekhi citre katamaḥ kṛśodari  || 7.54 ||

lateva sammīlitaṣaṭpadasvanā kiyacciraṃ nirvacanaiva tiṣṭhasi  |
itaścakorākṣi vicintya sūnṛtaṃ mamottaraṃ kiṃ cana dātumarhasi  || 7.55 ||

yathātijihreṣi yathātivepase yathā kapole pulakaṃ bibharṣi ca  |
tathātra manye tava pakṣapātavannitāntamantaḥkaraṇaṃ kṛśodari  || 7.56 ||

hriye taveyaṃ yadi kalpate kathā kimetayā naḥ prakṛte yatāmahe  |
yadarthamete vayamāgatāḥ svayaṃ sa nārpyate kiṃ karabhoru sāyakaḥ  || 7.57 ||

salīlamevaṃ vadati smitānane nṛpe navapremasārdracetasi  |
vivartayantī maṇikaṅkaṇaṃ kare mumoca maunaṃ na phaṇīndrakanyakā  || 7.58 ||


__________________________________________

mālyavatīvacanam

tayā tathā dṛṣṭamathāntarāntarā kaṭākṣakāntiḥ snapitāvataṃsayā  |
iti smitāpyāyitadantadīdhitirjagāda taṃ mālyavātī viśāmpatim  || 7.59 ||

tavaitayā satkṛtipātra satkṛtaṃ svayaṃ na yatkalpitamalpamadhyayā  |
na sa avalepo na ca sā pramāditā na ca trapā tatra nṛpo'parāddhyati  || 7.60 ||

kṛtīti vārtā tava vetsi vāñchitaṃ tvamantarātmeva na kasya vā bhuvi  |
ataḥ sakhībhāvagatasya gopanaṃ na yujyate nastvayi tanniśāmyatām  || 7.61 ||

śilīmukhe'smiṃstava nāmalāñchite mṛgopanīte mṛgaśāvalocanā  |
pramodamāpteyamito vilokite kare cakorīva tuṣāradīdhiteḥ  || 7.62 ||

ka eṣa rājeti muhuḥ kutūhalādiyaṃ yadā pṛṣṭavatī sakhījanam  |
atastadāsyai kathitaḥ savistaraṃ mayā tvamurvītalamīnalāñchānaḥ  || 7.63 ||

kareṇa sāsūyamapāsya karṇataḥ kvaṇaddvirephāvali nīlamutpalam  |
tadaitayābhyudgatapakṣapātayā śrutā guṇāḍhyasya bṛhatkathā tava  || 7.64 ||

imāṃ tvadākāranirūpiṇe sakhīmavetya paryutsukalocanāmiva  |
tato mayā viśvavilocanotsavastvameva citre likhito'si pārthiva !  || 7.65 ||

udañcitavyāyatapakṣmaṇā tataḥ sakhīsamakṣaṃ nibhṛtena cakṣuṣā  |
ciraṃ nipītasatṛṣeva mugdhayā tvayaitayā madhyamalokavāsasaḥ  || 7.66 ||
[var7.66 {tvayaitayā|lem |em̭ tvayetayā |ed}]

ato varo'yaṃ yuvayoḥ samāgamaḥ kumudvatīcandramasorivocitaḥ  |
iyaṃ hi bālocchvasito manobhuvastvamatra lokatritayaikasundaraḥ  || 7.67 ||

nṛpa gṛhītuṃ nayamatra ko'pyalaṃ sureṣu vā pannagapuṅgaveṣu vā  |
sa eṣa paṅkeruhakarṇikāmṛdustvayā śayo'syāḥ kriyate sakautukaḥ  || 7.68 ||

ayācito'pyarpita eva te śaraḥ kṣiterayaṃ nyāyavidāṃ varaitayā  |
tadenamabhyarthayase kathaṃ punaḥ kalatrameṣā hi vasundharā tava  || 7.69 ||
[7.69 em. kṣitir?]

itītivṛttaṃ tadupāśrayaṃ tathā prakāśayantī pṛthivīpatiṃ prati  |
chalādalīkabhṛkuṭiṃ vidhāya sā tayāluloke phaṇirājakanyayā  || 7.70 ||

atha dvirephasya mukhābjapātino nivāraṇayoragarājakanyakā  |
śilātalātsaṃbhramamīlitasmṛtistadāśu līlāśatapatramādade  || 7.71 ||

tataḥ sa romañcanipīḍitāṅgado ramāṅgadaṃ vyaktaśaśiprabheṅgitaḥ  |
apaśyadindīvaradāmadīrghayā pramodavistaritayā dṛśā nṛpaḥ  || 7.72 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavacanam

samarpitā pārthiva puṣpaketunā taveyamārdrapraṇayā manasvinī  |
asīmasaundaryasudhāvilāsabhūrudanvatevendukalā pinākinaḥ  || 7.73 ||

kimanyadatrollasitaṃ jagattraye tavaiva saubhāgyapatākayā nṛpa  |
yadāgatā manmathapatriṇāmiyaṃ śaravyatāmevamapi śrute tvayi  || 7.74 ||
