vadhūrdilīpasya sudakṣiṇā yathā yathā sunandā bharatasya bhūpateḥ  |
raghūdvahasyāvanikanyakā yathā tathā taveyaṃ vidhinā upapāditā  || 7.75 ||

kimanyadasyāḥ kṛtapāṇipīḍanaḥ padaṃ nidhatse gṛhamedhināṃ dhuri  |
iti prasarpatsmitacandrikaḥ śanairabhāṣatorvītilakaṃ ramāṅgadaḥ  || 7.76 ||
[syntax kulakam]

vilokitaṃ citramalīkabhāṣiṇī bhavatsakhīyaṃ pratibhāsate mama  |
udīritaivaṃ kila pārthivena sā bhṛśaṃ lalajje nibhṛtaṃ jahāsa ca  || 7.77 ||

vilokayantī tamapāṅgalocanā samullasatsvedalavāṅkitastanī  |
tataḥ sujātastabakāstamauktikā lateva sā hemamayī vyakampata  || 7.78 ||

mukhe tavāsaktamidaṃ śaśiprabhe dṛśāvataṃsāgatayaitadīyayā  |
sakhījanaḥ sasmitamityuvāca tāmavantināthaṃ ca mitho ramāṅgadaḥ  || 7.79 ||

atrāntare jhaṭiti cittamivācchamambhaḥ kṣobhaṃ jagāma saritastuhināṃśusūteḥ  |
vāti sma ca prasabhabhagnatamālatāla hintālasālasaralaḥ sahasā samīraḥ  || 7.80 ||


__________________________________________

payododayaḥ

udanamadatha tatkṣaṇādudañcatkanakapiśaṅgataḍillataḥ payodaḥ  |
adharitamurajadhvanīni muñcan vidhuritakarṇatalāni garjitāni  || 7.81 ||

atha samuditatrāsā meghasvanānnanu bhūpaterbhujaparighayoranto bālā praveṣṭumiyeṣa sā  |
kimasi cakitā mā tvaṃ bhaiṣīrito bhava lajjayā kṛtamiti ca tāmūce devaḥ sa sāhasalāñcanaḥ  || 7.82 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkaguptasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye śaśiprabhāsaṃllāpo nāma saptamaḥ sargaḥ samāptaḥ

************************************************


aṣṭamaḥ sargaḥ


nāyikātirodhānam

sāpade'tha bhujagendrakanyakā khe payodapatalaistirohite  |
narmadāpulinapallavasthitā haṃsapaṅktiriva kampamādade  || 8.1 ||

spardhayeva nicayaḥ payomucāṃ tāramandramanadadyathā yathā  |
sā narendratilakaṃ tamaikṣata mugdhalolanayanā tathā tathā  || 8.2 ||

ullasannavapayodharālasāṃ dyāṃ ca tāṃ ca sumukhīṃ vilokayan  |
bhūpatiḥ sa nitarāmabhūdvaśo vismayasya ca manobhavasya ca  || 8.3 ||

vāmanatvamalinatvamatyajan vyañjitakrama iva trivikramaḥ  |
krāmati sma sahasaiva meghabhūrandhakāranikaro'tha rodasī  || 8.4 ||
[8.4 andhaka pun?]

netrarodhini tayorna kevalaṃ mūrcchite tamasi vaiśasaṃ hṛdi  |
yāvadāsyavigaladbisāṅkure cakravākamithune'pyajṛmbhata  || 8.5 ||

karṇabhūṣaṇamaṇiprabhālavaistokalakṣitakapolapatrayā  |
kṛṣyamāṇanayano'tha pipriye pannagendrasutayā tayā nṛpaḥ  || 8.6 ||

tejasi sphurati tāḍite muhurbibhyatī kanakabhaṅgapiṅgale  |
taṃ hṛdisthitamiveśamīkṣituṃ sāpi mīlitavilocanābhavat || 8.7 ||


__________________________________________

ākāśavāṇī

eṣa te na ghaṭate manorathaḥ pārthivāhipatikanyakāṃ prati  |
gaccha vindhyavipināntadṛṣṭayā vañcito'si mṛgatṛṣṇayaitayā  || 8.8 ||

sāhasodadhiviloḍane svayaṃ yo'ṃsamaṃsalatayārpayiṣyati  |
śrīrivorasi muradviṣaḥ padaṃ tasya niścitamiyaṃ vidhāsyati  || 8.9 ||

paśya paśya capaleyamantikātnīyate tava yathocitaṃ kuru  |
evameva yadiyaṃ na labhyate jānakīva janakapratijñayā  || 8.10 ||

kanyakāsi kimidaṃ śaśiprabhe yuktamehi piturantikaṃ vraja  |
ityavigrahavatī nabhastalātullalāsa sahasā sarasvatī  || 8.11 ||

tāṃ niśamya sa nikāmavismitaḥ sācikārmukalatāmalokata  |
indumauligalakandalāsitaṃ tatkṣaṇācca timiraṃ nyavartata  || 8.12 ||

sācirāṃśutapanīyamekhalā śakracāpamaṇikuṇḍalā tataḥ  |
kvāpi mudritamayūratāṇḍavā saṃhatirjalamucāṃ tirodadhe  || 8.13 ||

sā purātanapathena pāvanī somasūtirapi nimnagā vahat |
svādunirmalarasorminirbharā bhāratīva masṛṇaṃ mahākaveḥ  || 8.14 ||

apyapākṛtarayaḥ sa narmadā vīcilāsyaracanākutūhalī  |
āvavau śabararājayoṣitāṃ nartitālakalataḥ samīraṇaḥ  || 8.15 ||

ityakālajaladādivaikṛte kena cidviracite gate śamam  |
bhūpateḥ śaśimukhī sakhīyutā netranirviṣayatāmavāpa sā  || 8.16 ||

kvāpi nūnamapahṛtya tanmano nāgarājaduhitā jagāma sā  |
utpalasya sarasaṃ lasatspṛhā cakravākavaniteva kesaram  || 8.17 ||

agrataḥ kṛtapayodharaśriyā dhautataptatapanīyakāntayā  |
vidyuteva sadṛśā tayākulaḥ sa abhavajjhaṭiti dṛṣṭanaṣṭayā  || 8.18 ||

sā puro mama hṛteti lajjayā cintayā kimiva sā carediti  |
sā puno na sulabhetyasau śucā tapyate sma tisṛbhiḥ kṣitīśvaraḥ  || 8.19 ||

tasya tāpajananena mānasaṃ tena bālaviraheṇa vivyathe  |
ketakacchadakadarthane paraṃ yatkaṇo'pi śiśiraḥ pragalbhate  || 8.20 ||

jitvaraṃ jagati puṣpaketunā tadvikṛṣya tarasā śarāsanam  |
tāḍyate sma hṛdaye patatṛiṇā so'tha mālavakuraṅgalāñchanaḥ  || 8.21 ||

lajjayā valitakaṇṭhakandalaṃ locanāñcalamiladvataṃsakam  |
tasya vartitamivābhavattadā tatpriyāvadanamunnasaṃ hṛdi  || 8.22 ||

mlānimāpa sastayā vinā nṛpastatra pannagapatestanūjayā  |
svāṃ ruciṃ na hi kadā cidaśnute śarvarīvirahadhūsaraḥ śaśī  || 8.23 ||

pāṇḍupakṣmaladṛśaḥ paricyutaṃ so'tha mālyaśakalaṃ vyalokata  |
tādṛśi vyatikare vinirgataṃ hāsaleśamiva puṣpadhanvanaḥ  || 8.24 ||

śaṃsadujjvalakapolasaṃgataṃ kuṅkumena dalakoṭicumbinā  |
sambhrameṇa galitaṃ natabhruvaḥ karṇatāmarasamādade'tha sa  || 8.25 ||

kārmuke sati śareṣu satsvapi preyasī tava hṛtāntikāditi  |
tena saurabhahṛtāliniḥsvanairvācyateva nṛpatervyadhīyata  || 8.26 ||

koṣṇaniḥśvasitavepitacchadaṃ tanniveśya vadane saḥ sādaraḥ  |
ardhanimīlitalocanotpalaḥ pustakalpita ivābhavatkṣaṇam  || 8.27 ||

narmadormilulite tadaṃśuke pallavālikhitahaṃsahāriṇi  |
āhṛtastanavilepane dṛśā so'kṛtapraṇayamindupāṇḍuni  || 8.28 ||

mā viṣīda navasāhasāṅka, te kāntayā gatamanena vartmanā  |
paśya tatpadamitīva revayā tasya sārasarutairasūcyata  || 8.29 ||

kiṃ nimagnamiha bālayā tayā bhītayā bhujagarājakanyayā  |
naivamatra niyataṃ rasātale vidyate vivaramityatarkayat || 8.30 ||

so'timātragahane'pi raṃhasā pārthivaḥ patitumaicchadambhasi  |
jīvitaṃ tṛṇamivāvajānate sāhasavyasanino'pi tādṛśāḥ  || 8.31 ||

tatsamīhitamavantivāsavastasya nāvadadupāntavartinaḥ  |
eṣa vighnamiha sāhasotsave kalpayiṣyati mameti śaṅkitaḥ  || 8.32 ||

ujjhati sma saḥ śanaiḥ samucchvasan pallavāsanamupāsya śāsanaḥ  |
agrahīcca saśaraṃ kareṇa tadvyaktarājakakudena kārmukam  || 8.33 ||

pannagendraduhituḥ kareṇa yaḥ sakhyamāpadaravindabandhunā  |
arpitaṃ praṇayinā tamapyasau sāyakaṃ kanakapuṅkhamādade  || 8.34 ||

subhruvaḥ smaravilāsadeśikaṃ taṃ śaśaṃsa saḥ śaraṃ patatriṣu  |
pītaśītakaramūrtitānavaṃ bhānumāniva mayūkhamaṃśuṣu  || 8.35 ||

vyaktataccaraṇalakṣmaṇā tataḥ srastakeśakusumākulālinā  |
meghasiktasikatenavartmanā narmadājalasamīpamāpa sa  || 8.36 ||

eṣa jātu na vikatthate kva cillakṣyate'sya phalataḥ sadā kriyā  |
tatkariṣyati kimatra sāhasaṃ cetasīti vidadhe ramāṅgadaḥ  || 8.37 ||


__________________________________________

narmadāpraveśaḥ

akṣipattaṭaśilāviṭaṅkataḥ pārthivaḥ svamatha narmadāmbhasi  |
vāridheḥ payasi viśvadīpakaḥ sāyamadriśikharādivāryamā  || 8.38 ||

tatra mīnamakarākule patanāsasāda sa vilāsamiśvaraḥ  |
yāmunāmbhasi nipātinaḥ purā gopatāmupāgatasya śārṅgiṇaḥ  || 8.39 ||

ambhasastadavapātatāḍitādūrdhvametya nipatatsu binduṣu  |
sāhasena paritoṣitaiḥ surairmauktikārgha iva tasya cikṣipe  || 8.40 ||

kiṃ cidantaritamūrtibhiḥ kṣaṇādanvagacchadatha taṃ ramāṅgadaḥ  |
yena yātyaruṇasārathiḥ pathā vāsarastamavalambate na kim ?  || 8.41 ||

kastulāgramadhiropya jīvitaṃ svāminaṃ tvamiva sevatāmiti  |
tasya haṃsaninādena valgunā sādhuvādamiva narmadā dadau  || 8.42 ||


__________________________________________

bilapraveśaḥ

tau muhurjalacarairadṛśyatāmagrataścakitamuktavartmabhiḥ  |
dhvāntasantatibhide rasātalaṃ prastitau raviniśākarāviva  || 8.43 ||

āśrayatyavanimeghavāhane vārigarbhamabhito garīyasi  |
prāpa mekalasutā samānatāmantarāhitanidhānayā bhuvā  || 8.44 ||

ujjhitā jhaṭiti kāryagauravādīśvareṇa ruruce na medinī  |
sodyamena puno'pyavaiṣyatā bhānuneva padavī payomucām  || 8.45 ||

āpapāta saramāṅgadaḥ kṣaṇātsarvataḥ sastamasāvile bile  |
gūḍhamatsaraviṣe viśeṣavān durjanasya manasīva sadguṇaḥ  || 8.46 ||

yadbabhūva purato'sya bhūpaterekakuṇḍalapaṭāsitaṃ tamaḥ  |
tasya taddinakarāṃśubhāsurairmauliratnakiraṇairabhajyata  || 8.47 ||

sandrahemarajasā mahaujasāmagraṇīragarudhūpagandhinā  |
so'tha tena bilavartmanā śanaiḥ krośamātramagamannareśvaraḥ  || 8.48 ||


__________________________________________

siṃhadarśanam

lagnasāndragajaśoṇitacchaṭaiḥ śauryapāvakaśikhāṅkurairiva  |
kesarairatikarālakandharo mārgamasya rurudhe'tha kesarī  || 8.49 ||

muktagharghararavaḥ sa raṃhasā taṃ viśāmadhipamabhyadhāvata  |
vyāttadīrghadaśanāsyakandaraḥ pūrṇamindumiva siṃhikāsutaḥ  || 8.50 ||

ardhacandramatha tajjighāṃsayā saṃdadhe dhanuṣi yāvadīśvaraḥ  |
tāvadasphuṭitakorakaṃ puro bālakundaviṭapaṃ tamaikṣata  || 8.51 ||

bibhrato vikaṭadaṃṣṭramānanaṃ kālameghaśakalāsitatviṣaḥ  |
āyato'bhimukhamīrṣyayā javāttena vartma mumuce na potriṇaḥ  || 8.52 ||


__________________________________________

gajadarśanam

kiṃ cidasya purato'tha gacchataḥ karṇatālavidhutālipaṅktinā  |
ruddhyate sma samadena paddhatirdīrghadantamusalena dantinā  || 8.53 ||

mandrakaṇṭhaninado'tivegavānūrdhvavāladhirudagralocanaḥ  |
kuṇḍalīkṛtakarastamabhyagātsaḥ krudhā nibhṛtakarṇapallavaḥ  || 8.54 ||

yāvadaṅkuritamatsaro'bhavattasya saṃmukhamadhijyakārmukaḥ  |
tāvadaikṣata na saḥ kva ciddvipaṃ rājagandhamadagandhakesarī (|edemends? gandhamada to gandhavaha)  || 8.55 ||

utpatannipatadagrato muhurmuñcadaṭṭahasitaṃ sahārciṣā  |
kevalaṃ kapilakuntalaṃ śiraḥ paśyato'sya na camatkṛtaṃ manaḥ  || 8.56 ||

evamādi yadabhūnmahīpateradbhutaṃ pathi bibhīṣikāvaham  |
tadbibheda nijasattvasampadā tigmadīdhitiriva tviṣā tamaḥ  || 8.57 ||


__________________________________________

sariduttaraṇam

tāṃ dadarśa saritaṃ sudustarāmagrato'tha bilakalpavinnṛpaḥ  |
sparśataḥ kila yadambhasāṃ jhaṭityaśmabhāvamupayāntyasūrayaḥ  || 8.58 ||

mārutairaparapāranunnayā prāṃśu vaṃśalatayā saḥ sānugaḥ  |
tāmalaṅghayadathopagūḍhayā janmabhītimiva yogavidyayā  || 8.59 ||


__________________________________________
nagaradarśanam

prasthitastadanu sodyamaṃ puraḥ sa atha sāhasavatāṃ puraḥsaraḥ  |
nirmitaṃ maṇimayūkhapallavairbālamātapamiva vyalokata  || 8.60 ||

indranīlakapiśīrṣakaṃ tataḥ sa abhitaḥ sphaṭikasālamaikṣata  |
sāvaśeṣajalanīlakoṭibhiḥ śāradairghaṭitamambudairiva  || 8.61 ||

utpatākamaṇitoraṇāṅkitaṃ maṇditaṃ kanakapallavasrajā  |
kiṃ ca kāñcanakapāṭasaṃpuṭaṃ tatra gopuramapaśyadīśvaraḥ  || 8.62 ||

vismayena viṣayīkṛtaḥ puraṃ tena sa aviśadavantivāsavaḥ  |
nirvṛtaiḥ padamivojjhitāvaniḥ sūryamaṇḍalapathena yogavān  || 8.63 ||

tatra vaidrumagavākṣamucchritaṃ hemaharmyamavalokate sma sa  |
meruśṛṅgamiva dhātutāmrayā sandhyayā kṛtapadaṃ kva citkva cit || 8.64 ||

agrataḥ saśca yaśobhaṭo'viśatso'tha kautukahṛtastadaṅgaṇam  |
indranīlamaṇikāntimecakaṃ vyoma sāruṇa ivoṣṇadīdhitiḥ  || 8.65 ||

padmarāgaracitālavālakā vedikāmaṇiviṭaṅkavistṛtā  |
tena tatra dadṛśe kutūhalādantike kanakamādhavīlatā  || 8.66 ||


__________________________________________

strīdarśanam

tattale sthitimupeyuṣā śamāttena kā cidabalā vyalokata  |
nirgatā jhaṭiti hemaveśmataḥ śrīḥ suvarṇakamalodarādiva  || 8.67 ||

aṃśukena śaradindubandhunā tyāgiteva yaśasāvabhāsitā  |
kāntimatyadharanīlavāsasā yāmunena payaseva jāhnavī  || 8.68 ||

bandhujīvakumudachavī mukhe bibhratīva kuruvindakuṇḍale  |
śarvarīva sitapakṣaparvaṇaḥ śītadīdhitipataṅgamaṇḍale  || 8.69 ||

śobhitā kimapi hāralekhayā bhinnakhelalolayorasi  |
tatkṣaṇasphuṭitakundaśuddhayā gandhavāhapadavīva gaṅgayā  || 8.70 ||

ānanena lalitākṣipakṣmaṇā niryadujjvalakapolakāntinā  |
kurvatīva phaṇilokamaṅkitaṃ yāminītilakabindunendunā  || 8.71 ||

puṣpadāma dadhatī saṣaṭpadaṃ dakṣiṇena śaśipāṇḍupāṇinā  |
sākṣataṃ sadadhidūrvayāñcitaṃ hemapātramitareṇa bibhratī  || 8.72 ||
[syntax kulakam]

tannirīkṣaṇasavismayaṃ tataḥ pārthivaṃ janitakautukaḥ śukaḥ  |
ityuvāca maṇipañjare sthito bālacūtaviṭapāvalambini  || 8.73 ||


__________________________________________

śukavākyam

satkriyāṃ racayituṃ tavātithernarmadā bhagavtīyamudyatā  |
ślāghanīyacarito jagattraye kasya nāsi bahumānabhājanam  || 8.74 ||

deva pannagavadhūbhirujjvalaṃ vallakīkalaravaṃ priyaiḥ saha  |
mallikādhavalamatra gīyate keliratnabhavaneṣu te yaśaḥ  || 8.75 ||

durmanā nṛpa, pathāmunā gatā sā vilāsavasatiḥ śaśiprabhā  |
tatsakhījanakathānvayaśruteryuṣmadāgamanamūhitaṃ mayā  || 8.76 ||

durlabho'yamatithirmamāpi tadguhyatāmucitayā saparyayā  |
pārthivo hi navasāhasāṅka ityeṣa sīyakanarendranandanaḥ  || 8.77 ||

peśaloktinipuṇasya pakṣiṇastasya gāmiti niśamya sasmitaḥ  |
tāmatha praṇamati sma nimnagāmindusūtimavanīndurādṛtaḥ  || 8.78 ||
[syntax kulakam]


__________________________________________

narmadākṛtaḥ satkāraḥ

kāritāsanaparigrahe puro bhūpatāvapacitiṃ vidhāya sā  |
āsta mauktikaśilātale tataścetasīva sukaveḥ sarasvatī  || 8.79 ||

sthitvātha kiṃ cittamavantināthamapṛcchadacchannakutūhalā sā  |
nivedyatāṃ mānavadeva kasmādalaṅkṛtā bhūmiriyaṃ tvayeti  || 8.80 ||

tasyai śaśaṃsa nijamā mṛgayāvihārādvṛttāntamantavirasaṃ sa viśuddhavṛttiḥ  |
kāntāsmṛtiprasabhakaṇṭakitāṅgajāta lajjāvanamravadano navasāhasāṅkaḥ  || 8.81 ||

iti parimalāparanāmno mṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite nāgalokāvatāro nāmāṣṭamaḥ sargaḥ samāptaḥ


************************************************


navamaḥ sargaḥ

__________________________________________

narmadāvākyam

atha svareṇāṅgaṇadīrghikāṇāṃ saṃvāhayantī kalahaṃsanādam  |
tamityavantīśvaramā babhāṣe sā mekalakṣmādhararājakanyā  || 9.1 ||

nāsya kṣitīśopakṛtaṃ janasya kiyattayā pannagarājaputryā  |
yasyāḥ kṛte samprati bhūṣiteyaṃ bhūmistvayā bhūśaśalāñchanena  || 9.2 ||

idaṃ nṛpa tvāmavalokya jātaṃ manaḥ pramodena mamāsvatantram  |
no kasya lokatrayasaṃmatānāṃ bhavetsatāṃ saṅgatamutsavāya  || 9.3 ||

adyaiṣa kasyāpi mayā śubhasya tvaddarśanenānumito vipākaḥ  |
ātithyamakṣṇoḥ kathamanyathaivamāyānti ratnāni bhavadvidhāni  || 9.4 ||

so vatsa, jāte janakaḥ kṛtātmā sā puṇyamūrtijananī jagatsu  |
mahīkalāpodvahanādipātraḥ putro yayostvaṃ naralokapālaḥ  || 9.5 ||

rūpeṇa tejasvitayārjavena priyaṃvadatvena tavāmunā ca  |
dilīpaduṣyantabhagīrathādīn tānādirājān jhaṭiti smarāmi  || 9.6 ||

samānabhāvaistribhireva manye samudranemivasudhā dhṛteyam  |
bhujaṅgamendreṇa ca meruṇā ca doṣṇā ca maurvīkiṇaśobhinā te  || 9.7 ||

tvayi sthite samprati jāgarūke jagadvidheyeṣu vidhūtacintaḥ  |
karoti netre bhagavānavaimi sa yoganidrāmukule mukundaḥ  || 9.8 ||

na kiṃ cidikṣvākukulāvatīrṇādrathāṅgapāṇeḥ parihīyate te  |
ajāyatāmbhojadṛśā viyogo vane yathā tasya tathā tavāpi  || 9.9 ||

akṛtrimo'yaṃ guṇavatsu jāne jātyaiva te pārthiva pakṣapātaḥ  |
yatprauḍhalāvaṇyasudhāsravantyā tayā vinā cetasi tāmyasīva  || 9.10 ||

alaṃ viṣādena ghanādhirūḍhā lalāmabhūtā jagato'khilasya  |
tavāṅkamabhyeṣyati sācireṇa śaśiprabhā pārthivakairavasya  || 9.11 ||

ito'dya yāntī purato mayā sā dṛṣṭā bhujaṅgādhipatestanūjā  |
udagrabhogairahibhiḥ parītā lateva tanvī haricandanasya  || 9.12 ||

uddaṇḍahemāmburuhāsu kheladetāsu līlāgṛhadīrghikāsu  |
samutkayantī kalahaṃsayūthamāmañjunā nūpurasiñjitena  || 9.13 ||

vyāpārayantī valitānanenduḥ paścāddṛśau ketakapatradīrghe  |
itastataḥ śūnyatayā skhalantī same'pi mārge dadatī padāni  || 9.14 ||

visrastamālyaḥ ślathabandhanatvādaṃsāvakīrṇāṃ kabarīṃ vahantī  |
kalindakanyāmasṛṇorminīlāṃ nistṛṃśalekhāmiva manmathasya  || 9.15 ||

mukhaṃ niśāghrātamivāravindaṃ viṣadavītaprabhamudvahantī  |
vilumpatī niḥśvasitena kāntimāpāṭalasyādharapallavasya  || 9.16 ||

unmocayantīmalakāgrametya lagnaṃ calatkuṇḍalaratnakoṭau  |
kimapyudañcaddaśanāṃśulekhā sakhīṃ śanaiḥ sasmitamālapantī  || 9.17 ||

sudhāsitaṃ kṣaumamivāstṛtaṃ taṃ nakhāṃśurekhāvalayacchalena  |
kṛtaṃ dadhānopari pāṇipadmamudagrakampasya kucadvayasya  || 9.18 ||

gatāni sadyaḥ ślathatāṃ sakhībhirvihasya sākūtavilokitāni  |
krameṇa kiṃ citpratisārayantī vilajjamānā maṇikaṅkaṇāni  || 9.19 ||

aśokapuṣpagrathitāṃ dadhānā prālambamālāmavalagnamadhyā  |
āropitajyeva jagajjayāya svacāpalekhā makaradhvajena  || 9.20 ||

ārdravraṇāṅkasya kṛpārdracittā kelīmṛgasya svayameva tasya  |
ācumbatī pāṇḍukapolalekhaṃ vataṃsadūrdhvāṅkuramarpayantī  || 9.21 ||

mārgeṣu rūḍhāsu nirūḍhabhāvātdvirephasampātasamākulāpi  |
latāsu puṣpāvacchayacchalena pade pade vatsa vilambamānā  || 9.22 ||
[syntax kulakam]

tvadīyaviśleṣamavāpya bālā sā lakṣyate kiṃ cidanirvṛteva  |
bhavādṛśāmekapade viyogo na kasya, rājendra, mano dunoti  || 9.23 ||

pṛthupratāpaḥ savitā yathaiva yathā kalānāṃ nidhiroṣadhīśaḥ  |
yathā vasantaḥ sumano'nukūlastathāsi bhūmiḥ spṛhaṇīyatāyāḥ  || 9.24 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

iti kṣitīśaśrutiśuktipeyāmudīrya vācaṃ virarāma revā  |
sa ca smitadyotitadantamevamuvāca tāṃ madhyamalokapālaḥ  || 9.25 ||

sthāne yadāhlādayasi prapannaṃ pīyūṣadhārāmadhurairvacobhis |
sudhaikasūtiḥ sa yadākaraste caṇḍīśacūḍābharaṇaṃ śaśāṅkaḥ  || 9.26 ||

ṛjuḥ prakṛtyāsi paraṃ tadamba vīcīśu paryāptamanārjavaṃ te  |
na kevalaṃ sā payasi prasaktirālakṣyate te bata mānase'pi  || 9.27 ||

yā jūṭamadhye ca śaśāṅkamaulerunnidrakundasragivāvabhāti  |
tāmapyatīva trijagatpratīkṣyāṃ triḥsrotasaṃ puṇyatayātiśeṣe  || 9.28 ||

yā sāsya śaktiḥ prasarāmbupaṅke tvayā vṛtā dharmavihāravīthiḥ  |
salīlamuddhūlakulācaleyaṃ mahī mahāsūkaradaṃṣṭra eva  || 9.29 ||

bhavādṛśīnāṃ mahatāṃ nadīnāmadbhirjagatyastamitopasarge  |
sukhaṃ sadaivāsurajitsamudre nidrāti paryaṅkitapannagendraḥ  || 9.30 ||

asantamapyamba mayi prasannā sambhāvanābhāramaho nidhatse  |
astyeva bhakteṣvativatsalatvātbalādguṇāropaṇakautukaṃ te  || 9.31 ||

anena me ko'pi hṛdi praharṣastava prasādātiśayena jātaḥ  |
anārdratāmindumarīcisakhye kiyacciraṃ candramaṇirbibharti  || 9.32 ||

vidhāya tattādṛśamindrajālaṃ sā kena nītā phaṇirājakanyā  |
apaśyato hetumihopapannaṃ kimapyaho vismayate mano me  || 9.33 ||

ajñānamasmin viṣaye kimanyatmamaitadarhasyapanetumamba  |
dināntasammūrcchitamandhakāraṃ niśāmukhasyeva śaśāṅkalekhā  || 9.34 ||


__________________________________________

puno narmadāvākyam

uktveti tūṣṇīmabhavannṛsomaḥ sā somasūtiḥ saridityuvāca  |
atretivṛttaṃ kathayāmyaśeṣaṃ niśamyatāṃ mālavalokapālaḥ  || 9.35 ||


__________________________________________

gṛhadevatāvācaḥ

yadaiva sā tarjitacandrakāntirajāyatendīvarapatranetrā  |
citrasthitānāṃ gṛhadevatānāmiti sphuranti sma tadaiva vācaḥ  || 9.36 ||

ratnākaratvaṃ, bhujagendra jātaṃ kanyā tava śrīḥ śubhalakṣaṇeyam  |
vakṣaḥsthalaṃ madhyamalokabharturvibhūṣayitrī puruṣottamasya  || 9.37 ||
[9.37  readratnākarastvaṃ bhujagendra jātā? orfollow |k?]

bhujaṅgavaṃśārṇavakaumudīyamiyaṃ patākāsya rasātalasya  |
upāgateyaṃ nidhanāgradūtī vajrāṅkuśasyāsurapuṅgavasya  || 9.38 ||
[syntax kulakam]
[note vajrāṅkuśa isthe eastern guardianof the vajradhātumaṇḍala!]

svavīryaparyastapurandareṇa tenāsurendreṇa kadarthitasya  |
tejaścirāducchvasitaṃ tadābhūditi śrute bhogabhṛtāṃ kulasya  || 9.39 ||

taddehakāntistimiraṃ vyanaiṣītyadatra moghīkṛtaratnadīpā  |
pitrā tadasyāḥ kṛtamarthayuktamāhlādanaṃ nāma śaśiprabheti  || 9.40 ||

tataḥ sudhāsūtikarābhirāmairguṇaiḥ parītā sahajanmabhiḥ sā  |
śanaiḥ śanairvṛddhimavāpadatra rasātale bālamṛṇālokeva  || 9.41 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprābhāpituḥ pratijñā

yadarthitābhūdanubaddhamānasaiḥ suraiśca siddhaiśca mahoragaiśca  |
teṣāṃ purastādakṛtavyavasthāmityekadā saṃsadi pannagendraḥ  || 9.42 ||

guptābhihito yattridaśārivīrairvajrāṅkuśākhyasya mahāsurasya  |
asūta līlāgṛhadīrghikeha haimaṃ harernābhirivāravindam  || 9.43 ||

ānīya tadyo duhiturmamāsyāḥ karṇāvataṃsapraṇayīkaroti  |
tasyeyamiṣvāsabhṛtaḥ kalatraṃ pārthasya pāñcālanṛpātmajeva  || 9.44 ||
[syntax kulakam]

tenaivamukte ca tadā pareṣu tamarthamaṅgīkṛtavānna ko'pi  |
vanyadvipādudgatadānarājeḥ kaḥ kumbhamuktāphalamādadīta  || 9.45 ||

tataḥ prabhṛtyadbhutarūparekhā sā bālikābhūdavarā varāpi  |
citte vacastatkuladevatānāṃ kṛtvāpi tasyāḥ saśca nānvaśeta  || 9.46 ||

sampratyavaimi prathitā yadātaḥ svarge ca bhūmau ca bhuvastale ca  |
nīto'si netrātithitāṃ tvamasyāḥ puṇyena janmāntarasambhṛtena  || 9.47 ||

ānetukāmena bhavantamatra nijaṃ vacastannayatā pratiṣṭhām  |
āptaprayatnena tathā sa manye phaṇīśvareṇopakṛtaḥ prapañcaḥ  || 9.48 ||

ekaḥ kṣitau sāhasikastvameva nānyo'sti rājannavasāhasāṅkaḥ  |
nisargadurgāmapi bhūmimetāṃ svodyānavīthīmiva yaḥ praviṣṭaḥ  || 9.49 ||

tadasya kāryasya puraskṛtasya yatasva sīmāntavilokanāya  |
vigāhamāno'mbaramardhamārgaṃ nivartate jātu kimuṣṇarāśmiḥ  || 9.50 ||

ito'sti gavyūtiśatārdhamātraṃ gatvā purī ratnavatīti nāmnā  |
vinirmitā śilpikalāmayena mayena yā nākajigīṣayena  || 9.51 ||
[note allusion to aṇahilapura, capitalof the solaṅkis?]

tasyāsurendrasya narādhipendra jagaddruhaḥ sā kila rājadhānī  |
samedhitasyābjabhuvā vareṇa raṇeṣvavadhyo marutāṃ bhaveti  || 9.52 ||

so mauliratnāni mahoragāṇāmutkhyāya cotkhāya ca kautukena  |
karoti nirvāsitanāyakeṣu nijāṅganāhāralatāntareṣu  || 9.53 ||

so bāṣpaparyākulalocanāni niḥśvāsabhinnādharapallavāni  |
karoti vaktrāṇyamarāṅganānāmutsannalīlāsmitacandrikāni  || 9.54 ||

kṛtāṅgadaḥ kambalakāliyābhyāṃ yajñopavītīkṛtaśaṅkhacūḍaḥ  |
sastakṣakāpāditakaṇṭhabhūṣo bibharti līlāmaśivaḥ śivasya  || 9.55 ||

āstāṃ kimanyaiḥ phaṇibhiḥ saścintyastasyāpi śeṣasya ca vāsukeśca  |
rāhuryathā viśvabhayaikahetustārādhipasyāhimadīdhiteśca  || 9.56 ||

khagendrabhaṅgena tathā tathā ca na sarpayajñe janamejayasya  |
nidhānamātaṅkaparamparāṇāṃ jāto yathā samprati nāgalokaḥ  || 9.57 ||

harestvamaṃśo'tra kṛtāvatārastasyāsurendrasya nibarhaṇāya  |
avaimi lokatrayakaṇṭakasya laṅkādhipasyeva sa maithilīśaḥ  || 9.58 ||

śrīkaṇṭhavaikuṇṭhapurandarādyairupekṣitaṃ yattridaśairaśaktyā  |
kṛtasya tasyāsya bharaṃ visoḍhuṃ sambhāvyase me nṛpate tvameva  || 9.59 ||

kimanyaduttiṣṭha gṛhāṇa yātrāṃ vajrāṅkuśaṃ pratyamitapratāpa  |
taccāvataṃsīkuru hemapadmamānīya, bhūśakra, śaśiprabhāyāḥ  || 9.60 ||

sā te samāptādbhutasāhasasya vatsāṅkamabhyetu phaṇīndrakanyā [[vatsāṅka pun?]]  |
sītā yathā daśaratheḥ salīlamāropitatryambakakārmukasya  || 9.61 ||
[9.61  readasamāpta॰ ?]

agādhapātālatalodgatāni vinidrakundacchadasundarāni  |
lokadvaye samprati te yaśāṃsi ākalpapallavīphalavaccarantu  || 9.62 ||

prasādamāptena cirādvilīne tasmin surārātighanoparodhe  |
mukhendunā pannagasundarīṇāṃ punaḥ samāgacchatu patralekhā  || 9.63 ||

āsītpurastāttripurāvabhaṅge yanmaṇḍalaṃ bālamṛgāṅkamauleḥ  |
mahāsurairbhāvini sāmparāye tavāstu tatsaṃyugajāmadagnya  || 9.64 ||

panthāḥ śivo'yaṃ purato'tra gantā vaṅkurmunirlocanagocaraṃ te  |
upācarestaṃ ca tathāvidhānāṃ bhaktiṃ hi gāṃ kāmaduhāmuśanti  || 9.65 ||

ityudīrya maṇikāntikandalīḥ kalpitaṃ tridaśacāpamasya sā  |
ā mumoca nijakaṇkaṇaṃ bhuje jyākiṇāṅkakaṭhiṇīkṛtatvaci  || 9.66 ||


__________________________________________

narmadoktisvīkāraḥ

avadadatha saḥ sāhasonmukhastāmiha hi vayaṃ vacasi sthitāstaveti  |
taḍidiva na cirādudīritāśīḥ saridapi sāsya purastirobabhūva  || 9.67 ||

iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye narmadāsaṃvādo nāma navamaḥ sargaḥ

************************************************


daśamaḥ sargaḥ


nāyakasya ramāṅgadaṃ prati praśnaḥ

atha mekalācalasutātirohitau atimātravismayarasārdramānasaḥ  |
daśanacchavicchuritapāṭalādharaḥ sa ramāṅgadaṃ nṛpatirityavocat || 10.1 ||

adhirohati svayamacintitāpyaho śubhasampadaṅkamaparāṅmukhe vidhau  |
savapurvilocanapathaṃ yadāvayoramṛtāṃśusūtiriyamāpadāpagā  || 10.2 ||

idamindrajālamiti me samutthitāṃ matimetadarpitamudārayā tayā  |
vikasanmarīciracitendrakārmukaṃ karavarti ratnavalayaṃ vilumpati  || 10.3 ||

ayi mekalādritanayāpuraḥkṛte kriyate kimatra vada kṛtyavastuni  |
tava yatsadā nayarahasyavedino na dhiyaścarantyanayapāṃsule pathi  || 10.4 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavākyam

iti pārthivena kathite dadhanmanākpulakena cumbitakapolamānanam  |
idamāttanītipathamādade vacaḥ smitapuṣpitādharadalo ramāṅgadaḥ  || 10.5 ||

iha kiṃ pratisphurati me tavāgrato nayaśāstranīranidhipāradṛśvanaḥ  |
avalīḍhaviśvatamasaḥ puro raverna hi jātu dipakaśikhā prakāśate  || 10.6 ||

viṣaye'tra maunamucitaṃ hi mādṛśāmavaśastathāpi kathayāmi kiṃ cana  |
kṣitipālamaulimaṇiveṇikātithestava kena śāsanamidaṃ vilaṅghyate  || 10.7 ||

prathamaṃ hi maṇḍalamakhaṇḍaśāktibhirvijigīṣubhiḥ svamabhitaḥ prasādhyate  |
paramaṇḍale tadanu nītipāragairavanīpurandara, karaḥ prasāryate  || 10.8 ||

tadapāsyamevamavitarkitotthite paripanthināmiha vidheyavastuni  |
yadi nābhaviṣyadabhimānaśālinastava rājyamuddhṛtasamastakaṇṭakam  || 10.9 ||

śrutaśaktisaṅkalitamantraniścayairupalabdhaṣaḍguṇavivekavartmabhiḥ  |
tadupāyatattvamadhigamya dhūḥ kṣitestava mantribhirnṛpa durudvahohyate  || 10.10 ||

atisāndrakāñcanamarīcipiṅgalaṃ digupāhitapracurapatraśobhinaḥ  |
tava kośamutsṛjati na kṣaṇaṃ ramā paramāravaṃśasarasīsaroruha  || 10.11 ||

priyakīrtayo jayapavitritāśayāstarasā tṛṇīkṛtajagattrayā yudhi  |
jagatīviśeṣaka tavānujīvino nivasantyavantiviṣaye sahasraśaḥ  || 10.12 ||

pṛthivībhṛtaḥ prathitavikrameṇa ye gamitāstvayā vaśamupāyasampadā  |
nayavartmagāḥ kṣitipate tavānyathā na bhavanti bhāvisamarābhiśaṅkayā  || 10.13 ||

apakartumatra samaye tavāttabhīrmanasāpi hūṇanṛpatirna vāñchati  |
ibhakumbhabhittidalanodyame harerna kapiḥ kadā cana saṭāṃ vikaṛṣati  || 10.14 ||

asikāntijālajaṭilāgrabāhunā raṇasīmni nātha nihateṣu bhartṛṣu  |
bhavatātra vāgaḍavadhūjanaḥ kṛto ratisandhivigrahakathāparāṅmukhaḥ  || 10.15 ||

adhunāpi deva muralāṅganājanaisvijayapraśastiriva likhyate tava  |
galadañjanāśrupṛṣatāvalicchalāllasadindupāṇḍuṣu kapolabhittiṣu  || 10.16 ||

rabhasādapāsya maṇikaṅkaṇāvalīḥ kanakāravindakatakeṣu te'sinā  |
na kimarpitāni nṛpa lāṭayoṣitā sphaṭikākṣasūtravalayāni pāṇiṣu  || 10.17 ||

nayanāmbubhiḥ snapitadhūsarādharāḥ pratibaddharūkṣamalinaikaveṇayaḥ  |
nihitā na kiṃ mahati śokasāgare jagatīndra kosalapateḥ purandhrayaḥ  || 10.18 ||

uditena vairitimiradruhābhitastava nātha vikramamayūkhamālinā  |
gamitāḥ prabhāvalayaśūnyatāṃ jhaṭityaparāntapārthivavadhūmukhendavaḥ  || 10.19 ||

ativelamuttaradigantavartinā samaraśramābhyuditagharmabindunā  |
śaradindunirmalamapāyi bhūbhṛtāmasipatrapātrapatitaṃ tvayā yaśaḥ  || 10.20 ||

nijarandhragopanapaṭīyasābhitaḥ pararandhradṛṣṭipaṭucāracakṣuṣā  |
nayabhinnasāhasabhuvā bhuvastale bhavatā samaṃ kathaya ko viruddhyate  || 10.21 ||

naradeva daivamadhikṛtya yā vipatnipatatyavantiviṣaye kathaṃ cana  |
śikhimuktamantrahaviṣā vihanyate tava sā vasiṣṭhamahasā purodhasā  || 10.22 ||

nṛpa vāsarāṇi nirupaplavāḥ prajāḥ sukhamātmakarmaṇi ratā nayanti yat |
vijayaṃ jayaikasuhṛdo'sya sarvadā nanu kārmukasya tava tadvijṛmbhitam  || 10.23 ||

iti kiṃ cideva na tava svamaṇḍale nṛpa cintyamastyuditaśaktisampadaḥ  |
adhunā tu nītinihitena cetasā phaṇilokakṛtyamidameva cintyatām  || 10.24 ||

prabhuśāktirudyamaparatvamarpita trijagaccamatkṛtirahaṅkṛtiśca sā  |
asurasya tasya kathitā narendra te nagarī ca mekalanagendrakanyayā  || 10.25 ||

abhigamya eva sastavādhunā ripurmarutāmudāranijakāryasiddhaye  |
śrutilagnagandhagajabṛṃhitaḥ kṣaṇaṃ nṛpa kesarī kathaya kiṃ vilambate  || 10.26 ||

navasāhasāṅka, na tavāsurādahaṃ kalayāmi samprati kimapyatādṛśam  |
vidhutiḥ kadā cana vibho na bhūbhṛtaḥ kalaviṅkapakṣapavanena śāṅkyate  || 10.27 ||

bhavatā yadoccalita eṣa dakṣiṇaścaraṇastadaiva suravairiyoṣitām  |
vigalanti deva nayanodabindavaḥ śaradindupāṇḍuni kapolamaṇḍale  || 10.28 ||

vijayaikasadmani guṇaḥ śarāsane tava yāvadatra na nṛpādhirohati  |
tuhinacchaṭādhavalacāmarasmitā vilasanti tāvadasurālaye śriyaḥ  || 10.29 ||

kanakāravindamaravindalocana praṇayena naiva saḥ samarpayiṣyati  |
suranirjayārjitamadāvṛte'ntaraṃ labhate na sāma kila tādṛśaṃ hṛdi  || 10.30 ||

bhavataḥ kuto'pi nṛpa yāvadāgamaṃ na sa vetti tāvadabhiyoktumarhasi  |
sahasānyathā rahasi mantribodhitaḥ paritaḥ svadurghaṭane yatiṣyate  || 10.31 ||

kṛtanūtanārgalakapāṭasaṃpuṭāṃ subhaṭairudāyudhakarairadhiṣṭhitām  |
paritaḥ sukhātaparikhāṃ punaḥ purīṃ racitaikadurgamapathāṃ vidhāsyati  || 10.32 ||

valite'pi kiṃ cana dhanuḥparigrahe bhuvanatrayaprathitasāhase tvayi  |
api jāyate dhṛtiviparyayo harerasureṣu kaiva gaṇanā tapasviṣu  || 10.33 ||

niyataṃ, narendra, vidatphaṇāmaṇi sphuradaṃśusūtritanavātapaṃ nabhaḥ  |
suravairivīryadṛḍhamatsaraṃ puraḥ phaṇisainyamājibhuvi te bhaviṣyati  || 10.34 ||

adhunaiva te'tra nijatāṃ vrajanti vā subhaṭāḥ svayaṃ vidhivaśena ke cana  |
kapayaḥ purā raghupateryathā vane hanumatpataṅgatanayāṅgadādayaḥ  || 10.35 ||

yadudīritaśca purukutsakāntayā saritāsi vaṅkumunidarśanaṃ prati  |
pratibhāti kiṃ cana mamaiva tatra te kimu nirmukheṅgitavidastadiṅgitam  || 10.36 ||

athavaika eva vibhurasyarervadhe nanu dhāma tatsphurati śārṅgiṇastvayi  |
uditakrudhastripuradāhaḍambare śaratāmavāpa kila yatpinākinaḥ  || 10.37 ||

avalokayāmi śakunaṃ yathā tathā tadavaimi pakṣmaladṛśaḥ sabhāntare  |
na cirādupoḍḥpulakena pāṇinā kanakāravindamavataṃsayiṣyasi  || 10.38 ||

tvamihaiva nātha maṇidhāmni tiṣṭha vā na hi nāma tādṛśamidaṃ prayojanam  |
asuraṃ nihatya sahasaiva tatkṣaṇādahamānayāmi tapanīyapaṅkajam  || 10.39 ||

vijayī yadasmi smareṣu jitvarāḥ prabhavanti tatra tava pādapāṃsavaḥ  |
aruṇo yadandhatamasaṃ niṣedhati sphuritaṃ narādhipa tadarkatejasām  || 10.40 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

masṛṇoktipallavitanītivikrama kramamityudīrya virate ramāṅgade  |
saḥ sarasvatīmukhararatnanūpura dhvanipeśalaṃ nṛpatirādade vacaḥ  || 10.41 ||

tvadṛte mukhātsukhanirastasaṃśaya prasareyaṃ bhāratī ullasati kasya bhāratī  |
śaśalakṣmaṇaḥ paramakharvaśarvarī timirachiducchalati kāntikandali  || 10.42 ||

tava vedmi pauruṣamahaṃ tvayā vinā na vapuḥsthitiṃ kva cana kartumutsahe  |
dhanuṣīva dīrghaguṇasaṅgate yatastvayi me dṛḍhapraṇayavāsitaṃ manaḥ  || 10.43 ||

gamane tadehi sahitau yatāvahe jhaṭiti triviṣṭaparipoḥ purīṃ prati  |
apade yadudyamakathāvirodhinī na hi siddhaye bhavati dīrghasūtratā  || 10.44 ||


__________________________________________
śukavākyam

iti pārśvavartinamudīrya maunavānabhavatsa mālavakuraṅgalāñchanaḥ  |
tvarayāvatīrya saśca ratnapañjarātpurataḥ śuko'sya puna ityabhāṣata  || 10.45 ||

śṛṇu śaṅkhacūḍaśucivaṃśabhūrahaṃ nṛpa, ratnacūḍa iti nāgarakaḥ  |
udapādi kaṇvamuniśiṣyaśāpataḥ śukatā mameyamanimīlitasmṛtiḥ  || 10.46 ||

praṇayoktibhirmuniratha prasedivāniti me saḥ śāpatimirāvadhiṃ vyadhāt |
vaśināṃ ruṣo matiṣu nāsate ciraṃ jalavipuṣaśca, nṛpa, sasyasūciṣu  || 10.47 ||

tvamaphalgu neṣyasi śaśiprabhāntikaṃ navasāhasāṅkanṛpateryadā vacaḥ  |
niyataṃ bhaviṣyati tadā kumāra te śukarūparūpaparivartanotsavaḥ  || 10.48 ||

tadanaṅgaṣaṣṭhaśaraṃ saṃdiśa svayaṃ śanakaiḥ kimapyuragabālikāṃ prati  |
hṛdi yannidhāya sahasaiva yāmyahaṃ phaṇināmanamramaṇitoraṇāṃ purīm  || 10.49 ||
[var10.49  {॰śaraṃ|lem |em̭ ॰śara |ed}]

ayi maunametadavanīndra, mucyatāṃ drutamucyatāṃ ca kimiyaṃ mayi trapā  |
pṛthagasmi deva na hi te paricchadātucitaṃ na tanmayi rahasyagopanam  || 10.50 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

iti valgu jalpati śuke'tha vismayādapi vismayaṃ paramavāpa pārthivaḥ  |
avadacca pañjaramivāsya kalpayan daśanāṃśubhiḥ sphaṭikasūcikomalaiḥ  || 10.51 ||

vipadaṃ vilokya tava duḥsahāmimāmayi ratnacūḍa mama dūyate manaḥ  |
patitaṃ kukūladahane na kasya vā mṛdumālatīmukulamālyamādhaye  || 10.52 ||


__________________________________________

nāyikāṃ prati saṃdeśaḥ

ghaṭitaṃ vidheridamajaryamāvayorna ramāṅgadānmama sakhe'tiricyase  |
idamārya tattvayi vimuktayantraṇo nanu saṃdiśāmi hariṇīdṛśāṃ prati  || 10.53 ||

virate'pi meghatimire natāṅgi me na gatāsi locanapathaṃ yadā tadā  |
phaṇilokabhūmimatidurgamāmimāmaviśaṃ tava anupadameva sudnari  || 10.54 ||

nagarīṃ tvadāttahṛdayo'pi bhogināmahamāgato na mṛgadīrghalocane  |
śrutayendusūtisaritānyato hṛtaḥ sahasaiva hemaśatapatravārtayā  || 10.55 ||

atipāṭalādharamavāñcitaṃ hriyā smitakāntimatstimitaratnakuṇḍalam  |
tadapāṅgasaṅkalitalocanotpalaṃ phaṇilokakaumudi mukhaṃ smarāmi te  || 10.56 ||

dvitaye dvayena sahasojjhitastadā śaśisūtisindhupulinodare śaraḥ  |
jagadekavibhramabhvā bhuvastale sutanu tvayā mayi ca puṣpadhanvanā  || 10.57 ||

dhṛtamūrmihastanivahena revayā nanu phenakānti karabhoru me patat |
viṣaye dṛśorupadaśaṃ manaḥśilā likhitaikahaṃsamithunaṃ tavāṃśukam  || 10.58 ||

maṇikāntiluptatimire rasātale bhavatīmihānusaratā tanūdari  |
avalokitānyatha mayā padāni te sahasā suvarṇasikatāṅkite pathi  || 10.59 ||

sarale jaṭhityuditakārśyadorlatā gailtāni ratnavalayāni te mayā  |
kathamapyudaśrupṛṣataṃ pade pade cakitena candramukhi vīkṣitāni ca  || 10.60 ||

manasā kimālikhati kiṃ samācaratyadhunā kiminduvadanā ca vakti sā  |
iti me'padiśya bhavatīṃ pravṛttayā hṛdayaṃ saśalyamiva hanta cintayā  || 10.61 ||

paritāpavatyaviralocchalatprabhā tuhinacchaṭābhirasitābjalocane  |
śaradindudīdhitikalāpasundarastava hāra eṣa hṛdi siñcatīva mām  || 10.62 ||

kathaya priye nihitasāndracandana dravaśītalojjvalakarā kucadvaye  |
mama hārayaṣṭirapi sā sakhīva kiṃ madanābhitāpamapaṭūkaroti te  || 10.63 ||

kṣaṇamapyaho patasi me śucismite na samutsukasya tava vismṛteḥ pathi  |
jhaṭiti praviśya hṛdaye mamātra kiṃ likhitāsi padmamukhi puṣpaketunā  || 10.64 ||

samudvahantī sravadañjanāśru ghorotkaraśyāmitakaṅkaṇena  |
karāravindena mukhendubimbamāpāṇḍurakṣāmakapolabhitti  || 10.65 ||

nave nave paṅkajinīpalāśa mṛṇālahārādisanāthapārśve  |
pravālalīlāstaraṇe niṣaṇṇā siṃhāsane manmathapārthivasya  || 10.66 ||

bālapravālāṅkurapāṭalasya lāvaṇyaratnākarakaustubhasya  |
udūṣmaṇā niḥśvasitena kāntiṃ kadarthayantī daśanacchadasya  || 10.67 ||

analpasaṅkalpavikalpajāla viloḍanairna svamapi smarantī  |
sasādhvasenāvirataṃ mayā tvamutprekṣyase pannagarājaputri  || 10.68 ||
[syntax kalāpakas]

brūmaḥ kiyannaya kathaṃ cana kālamalpamatrābjapatranayane nayane nimīlya  |
hemāmbujaṃ taruṇi tattarasāpahṛtya devadviṣo'yamahamāgata ityavehi  || 10.69 ||

bhadraitadvraja ratnacūḍanibiḍapremārdramasmadvacastasyāstatra kuraṅgaśāvakadṛśaḥ karṇāvataṃsīkuru  |
śāpānte bata vismariṣyasi bhrātastadekaṃ kimapyādāya svayameva tatprativacaḥ pārśvaṃ mamābhyeṣyasi  || 10.70 ||

iti nṛpateḥ svānte kṛtvā manomṛgavāgurāṃ giramudakamannistriṃśābhe nabhasyaśanaiḥ śukaḥ  |
ciravinihitāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ tasmānnivartya tathotsuko jhaṭiti gamane devo'pyasītsaḥ sāhasalāñchanaḥ  || 10.71 ||

iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye ratnacūḍasaṃpreṣaṇo nāma daśamaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


ekādaśaḥ sargaḥ


śatrujayārthaṃ prasthānam

atha bibhratsarāgeṇa hṛdayena kṛśodarīm  |
saḥ pratasthe mahīnāthaḥ kareṇa ca dhanurlatām  || 11.1 ||

indranīlapratolītaḥ sa nirgacchan patiḥ śriyaḥ  |
tatkāntiśyāmatāṃ gatvā kṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇa ivābabhau  || 11.2 ||

āsannapadmasarasā kusumānamraśākhinā  |
so narmadopadiṣṭena gantuṃ pravavṛte pathā  || 11.3 ||

yāntamekāntaśiśirāḥ samīrāstaṃ siṣevire  |
elālavaṅgakaṅkola jātīphalasugandhayaḥ  || 11.4 ||

cakrastasyānilasparśa kvaṇatkāñcanapallavāḥ  |
mauktikastabakasmerā vismayaṃ ratnavīrudhaḥ  || 11.5 ||

amandamārutākṣepa muktamuktāphalacchalāt |
caladvaṃśalatā mūrdhni tasya lājānivākiran  || 11.6 ||

agalan kusumavyājāttasminnabhyarṇagāmini  |
pātālakalpavṛkṣāṇāmānandāśrulavā iva  || 11.7 ||

aravindamukhakroḍa krīḍanmukharaṣaṭpadā  |
anāmayamivāpṛcchattamabhyāgatamabjinī  || 11.8 ||

śaśiprabhākṣivistāra saṃvādinyaḥ pade pade  |
taṃ kimapyārdratāṃ ninyuraraṇyahariṇīdṛśaḥ  || 11.9 ||

upayuktāmṛtaspardhi nārikelaphalodakān  |
āsvāditalavāṅgailā pūganāgalatādalān  || 11.10 ||

śayyīkṛtātanusvarṇa kadalībālapallavān  |
viṣṭarīkṛtavistīrṇa candrakāntaśilātalān  || 11.11 ||

tatkālocitakartavya vyāvṛttaikaramāṅgadān  |
āvṛttivihitapreyaḥ phaṇirājasutākathān  || 11.12 ||

hṛdayanyastakarpūra mṛṇālanalinīdalān  |
puṃḥkokilakulollāpa janitasmarasañjvarā  || 11.13 n ||

jāmbūnadalatāgulma vihitāśrayasauhṛdān  |
gacchan sastatra kati cinnivāsān vyadhitādhvani  || 11.14 ||
[syntax kulakam]


__________________________________________

vaṅkumunyāśramaprāptiḥ


saṃprāpa pṛthivīpālaḥ kālena kiyatāpyatha  |
saḥ kūlopāntavicaran yaṅku vaṅkutapovanam  || 11.15 ||


__________________________________________

āśramavarṇanam


hṛtaṃ kutūhalenālaṃ tadālokanajanmanā  |
patiṃ madhyamalokasya taṃ jagāda ramāṅgadaḥ  || 11.16 ||

sīmā satītiśabdasya sakuśāṅkālpapallavā  |
maithilīva śriyaṃ dhatte kāmapyāśramabhūriyam  || 11.17 ||

ito vānti havirdhūma latālasyapradā ime  |
marutaḥ pāvanāḥ pakva puroḍāśasugandhayaḥ  || 11.18 ||

ito hiraṇmayī bhūmistaravo hemavalkalāḥ  |
unnidrahemapadmāni payāṃsīva pade pade  || 11.19 ||

kākapakṣāṅkamūrdhānaḥ paśyaite guruśikṣayā  |
baṭavaḥ khaṇḍayantyatra samidhaśca padāni ca  || 11.20 ||

anayā sāma gāyantyā svarasaṃśayavānayam  |
itaḥ karoti kalahaṃ śukaḥ sārikayā samam  || 11.21 ||

idamatrādbhutaṃ paśya madaklinnaṃ gajasya yat |
gaṇḍalekhāṃ nakhāgreṇa śanaiḥ kaṇḍūyate hariḥ  || 11.22 ||

prabhāmaṇḍalaparyasta tamasaḥ śataśaḥ pathi  |
tavāpatanti pātāla ravayo'mī maharṣayaḥ  || 11.23 ||

eṣāṃ dvitayametābhiḥ kapilābhiralaṅkṛtam  |
uṭajaprāṅgaṇaṃ gobhirjaṭābhirabhitaḥ śiraḥ  || 11.24 ||

ito gātraparāvṛtti bhagnāsthi puruṣetarān  |
muneḥ śayyākuśānatti bālaḥ kastūrikāmṛgaḥ  || 11.25 ||

ito'pyayamṛṣiḥ paśya japāpāṭalayānayā  |
gavānugamyate sāyaṃ saṃdhyeva divākaraḥ  || 11.26 ||

sahasaivātithiḥ prāptaḥ ko'pyayaṃ bhavatāmiti  |
eṣa pratyuṭajaṃ vakti sasaṃbhramamayaṃ śukaḥ  || 11.27 ||

ataḥ saṃprati vīkṣante kautukottānitekṣaṇāḥ  |
tvāmindumiva paryāpta maṇḍalaṃ munikanyakāḥ  || 11.28 ||

bhūdattasmarasāmrājyaṃ mukhaśrītarjitendu ca  |
āsāmindīvarākṣīṇāmalaṅkāro navaṃ vayaḥ  || 11.29 ||

muktāstraḥ strīṣu kandarpo devātrānuśayādiva  |
śaṅke saṃtyajya kodaṇḍamāttadaṇḍastapasyati  || 11.30 ||


__________________________________________

vaṅkumunidarśanam


tasminnityuktavatyeva tathā savidhavartmani  |
tataḥ pṛthvīśaśāṅkena vaṅkumuniradṛśyata  || 11.31 ||

aṃsāvalambinīrbibhratsandhyābhrakapiśā jaṭāḥ  |
prasṛtā iva nirgatya paramajyotiṣaḥ śikhāḥ  || 11.32 ||

dadhadyajñopavītena sīmantitamuraḥsthalam  |
jāhnavīnirjhareṇeva nabhaḥ prāleyapāṇḍunā  || 11.33 ||

śuddhaikaguṇasaṃpṛktāmakṣamālāṃ dadhatkare  |
mūrtāṃ tīvratapaḥsiddhimātmanaḥ phalitāmiva  || 11.34 ||

yogakṣemopapattyarthamupaviṣṭaḥ kuśāsane  |
napteva maithilībharturatithirnāma pārthivaḥ  || 11.35 ||

priyasomaḥ sadāyuktaḥ priyayā cānasūyayā  |
pātramatririvogrāṇāṃ tapasāṃ tejasāmiva  || 11.36 ||

so dṛṣṭipathamāyāti yayātipratime nṛpe  |
tutoṣa kasya vā na syādākṛtistasya sā mude  || 11.37 ||

tataḥ kṛtapraṇāmasya tasya praṇatabhūbhṛtaḥ  |
vidadhe sa viśāmpatyurātithyamattithipriyaḥ  || 11.38 ||

athādūre sukhāsīnaḥ sukhāsīne mahībhṛti  |
iti sūnṛtayā vācā sa vaktumupacakrame  || 11.39 ||

adya naḥ puṇyabījena mukto yatsatyamaṅkuraḥ  |
lalāma lokatritaye yena tvamavalokitaḥ  || 11.40 ||

tava śaṃsati saubhāgyamabhijāteyamākṛtiḥ  |
indoḥ sudhānidhānatvaṃ jyotsnayā yatpratīyate  || 11.41 ||

yathā pradeśamāyātairvyaktiṃ vajrāṅkuśādibhiḥ  |
cakravartītyanukto'pi cihnaistvamanumīyase  || 11.42 ||

tvadarśanotsavenaiva kṛtārthaṃ cakṣuradya naḥ  |
vimuñcati śaraccandre cirarūḍhamapi spṛhām  || 11.43 ||

hetudvitayamevātra paramānandasampadaḥ  |
parabrahmopalabdhirvā saṅgataṃ vā bhavādṛśām  || 11.44 ||

akṛtvā bhavataḥ praśnaṃ na sthātumahamutsahe  |
dhīratāṃ mama bhindanti yatkautukarasormayaḥ  || 11.45 ||

tvayā mahībhṛtāmatra vaṃśaḥ keṣāmalaṅkṛtaḥ?  |
śrotrapīyūṣagaṇḍūṣaḥ kāni nāmākṣarāṇi te ?  || 11.46 ||

anena guṇinā sārdhaṃ dhanuṣānucareṇa ca  |
kena kāryātibhāreṇa tvametāmāgato bhuvam ?  || 11.47 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavākyam

ityuktvā virate tasmin rājñā sasmitamīkṣitaḥ  |
sthitvā kṣaṇamuvācedamiṅgitajño ramāṅgadaḥ  || 11.48 ||


__________________________________________

arbudācalavarṇanam

brahmāṇḍamaṇḍapastambhaḥ śrīmānastyabudo giriḥ  |
upoḍhahaṃsikā yasya saritaḥ sālabhañjikā  || 11.49 ||

yaḥ sūryāṃśuśalākasya viśvasyopari tiṣṭhataḥ  |
vyomanīlātapatrasya daṇḍatvamadhirohat || 11.50 ||

ādātumavataṃsāya svarṇadīhemapuṣkaram  |
yaḥ sendranīlakaṭako bhuvo bhuja ivoddhṛtaḥ  || 11.51 ||

śikharāsannanakṣatro lakṣyate yaḥ pratikṣapam  |
saśīkara ivodasto hastaḥ pātāladantinā  || 11.52 ||

yasya śṛṅgendranīlāṃśu śyāmamādityamaṇḍalam  |
kṣaṇaṃ puṭakinīpatra chatrākṛti vilokyate  || 11.53 ||

nīlakaṇṭhapriyā kāmaṃ kṛtapañcānanasthitiḥ  |
yasyāgrabhūmirgaurīva guhāpītapayodharā  || 11.54 ||

adhaḥsaṃnaddhamedheṣu sthitā yasyāgrasānuṣu  |
prāvṛḍvilāsālāsyānāmanabhijñāḥ kalāpinaḥ  || 11.55 ||

induḥ kaṭakamāṇikyaṃ yasya tuṅgasya bhūbhṛtaḥ  |
bhuvo yasya ca kāntāyā mekhalāmaṇiraṃśumān  || 11.56 ||

kva citkva citpatantyā yaḥ kṛṣṇasāraḥ śaśitviṣā  |
kaṇḍūyata ivāsannaṃ śṛṅgeṇa hi mṛgīṃ niśi  || 11.57 ||

pāṇḍuḥ śaraddhanairūrdhvamadhastālīvanāsitaḥ  |
yaḥ kailāsa ivāśliṣṭaḥ paulastyabhujasampradā  || 11.58 ||

harayaḥ śerate yasya mattebhavadhaniḥsahāḥ  |
guhāsu nakhanirmukta muktādanturabhūmiṣu  || 11.59 ||

alakacyutamandāra makarandasugandhibhiḥ  |
amartyamithunakrīḍā nikuñjairyasya sūcyate  || 11.60 ||

udañcadindracāpāni nānāratnāṃśupallavaiḥ  |
sānūni yasya sevante dvaye citraśīnaḥ  || 11.61 ||

patyā saha vanānteṣu viharantyādrikanyayā  |
nīyante śoṇatāṃ yasya śilāḥ sālaktakaiḥ padaiḥ  || 11.62 ||

pratibhānti puraste'pi yasya valmīkavāmanāḥ  |
śailāḥ suvelakailāsa mahendramalayādayaḥ  || 11.63 ||


__________________________________________

vasiṣṭhāśramavarṇanam

atisvādhīnanīvāra phalamūlasamitkuśam  |
munistapovanaṃ cakre tatrekṣvākupurohitaḥ  || 11.64 ||

hṛtā tasyaikadā dhenuḥ kāmasargādhisūnunā  |
kārtavīryārjuneneva jamadagneranīyata  || 11.65 ||
[var11.65  {hṛtā|lem |eḓ hṛtvā |buh}̭ 11.65 {sarga+|buh॰|leṃ sūrga+|eḓ }]

sthūlāśrudhārāsaṃtāna snapitastanavalkalā  |
amarṣapāvakasyābhūdbhartuḥ samidarundhatī  || 11.66 ||

athātharvavidāmādyaḥ samantrāmāhutiṃ dadau  |
vikasadvikaṭajvālā jaṭile jātavedasi  || 11.67 ||
[var11.67 {vikaṭlem |eḓ vikala+|buh}]

tataḥ kṣaṇatsakodaṇḍaḥ kirīṭī kāñcanāṅgadaḥ  |
ujjagāmāgnitaḥ ko'pi sahemakavacaḥ pumān  || 11.68 ||

dūraṃ saṃtamaseneva viśvāmitreṇa sā hṛtā  |
tenāninye munerdhenurdinaśrīriva bhānunā  || 11.69 ||

tatastāpasakanyābhirānandāśrulavāṅkitaḥ  |
kapolaḥ pāṇiparyaṅkātsāśrulekhādapāsyata  || 11.70 ||
[var11.70 {sāśrulekhāt |lem |eḓ sādhupūjyāt |buh}]


__________________________________________

paramāravaṃśavarṇanam

paramāra iti prapātsa munernāma cārthavat |
mīlitānyanṛpacchatramātapatraṃ ca bhūtale  || 11.71 ||
[var11.71 {ātapatraṃ|lem |eḓ adhipatyam |buh}]

pravartitātivistīrṇa saptatantuparamparaḥ  |
purāṇakūrmaśeṣaṃ yaścakārāmbhonidheḥ payaḥ  || 11.72 ||

sthāpitairmaṇipīṭheṣu muktāprālambamālibhiḥ  |
bhūriyaṃ yajvanā yena hemayūpairapūryata  || 11.73 ||

praśāntacintāsantāne cireṇa namucidviṣi  |
amocyatāstadaityena yenerṣyākalahaṃ śacī  || 11.74 ||

vaṃśaḥ pravaṛte tasmādādirājānmanoriva  |
nītaḥ suvṛttairgurutāṃ nṛpairmuktāphalairiva  || 11.75 ||

tasmin pṛthupratāpo'pi nirvāpitamahītalaḥ  |
upendra iti sañjajñe rājā sūryendusaṃnibhaḥ  || 11.76 ||

sadāgatipravṛttena sītocchvasitahetunā  |
hanumateva yaśasā yasyālaṅghyata sāgaraḥ  || 11.77 ||

śaṅkitendreṇa dadhatā pūtāmavabhṛtaistanum  |
akāri yajvanā yena hemayūpāṅkitā mahī  || 11.78 ||

atyacchadaśanodgacchataṃśulekhātaraṅgibhiḥ  |
dīrghairyasyārinārīṇāṃ niḥśvāsaiścāmārayitam  || 11.79 ||

tasmin gate narendreṣu tadanyeṣu gateṣu ca  |
tatra vākpatirājākhyaḥ pārthivendurajāyata  || 11.80 ||

dīrgheṇa cakṣuṣā lakṣmīṃ bheje kuvalayasya yaḥ  |
nārīṇāṃ diśatānandaṃ doṣṇā sattārakeṇa ca  || 11.81 ||

śithilīkṛtajīvāśā yasmin koponnatabhruvi  |
ninyuḥ śirāṃsi stabdhāni na dhanūṃṣi natiṃ nṛpāḥ  || 11.82 ||

vairisiṃha iti prāpajjanma tasmājjanādhipaḥ  |
kīrtibhiryasya kundendu viśadābhiḥ saṭāyitam  || 11.83 ||

paulomīramaṇasyeva yasya cāpe vilokite  |
cakitaiḥ sarasīva kṣmā rājahaṃsairamucyata  || 11.84 ||

śrīsīyaka iti kṣetraṃ yaśasāmudabhūttataḥ  |
dilīpapratimaḥ pṛthvī śuktimuktāphalaṃ nṛpaḥ  || 11.85 ||

lakṣmīradhokṣajasyeva śaśimaulerivāmbikā  |
vaḍajetyabhavaddevī kalatraṃ yasya bhūriva  || 11.86 ||

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalenāpya prajāpuṇyairmahodayam  |
kalisaṃtamasaṃ yena vyanīyata nṛpendunā  || 11.87 ||

vaśīkṛtākṣamālo yaḥ kṣmāmatyāyatāṃ dadhan  |
rājyāśramamalaṃcakre rājārṣiḥ kuśacīvaraḥ  || 11.88 ] ||

smitajyotsnādaridreṇa bāṣpasrāvimukhendunā  |
śaśaṃsurvijayaṃ yasya rudrapāṭīpatistriyaḥ  || 11.89 ||

akaṅkaṇamakeyūramanūpuramamekhalam  |
hūṇāvarodhavaidhavya dīkṣādānaṃ vyadhatta yaḥ  || 11.90 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavarṇanam

ayaṃ netrotsavastasmājjajñe devaḥ pitṛpriyaḥ  |
jagattamo'paho netrādatreriva niśākaraḥ  || 11.91 ||

śrīmadvākapatirājo'bhūtagrajo'syāgraṇīḥ satām  |
sagarāpatyadattābdhi parikhāyāḥ patirbhuvaḥ  || 11.92 ||

atīte vikramāditye gate'staṃ sātavāhane  |
kavimitre viśaśrāma yasmin devī sarasvatī  || 11.93 ||

cakrire vedhasā nūnaṃ nirvyājaudāryaśālinaḥ  |
te cintāmaṇayo yasya nirmāṇe paramāṇavaḥ  || 11.94 ||

yaśobhisinduśucibhiryasyācchataravārijaiḥ  |
apūryatā iyaṃ brahmāṇḍa śuktirmuktāphalairiva  || 11.95 ||

śriyaṃ nīlābjakāntyā yaḥ praṇayibhyo dadau dṛśā  |
arātibhyaśca sahasā jahre nistriṃśalekhayā  || 11.96 ||

aṃsaḥ savalkalagranthiḥ sajaṭāpallavaṃ śiraḥ  |
cakre yenāhitastrīṇāmakṣasūtrāṅkitaḥ karaḥ  || 11.97 ||

puraṃ kālakramāttena prasthitenāmbikāpateḥ  |
maurvīkiṇāṅkavatyasya pṛthvī doṣṇi niveśitā  || 11.98 ||

praśasti parito viśvamujjayinyāṃ puri sthitaḥ  |
ayaṃ yayātimandhātṛ duṣyantabharatopamaḥ  || 11.99 ||

anenāstaḥ kapoleṣu pāṇḍimā ripuyoṣitām  |
samāhṛtyeva tadbhartṛ yaśaso bāhuśalinā  || 11.100 ||

sadā samakarasyāsya lakṣmīkulagṛhasya ca  |
sindhurāja iti vyaktaṃ nāma dugdhodadheriva  || 11.101 ||

anena vihitānyatra yatsāhasaśatānyataḥ  |
navīnasāhasāṅko'yaṃ vīragoṣṭhīṣu gīyate  || 11.102 ||

vindhyāntaścaratānena mṛgayāsaktacetasā  |
kanyā śaśiprabhā nāma nāgasūtiradṛśyata  || 11.103 ||

adṛśyairatha sā nāgairasya pārśvādanīyata  |
tāmanveṣṭuṃ praviṣṭena kutūhalabalādiha  || 11.104 ||

samaṇistambhamagre'tha dhāma hiraṇmayam  |
tatra mūrtā tataḥ sindhurindusūtirvilokitā  || 11.105 ||

akṛtātithyametasya bhaktinamrasya sā tataḥ  |
nītā pṛṣṭena caitena svavārtāyāmabhijñatām  || 11.106 ||

tato vajrāṅkuśodyāna hemābjāhṛtisāhasam  |
hetuḥ śaśiprabhāvāptervivṛtyāveditastayā  || 11.107 ||

asūcayatprasaṅgena triviṣṭapariporatha  |
udagramasurendrasya vīryaṃ vajrāṅkuśasya sā  || 11.108 ||

tatastaṃ pratyamarṣo'sya jhaṭityaṅkurito hṛdi  |
anyatra vīravṛtteryadayamekāntamatsarī  || 11.109 ||

panthāḥ puro'surasyāsya prāñjaleḥ śaṃsitastayā  |
asūcyatāgrataścaitatamoghaṃ darśanaṃ tava  || 11.110 ||

athedaṃ ratnavalayaṃ dattvāsmai samamāśiṣā  |
kāntā tirohitā sā ca purukutsasya bhūpateḥ  || 11.111 ||

athaitena gṛhīteyaṃ yātrā vajrāṅkuśaṃ prati  |
eṣā ca sukṛtairdṛṣṭā pādapadmadvayī tava  || 11.112 ||


__________________________________________

vaṅkumunivākyam

ityuktvā sūkticaturo virarāma ramāṅgadaḥ  |
ādade munirapyudyatdantāṃśuśabalaṃ vacaḥ  || 11.113 ||

aho purāṇarājārṣi santānakathayaitayā  |
puṇyayā hṛtamātmānamadhunā manmahe vayam  || 11.114 ||

avaśyambhāvinī tatra siddhiḥ sāhasikasya te  |
śalyaṃ triviṣṭapasyāsya hṛdayāduddhariṣyasi  || 11.115 ||

eṣa vajrāṅkuśasyājau nākṛtvāntaṃ nivartitā  |
bhujo bhuvanabhartuste diṅnāgakarapīvaraḥ  || 11.116 ||

vadhūstavācireṇātra bhaviṣyati śaśiprabhā  |
yathā kuvalayāśvasya divaḥkanyā madālasā  || 11.117 ||

sthiro bhava mitaṃ kālaṃ sthitvāsminnastapovane  |
tvayā vinīyatāmeṣa dīrghadhvajanitaḥ śramaḥ  || 11.118 ||


__________________________________________

sindhurājavākyam

ityukte muninā sa atha rājenduridamabravīt |
ājñā vilaṅghyate tāta tava kena jagadguroḥ  || 11.119 ||

atha kramonmīlitasauhṛdāsu kathāsvanekāsu mithaḥkṛtāsu  |
viśramyatāmityavadanmaharṣiḥ patiṃ pṛthivyāḥ prathitaprabhāvaḥ  || 11.120 ||

devastataḥ sa munikalpitamindranīla paryaṅkavatkanakavedisanāthamadhyam  |
adhyāsta ratnasadanaṃ parito vitāna vyālambitamauktikalataṃ navasāhasāṅkaḥ  || 11.121 ||

iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite vaṅkumaharṣidarśanaṃ nāma daśamaḥ sargaḥ


************************************************


dvādaśaḥ sargaḥ

atha mānavamīnalakṣmaṇo maṇiparyaṅkagatasya tasya sā  |
apatatphaṇirājakanyakā jagadekābharaṇaṃ smṛteḥ pathi  || 12.1 ||

vyadhita praṇayaṃ dṛśāṃ puraḥ kamanīyeṣu sa yeṣu vastuṣu  |
janitotkalikāśataiḥ sastairaraterāyatanaṃ vyadhīyata  || 12.2 ||

muhuraṅgalatāvivartanaiḥ śvasitaiḥ śūnyavilokanena ca  |
kṣitibharturupāntavartinā madanākalpakamanvamīyata  || 12.3 ||

kadalīdaladattamāruto hṛdayanyastamṛṇālakandalaḥ  |
atha tasya babhūva yatnavānupacāre śiśire ramāṅgadaḥ  || 12.4 ||

abhavaddvayameva bhūpateḥ smarataptasya manovinodanam  |
sudṛśaḥ saḥ karātithiḥ śaraḥ saśca hāraḥ stanacandanāṅkitaḥ  || 12.5 ||

madanāntarito'pi laṅghitaḥ pathi jātena pariśrameṇa sa  |
stimitaḥ kṣaṇamāsta kaumudī viśadakṣaumatirohitānanaḥ  || 12.6 ||

atha pārśvacareṇa sādaraṃ mṛdusaṃvāhitapādapallavaḥ  |
saḥ kuraṅgadṛśeva nidrayā caturaṃ locanayoracumbyata  || 12.7 ||

svapuropavane samutsukaḥ sumukhīṃ svapnaprathena pārthivaḥ  |
avataṃsitahemapaṅkajāmatha tāmaṅkagatāṃ vyalokayat || 12.8 ||

abhikāntamapāṅgapātinā jitanīlābjadalena cakṣuṣā  |
dadhatīmapavartitaṃ hriyā mukhamāpāṇḍukapolamaṇḍalam  || 12.9 ||

śaradindumarīcinirmalaṃ vigaladvepathunā stanāṃśukam  |
muhurākṣipatīmalakṣitaṃ ślathamuktāvalayena pāṇinā  || 12.10 ||

navapallavakāntinā kimapyacirāvāsitapuṣpaketunā  |
lalitāmadhareṇa bibhratīṃ mukhacandrāṃśusaṭāṃ smitacchaṭām  || 12.11 ||

jagadekavilokanotsave vapuṣi svedakaṇairalaṅkṛtām  |
uditāmiva mandarāhatātudadherlagnasudhālavāṃ śriyam  || 12.12 ||

valitāhitaniḥsahāṅguli svakaraśleṣaviśeṣakampini  |
pulakinyadhikaṃ vimuñcatīṃ cakitaṃ vāmakuce vilocane  || 12.13 ||

atibhāsuraratnakuṇḍalāmatikāntāyatahāramaṇḍalām  |
jaghanaślathahemamekhalāmasameṣoradhidevatāmiva  || 12.14 ||
[syntax kulakam]


__________________________________________

nāyakoktiḥ

atha sasmitamāttavepathuḥ patito manmathapatriṇāṃ pathi  |
iti tāṃ praṇayārdragirā saḥ kilāmbhojamukhīmavocat || 12.15 ||

valitaṃ na vibhāti pṛṣṭhataḥ kabarīkāntamidaṃ tavānanam  |
ayi nīlapayodhalekhayā saḥ pariṣvaṅgamivendumaṇḍalam  || 12.16 ||

idamaṅgadavartinā karairmaṇinā ruddhamiveritaṃ hriyā  |
na samarthamitopavartituṃ vadanaṃ te lalitāṅgi kā gatiḥ  || 12.17 ||

idamardhavilokitādharaṃ madhurāpāṅgataraṅgitekṣaṇam  |
śriyamātanute sitāsitaṃ sutanu tryaśruvilokitaṃ tava  || 12.18 ||

militastava gaṇḍalekhayā sudati svedalavārdrapatrayā  |
kimapi spṛhaṇīya eṣa me marudāsannadivāntaśītalaḥ  || 12.19 ||

ayamutpalaḥpatralocane tava bimbādharapāṭalacchaviḥ  |
avalokaya kartumīhate padamastācalacūlake raviḥ  || 12.20 ||

duritaghnamidaṃ sudarśanaṃ dadhatā bimbamanūrusāratheḥ  |
smaralakṣmi vihāyasāmunā tava kṛṣṇena hṛte vilocane  || 12.21 ||

aravindakareṇa lohitaṃ kamalinyā dhṛtamātapāśukam  |
idamuṣṇakareṇa kṛṣyate valitenāparadigvadhūṃ prati  || 12.22 ||

karuṇārpitalocanaṃ mithaḥ kramaviśleṣagaladbisāṅkuram  |
idamārdrayatīva me mano mithunaṃ mānini cakravākayoḥ  || 12.23 ||

avalokaya bhīru samprati tritayena tritayaṃ viyujyate  |
dyumaṇiḥ prabhayā, śriyāmbujaṃ priyayā sāśrurayaṃ vihaṅgamaḥ  || 12.24 ||

idamambarapalvalodarādatitāmradyuti kāladantinā  |
ravivāriruhaṃ nirasyate kanakasnigdhamayūkhakesaram  || 12.25 ||

paricumbati vāruṇīṃ diśaṃ purato rāgahṛte vivasvati  |
digiyaṃ śatamanyulāñchitā bhavati śyāmamukhī mitodari  || 12.26 ||

iha bhāntyatilohitātapa stabakāḥ paśya vanāntabhūmayaḥ  |
tapanānugamotsavāṅkitā dinalakṣmyeva padaiḥ sayāvakaiḥ  || 12.27 ||

madirākṣi puro'valokyatāmaparasyāmayamānato diśi  |
stimitāmavagāhate gatiṃ gurugotraskhalitākulo raviḥ  || 12.28 ||

amunā śatapatrabandhunā sahasā sundari yadyadujjhitam  |
samamadriguhāmukhasthitaistimiraistattaditaḥkaṭākṣitam  || 12.29 ||

viramannayi pallavādhare suravīthīpathiko virocanaḥ  |
ayamastagirerniṣīdati svakarāmṛṣṭaśilātale tale  || 12.30 ||

iyamaśrutaraṅgitāṃ dṛśaṃ dvitaye cakravadhūrvimuñcati  |
navakuṅkumalohite ravau dayite cāndraviyogaviklave  || 12.31 ||

calito'si vada kva māṃ vinā virahaṃ soḍhumahaṃ na te kṣamā  |
kṛtapaṅkajakuḍmalāñjalirnalinī kāntamitīva yācate  || 12.32 ||

anupuñjitapiṅgadīdhiti drutalākṣāruṇadarpaṇopamam  |
parato'stagireridaṃ galatyanavadyāṅgi pataṅgamaṇḍalam  || 12.33 ||

sarale saha vārijaśriyā nibhṛtaṃ kvāpi gataḥ sa bhāskaraḥ  |
vada tena vinābjinī kathaṃ kṣaṇadāmadya natāṅgi neṣyati  || 12.34 ||

sphuṭavidrumarājinaikataḥ sadṛśaṃ jātamudañcatā nabhaḥ  |
sudati tvadapāṅgapaṭale paṭu sāndhye mahasi prasarpati  || 12.35 ||

paripiñjaritāsitāmbarairnibiḍaiḥ kaṃ na haranti hāribhiḥ  |
ayi sāyamimāḥ payodharairdhṛtasandhyātapakuṅkumairdiśaḥ  || 12.36 ||

kṣaṇadābhimukhena khaṇḍitā nanu sandhyā tamasā manasvinī  |
kupiteva nivartate javātativācālavihaṅganūpuram  || 12.37 ||

tava caṇḍi viḍamayatyadastanusandhyātapaliptamambujam  |
maṇikuṇḍalakāntisaṅkarātidamātāmrakapolamānanam  || 12.38 ||

uditāni tamāṃsi sā ca te dayitā dainyamupaiti padminī  |
dinabharturitīva śaṃsituṃ sahasā sundari sandhyayā gatam  || 12.39 ||

nihitaṃ balidīpakeṣu tattapanenāśu mahaḥ kṛśodari  |
svaśarasphuritaṃ manobhuvā tava savṛīḍavilokiteṣviva  || 12.40 ||

atasīkusumopamaṃ mukhe tadanu tvatkucacūcukadyuti  |
atha bālatamālamāṃsalaṃ prasṛtaṃ saṃprati sarvatastamaḥ  || 12.41 ||

tarukoṭaramūkaśārikaṃ nijanīḍāṅkanilīnakokilam  |
karabhoru sanidrabarhiṇaṃ pramododyānamidaṃ nimīlati  || 12.42 ||

prasṛtairgirikandarodarātidamindīvaradāmakāntibhiḥ  |
adhunā timirairvigāhyate bhuvanaṃ padmasaraśca dantibhiḥ  || 12.43 ||

timirāñjanabhaktiśobhinā dhavalenāyatapakṣmapaṅktinā  |
amunā bhavatīva cakṣuṣā kumudenaiti rucaṃ kumudvatī  || 12.44 ||

udarasthitayoḥ kutūhalātalinoḥ śrotumivāsphuṭaṃ vacaḥ  |
kamalasya nilīya niścalaṃ dalasandhiṣvavatiṣṭhate tamaḥ  || 12.45 ||

tarale'tisitāsitadyutāviha dolāyitamīkṣaṇadvaye  |
likhitāgarupatralekhayostimiraṃ mūrcchati te kapolayoḥ  || 12.46 ||

uḍubhiḥ khamitastataḥ kṣaṇāduditairbhaṅgurakeśi bhātyadaḥ  |
atigāḍhadinoṣṇajanmabhiḥ paritaḥ svedalavairivāṅkitam  || 12.47 ||

śabalaṃ śaśalāñchanatviṣā satamaḥ paśya mahendradiṅmukham  |
acalendrasutāsmitacchavi churitaṃ kaṇṭhamumāpateriva  || 12.48 ||

ahirājasute vilokyatāmiyamindoḥ prathamodgatā kalā  |
ayi bhāti yayā indradiṅmukhe pramadevārdranakhāṅkarekhayā  || 12.49 ||

yadi kautukamāyatekṣane na cirādeva sudhārdrayānayā  |
aravindadaladyutau kare mṛdu līlāvalayaṃ karomi te  || 12.50 ||

anavadyamitaḥ puraḥ sthitaṃ viditaṃ kiṃ śaśinā tavānanam  |
nabhasaḥ sahasāṅkameṣa yanna kalaṅkatrapayādhirohati  || 12.51 ||

idamudgatamindumaṇḍalaṃ digiyaṃ paśya bibharti lakṣmavat |
tvamivācchakapolamaṇḍala sphuṭakālāgarupatramānanam  || 12.52 ||

vigalattimirāṃśuke śanaiḥ spṛśati vyaktimādhīratārake  |
iha paśya niśāvadhūmukhe sphurati śvetamarīcikuṇḍalam  || 12.53 ||

ayamullikhati dhruvaṃ karairvidhurindīvaralocane tamaḥ  |
kumudeṣu tathā hi dṛśyatāṃ nipatantyasya lavā ivālayaḥ  || 12.54 ||

idamañjananīlamāhataṃ pihitāśaṃ tuhināṃśunā karaiḥ  |
acalendraguhāsu līyate śanakaiḥ saṃkucitaṃ punastanaḥ(?)  || 12.55 ||

masṛṇollasadaṃśumaṇḍala chalataḥ paśya divaḥkṛte'nayā  |
iyamindusamudgakādito niśayā hāralateva kṛṣyate  || 12.56 ||

yadabhūttamasā jagattathā pihite puṣkarapatralocane  |
tadidaṃ parataḥ prakāśitaṃ śaśinā kuṅkumakandapāṇḍunā  || 12.57 ||

prasṛteva vilocanodare tilake saṅkuciteva cāndena  |
kaliteva natāṅgi lakṣyate tava muktāvalayeṣu candrikā  || 12.58 ||

kucayoḥ pratibimbitaḥ samaṃ vidhureko'pi bhavatyayaṃ dvidhā  |
vidhineva vibhinnasaṃpuṭastava lāvaṇyasudhāsamudgakaḥ  || 12.59 ||

hṛtamugdhamadhūkaśobhayoranayoḥ pannagalokakaumudi  |
tava candrakalāḥ kapolayoḥ patitāḥ sparśakutūhalādiva  || 12.60 ||

ayamindumukhi tvayā yathā samupaiti spṛhaṇīyatāṃ janaḥ  |
anayaiṣa samāgatastathā niśayā paśya kuraṅgalāñchanas || 12.61 ||

dhanuṣi kriyate'dhirohaṇaṃ smaramaurvīlatayā tanūdari  |
śaśineritayā samucchrite pulinādrau ca payodhavelayā  || 12.62 ||
kṛtacāṭuśataiḥ parasparaṃ makarandārdrarajaḥsugandhiṣu  |
sthitamantaramīṣu sāṃprataṃ bhramaraiḥ puṣkaravāsaveśmasu  || 12.63 ||

marutā suhṛdeva vījitaṃ kumudāmodamucā śanairitaḥ  |
svapiti praṇayārdrayoridaṃ mithunaṃ mānini rājahaṃsayoḥ  || 12.64 ||

ayi cakravadhūriyaṃ puraḥ karuṇaṃ kūjati hā tapasvini  |
iha sākṣitayālamāvayorucitaṃ gantumataḥ kṛpāvati  || 12.65 ||

iti bhūtalavāsavaḥ sastāmabhidhāya pramadāṃ priyaṃvadaḥ  |
praviveśa tayāsamaṃ kila pramanāḥ kelinagendrakandaram  || 12.66 ||

jhaṭiti sphuṭabhāvasaṅkarāṃ madhurāmaṅgalatāṃ dadhānayā  |
śaśikāntaśilātalaṃ tataḥ saḥ kilādhyāsta tayā yuvānvitaḥ  || 12.67 ||

tadanu trapayā parāṅmukhīṃ pulakālaṅkṛtapīvarastanīm  |
saḥ kilāñcitacāṭurānayatsumukhīṃ tāmanukūlavṛttitām  || 12.68 ||

atha mantharalocanaṃ hriyā vinamatsmeramukhaḥ smitānncitam  |
so dadarśa kila prajeśvaraḥ sudṛśaḥ svinnakapolamānanam  || 12.69 ||

yadalaṃ kila mānavatyabhūdṛjuvannendumukhī kilaikṣat |
likhiteva kilāsta yatparaṃ nṛpatestena manaḥ kilāharat || 12.70 ||

parimṛjya mukhaṃ vilāsinā śravaṇendīvarareṇurūṣitam  |
sudṛśaḥ śamavāripaṅkilātalakāntastilakādapāsyata  || 12.71 ||

atha tāṃ śithilīkṛtatrapāmasamapremahṛtaḥ kileśvaraḥ  |
smarakelikalārasajñatāmanayadyūthapatirvaśāmiva  || 12.72 ||

śithilākulakeśapāśayā parimṛṣṭārdrakapolapatrayā  |
viralādhararatnarāgayā sulabhasvedamukhendubimbayā  || 12.73 ||

truṭitojjhitahāralekhayā nibiḍāśleṣakṛśāṅgarāgayā  |
asamagranakhāṅgamaṇḍita stanavinyastasakampahastayā  || 12.74 ||

adhikādhikajātalajjayā mṛdumīlannayanatribhāgayā  |
atha kāmapi nirvṛtiṃ tayā saḥ kilāpatphaṇirājakanyayā  || 12.75 ||
[syntax kulakam]

dadatā nalinīdalānilaṃ vikasatsvedakaṇe kucadvaye  |
caturaṃ kila dīrghacakṣuṣastadanu klāntiranena cicchide  || 12.76 ||

atha satrapayā dhṛtāṃśukāṃ jaghanasrastavisūtramekhalām  |
avataṃsitalocanotpalāṃ nijamaṅkaṃ lalanāṃ nināya ca  || 12.77 ||

sudṛśaḥ saḥ kilānyataścutaṃ(?) svapade maulimaṇiṃ nyaveśayat |
akarocca kilāruṇāṅgulirlalitāvartanakuñcitān kacān  || 12.78 ||

paśyātra darpaṇatale likhitā mayeyaṃ patrāvalī taruṇi te valitānaneti  |
svapnāntarapraṇayajalpitamātmabharturaśrūyata smitamukhena ramāṅgadena  || 12.79 ||

atha śuci paṭhatā śukena sāma sphuṭamuṭajāṅgaṇapādapasthitena  |
viracitadayitāsamāgamasya prasabhamabhajyata pārthivasya nidrā  || 12.80 ||

jhaṭiti vigate svapnāyātapriyānavasaṅgame puno'pi tathā tatpratyāśānimīlitalocanaḥ  |
likhita iva saḥ kṣmāpālo'bhūtkṣaṇaṃ nanu tādṛśāmapi manasijo dhairyaṃ lumpatyaho bata sāhasam  || 12.81 ||

iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye phaṇirājasutāsvapnasamāgamo nāma dvādaśaḥ sargaḥ

************************************************


trayodaśaḥ sargaḥ

tatastathā pañcaśarapratāritaḥ sajṛmbhamunmīlayati sma locane  |
kṛtāṅguliśleṣavivartitollasatbhujāṃsasampīḍitakuṇḍalo nṛpaḥ  || 13.1 ||

saḥ saṅgataṃ yanmṛgaśāvacakṣuṣaḥ śramāptanidraḥ sphuṭamanvabhūdiva  |
jhaṭityabhūjjāgradavasthayāsya tatpurātanālekhyamivāsphuṭaṃ hṛdi  || 13.2 ||

kucāṅgarāgaḥ kṛśamadhyayā tayā mayi dhruvaṃ saṅkramito bhavediti  |
śanaiḥ sa nidrākaluṣeṇa cakṣuṣā parāmamarśāṅgamanaṅgamohitaḥ  || 13.3 ||

apāsya vāmetarakarṇabhūṣaṇaṃ tathā śarairenamavākiratsmaraḥ  |
yathāsya dhairyaṃ galati sma mānasātsavikriyaṃ śuktipuṭādiva udakam  || 13.4 ||

dṛśaṃ viṣādastimitāmupāntage niveśya tenātha niśaśvase  |
yathā muhuḥ śyāmalatāṃ jagāhire vitānamuktāphalajālakasrajaḥ  || 13.5 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadavākyam

kayā nu sāraṅgadṛśāsi kāritaḥ kapolapatrāvalikalpanaśramam  |
tamityavocatparihāsavānatho(?) ramāṅgadaḥ kiṃcidiva ślathāṅgadam  || 13.6 ||

tataḥ sa muktāsitamādadhatsmitaṃ jitapravālatviṣi dantavāsasi  |
śaśaṃsa tasmai bhujagendrakanyakā samāgamaṃ svapnajamabjalocanaḥ  || 13.7 ||

tadāśrayaivānucareṇa vardhitā kathāsudhevāsya tato vilāsinaḥ  |
abhūtparaṃ manmathatāpaśāntaye na padminīpatramarunna candanam  || 13.8 ||

ajāyatāntaḥkaraṇena tāmyatā na hemapadmāharaṇāya satvaram  |
bhujaḥ sadā rakṣaṇadīkṣitaḥ kṣiteramandamaspandata cāsya dakṣiṇaḥ  || 13.9 ||


__________________________________________

vaṅkumunerāgamanam

tataḥ pinaddhojjvalahemavalkalaṃ vahantamaṃsārdhavilambinīrjaṭāḥ  |
sanāthavāmetarapāṇipaṅkajaṃ parisphurantyā sphaṭikākṣamālayā  || 13.10 ||

viśālanetrābharaṇairanudrutaṃ sadaiva darbhāṅkuralālitairmṛgaiḥ  |
saśiṣyamabhyāgatamaṅganāntike viśāmpatirvaṅkumaharṣimaikṣata  || 13.11 ||

vimuktaparyaṅkatalaḥ sasaṃbhramaṃ nirīkṣya sadyo maṇimandirādbahiḥ  |
kirīṭaratnadyutidīptabhūtalaṃ praṇāmamasmai saścakāra sādaram  || 13.12 ||

tataḥ kṛtāśīrmaṇivedikāstṛte munirnyaṣīdatsakuraṅgacarmaṇi  |
saścāsanatvaṃ tadanujñayānayannṛpacandrāścandramaṇeḥ śilātalam  || 13.13 ||


__________________________________________

munipraśnaḥ

api śrameṇāyatamārgajanmanā tanurmahārāja taveyamujjhitā  |
muniḥ praharṣeṇa kṛtārhaṇastadā sa rājacūḍāmaṇimityapṛcchata  || 13.14 ||


__________________________________________

bhūpativākyam

athādadhadvaktra ivāmśukāñcalaṃ tuṣārapāṇḍuprasṛtairdvijāṃśubhiḥ  |
akṛtrimapraśrayapeśalaṃ vacaḥ sa bhūpatirvaktumiti pracakrame  || 13.15 ||

amī sahante mama tāta na śramaṃ praṇāmalagnāstava pādapāṃsavaḥ  |
kiyacciraṃ candramarīcicumbite padaṃ nidhatte kumude dinaklamaḥ  || 13.16 ||


__________________________________________

kapidarśanam

nṛlokapātālatalāśraye mithaḥ kathānubandhe śithilībhavatyatha  |
paryāṇaparyutsukamānaso muniḥ sa yāvadāpraṣṭumiyeṣa pārthivaḥ  || 13.17 ||

anena tāvaddadṛśe puraḥsthito viśālalāṅgūlalato valīmukhaḥ  |
adhaḥsthalīnirgatajahnukanyako himojjvalaḥ pāda ivāmbikāguroḥ  || 13.18 ||

kareṇa bibhranmadhumattakeralī kapolavatpāṭalakānti dāḍimam  |
aharmukhākṛṣṭapataṅgamaṇḍalaḥ phalāśayā bāla ivāñjanīsutaḥ || 13.19 ||
[syntax sandānitakam]


__________________________________________

phalārpaṇam

dvayorivārthaḥ khalu dharmakāmayostayostrilokaspṛhaṇīyayoḥ kapiḥ  |
munīndrabhūcandramasoḥ sthito'ntare tapasvibhiḥ smeramukhairadṛśyata  || 13.20 ||

athārpitaṃ tena phalaṃ tadādade sa vismito madhyamalokavāsavaḥ  |
japāruṇaṃ mārutineva maithilī śikhaṇḍaratnaṃ daśakaṇṭhaśāsanaḥ  || 13.21 ||

akṛtrimaśrīnilayena rāgiṇā narendracihnāṅkitahastaśobhinā  |
suvṛttatāmudvahatā svabhāvataḥ sastena reje bhṛśamātmanā yathā  || 13.22 ||

ajātapākasya navātapādhikāṃ punaḥ punastasya vilokya śoṇatām  |
navapravālopamameṇacakṣuṣaḥ smaran sa bimboṣṭḥmavāpa śūnyatām  || 13.23 ||

athāsya sīdanmaṇibandhanātkarādavāñcataḥ kampavisūtritāṅguleḥ  |
papāta paścādiva hemakuṭṭime tadasphutaddrāgiva dāḍimīphalam  || 13.24 ||

tadantarātkiṃśukakāntitaskaraḥ sphuranmaṇīnāṃ nikaro'tha niryayau  |
udarciṣaḥ puṣpaśarāsanakrudhā kaṇotkarastryambakalocanādiva  || 13.25 ||

atha dvayenāvanipākaśāsanaḥ sa vismayasyāgramahīmanīyata  |
vanaukasā tena vinītavṛttinā vikīrṇabhāsā maṇidāḍimena ca  || 13.26 ||

saścādbhutaprābhṛtatoṣitaḥ kare cakāra revāmaṇikaṅkaṇaṃ kapeḥ  |
ghanātyayaṛtornijamindupāṇḍure payodakhaṇḍe harivāḍivāyudham  || 13.27 ||

tataḥ sudhāsūtimivojjhitākṛtirnavāñjanaśyāmalayāṅgalekhayā  |
nṛpaḥ kṣaṇādeva vicitrabhūṣaṇaṃ pumāṃsamagre na hariṃ tamaikṣata  || 13.28 ||

kṛtānatirvismitamānase munau ramāṅgade sādaramuktalocanaḥ  |
vyadhātpraṇāmaṃ saḥ kṛtāñjalirnṛpe kapolavellatkaladhautakuṇḍalaḥ  || 13.29 ||


__________________________________________

munikṛtaḥ praśnaḥ

alaṅkṛtaḥ kasya vadānvayastvayā padaṃ kva te kiṃ ca kapirbhavānabhūt |
tamevamāha sma savismayormiṇā nṛpeṇa sākūtavilokito muniḥ  || 13.30 ||
[com {āha pasttense}]


__________________________________________

śaśikhaṇḍavākyam

tataḥ sa mugdhendumayūkhabandhubhiḥ prasādayan dantamarīcibhirdiśaḥ  |
navāmbubhārālasanīradāvalī ninādadhīrāmiti vācamāde  || 13.31 ||

śikhaṇḍaketoḥ śaśikhaṇḍa ityahaṃ munindra vidyādharaśāsituḥ sutaḥ  |
surāṅganādhyāsitaratnakandhare mamādhivāsaḥ śaśikāntaparvate  || 13.32 ||

rathāṅgapāṇeḥ pratimā samudrataḥ svayaṃ mahānīlamayī vinirgatā  |
iti pravādaḥ paramīkṣituṃ ca tāṃ gatā maṇidvīpamitaḥ purastriyaḥ  || 13.33 ||

mamāpi tasyāmadhikaṃ kutūhalaṃ tadehi yāvaḥ kṛta eṣa te'ñjaliḥ  |
kadā cidevaṃ sahasopasṛtya māṃ priyā yayāce praṇipatya mālatī  || 13.34 ||
[syntax sandānitaka]

tataḥ khamindīvaranīlamekatastayā sahotpatya javena gacchataḥ  |
sastāta śailendrabharakṣamaḥ kṣaṇātpapāta me locanagocare'rṇavaḥ  || 13.35 ||

upoḍhanānāmaṇimauktikotkaraiḥ karairivordhvaṃ prasaradbhirūrmibhiḥ  |
anarghyamarghyaṃ jagadekacakṣuṣe samudyato dātumivāṃśumāline  || 13.36 ||

navapravāladyutipāṭalodaraḥ karo murāreriva śārṅgalāñchitaḥ  |
alaṅkṛto jahnumaharṣikanyayā pṛthurjaṭājūṭa ivāndhakadviṣaḥ  || 13.37 ||

udagrakallolakadarthitagrahairagādhapātālatalāvagāhibhiḥ  |
diśo nirundhannavameghanādibhirnimagnadiṅnāgamadāvilairjalaiḥ  || 13.38 ||

samedhitaśrīrabhitastalotthitaiḥ sphuranmaṇistomamayūkhadāmabhiḥ  |
yugāntajīmūtaśatodayārpitaiḥ pulomakanyāpatikārmukairiva  || 13.39 ||

upāntaviśrāntapayodamaṇḍalairjaladvipaprastutavaprakelibhiḥ  |
cirollasaddvīpadhiyā samīkṣitairviśālanetraistimibhiḥ kṛtādbhutaḥ  || 13.40 ||

sujātakāṭhiṇyapayodharāḥ spṛhāmupāharantīḥ pathi tādṛśoḥ pathi  |
kva ciddadhānaḥ śaradindupeśalāḥ śilāsu śuktīrjalamānuṣīriva  || 13.41 ||

nijaughasīmantitasānukardamairbṛhaddarīpuñjitaratnarāśibhiḥ  |
adhaḥ praviṣṭoddhṛtakacchapoddhṛtairmahācalairullikhitāmbaraḥ kva cit || 13.42 ||

savegavelānilavellitāḥ kva cinnavodgatā vidrumakandalīrdadhan  |
śikhā ivordhvaṃ taruṇārkalohitā vinirgatā vāḍavajātavedasaḥ  || 13.43 ||

kva citsudhāpāṇḍuni phenamaṇḍale nilīnadūrvādalanīlanīradaḥ  |
sanāthatāṃ nīta ivopari sphuṭaṃ phaṇīndraparyaṅkaśayena śārṅgiṇā  || 13.44 ||

kva cinmaṇīnāṃ kumudodaratviṣaḥ sitetarendīvaramecakāḥ kva cit |
kva ciddadhānaḥ śukacañcupāṭalāstaṭeṣu muktāśabalodarāḥ śilāḥ  || 13.45 ||
[syntax kulakam]

tathaiva tasyopari gatvarasya me tamālanīlena pathā payomucām  |
tvarāviśīrṇaślathabandhanāñcitaḥ papāta sīmantamaṇirmṛgīdṛśaḥ  || 13.46 ||
13.46  dṛṣṭavyam‌ . pāṇini 3.2.164]

pradhāvamānena mayāntarāntarā nakhāgranirlūnamayūkhapallavaḥ  |
saścāntaraṃ dīdhitimānivodadherviveśa kośāmratarucchaṭāruṇaḥ  || 13.47 ||

nivartamānaṃ tu haṭhādvikṛṣyatā turaṅgahastena nirundhatā nabhaḥ  |
kṣaṇādiva kvāpi rasātalodare karīva cikṣepa kṛtāravo'rṇavaḥ  || 13.48 ||


__________________________________________

strīdarśanam

mayātha tatra bhramatā savismayaṃ tamudvahantī maṇimutprabhaṃ kare  |
adṛśyataikā viśatī tapovanaṃ smarasya mūrtā mamateva kanyakā  || 13.49 ||

tataḥ priyāmaulimaṇirna me'rpitaḥ punaḥ punaḥ prārthitayāpi yattvayā  |
ahaṃ tadasyā makarāṅkite balādapāharaṃ manmatharatnapāduke  || 13.50 ||

kimāśramaṃ śūnyamidaṃ tapodhanairanena hā dhiṅmuṣitāsmi dasyunā  |
atheti bāṣpodgamagadgadaiḥ padairnuhurvadantī karuṇaṃ ruroda sā  || 13.51 ||


__________________________________________

munidarśanam

tatastadīye ruditadhvanau śrute sasaṃbhramaṃ ko'pi mahātapā muniḥ  |
viniryayau ratnaśilāgṛhādbahistamālabhāsastaraṇirghanādiva  || 13.52 ||

anena kenāpi tavāśrame balādidaṃ hi tātābharaṇaṃ hṛtaṃ mama  |
iti krudhaṃ tadvacasā sa ādade havirniṣekeṇa śikhāmivānalaḥ  || 13.53 ||


__________________________________________

śāpavarṇanam

nibaddhabhīmabhrukuṭirvilokayan dṛśā tadolkākapiśogratārayā  |
sastīvrakopasphuritādharo'vadadvaco mamākṣipya kṛtānateriti  || 13.54 ||

prasūnamapyatra na jātu vīrudhāṃ haratyayaṃ naḥ pavanastapovane  |
tvayā tu saṃpratyabalāvibhūṣaṇe śaṭhātmanāśāya karaḥ prasāritaḥ  || 13.55 ||

akāri kāpeyamidaṃ tvayedṛśaṃ yadadya sadyaḥ kapireva tadbhava  |
tataḥ sa māmityaśapatkamaṇḍalorapaḥ samādāya davānalopamaḥ  || 13.56 ||


__________________________________________

kopaśāntiḥ

athāsya kopaḥ praśaśāma mānase śanaiḥ kṛpā sānuśaye prasīdati  |
apāṃ kaṇastiṣṭhati vīcikampite na padminīpatrapuṭodare ciram  || 13.57 ||

ihānutāpo bhagavan vimucyatāmiyaṃ madīyā bhavitavyatedṛśī  |
taducyatāṃ śāpaniśāmukhodgataṃ kadā mamedaṃ timiraṃ vyaraṃsyati  || 13.58 ||

mayaivamuktaḥ sastadaivamūcivān yadā puro vaṅkumunerihāgataḥ  |
kare tavādhāsyati vatsa kaṅkaṇaṃ sa nārmadaṃ sīyakarājanandanaḥ  || 13.59 ||

tataḥ prabhṛtyeva valīmukhākṛteḥ samāḥ sahasraṃ vasato rasātale  |
anena me saṃprati pārthivendunā tavāśrame śāpatamastiraskṛtam  || 13.60 ||


__________________________________________

pratikriyākaraṇecchā

kṛtaṃ yadetena munīndra līlayā pratikriyāṃ tatra na kartumasmyalam  |
himatviṣaḥ pratyupakāragocaro marīcilīḍhakraśimā kimaṃśumān  || 13.61 ||

tathāpyayaṃ deva nijaprayojane laghīyasi kvāpi niyojyataṃ janaḥ  |
anūrumurvītalaratnadīpakaḥ kimāryamā nādhita sūtakarmaṇi  || 13.62 ||

tenaivamuktaḥ praṇayonmukhena nrpastrapānamramukho babhūva  |
atādṛśānāṃ stutayaḥ prakṛtyā madaṃ yaduddīpayituṃ yatante  || 13.63 ||


__________________________________________

nijavṛttakathanam

evaṃ sudhārasasamṛddhimanohareṇa ślāghyena tena śaśikhaṇḍasamāgamena  |
kāmapyavāpadumayā ghaṭitasya lakṣmīṃ saḥ kṣmāpatirjhaṭiti jūṭa ivāṣṭamūrteḥ  || 13.64 ||

tenātha sūnṛtavacaḥśrutaye saḥ pṛṣṭaḥ pṛthvītalāgamanahetumajānateva  |
ākhyātavān smitasudhāsnapitauṣṭhabimbastasmai nijavyatikaraṃ naralokapālaḥ  || 13.65 ||

vidyādharastadanu saḥ prahito'pi rājñā naiva svadhāmagamanābhimukho babhūva  |
pūrvopakāriṇi jane'nupakṛtya kiṃ cidyānto yadunnatadhiyaḥ kimapi trapante  || 13.66 ||

deva prasīda samitiḥ kriyatāmidānīṃ bhṛtyaḥ svapādarajasāmayamagrayāyī  |
tasyeti valgu vacanaṃ vacasā maharṣeḥ samrāṭtataḥ saḥ kathamapyurarīcakāra  || 13.67 ||


__________________________________________

vidyādharasainyāgamanam

āmandraśaṅkhapaṭahasvanasūcitaṃ prāksainyaṃ kṣaṇānnijamatha smṛtamātrameva  |
dhautāsipatrabahulāṃśulatopagūḍhaṃ pātālasaṃtamasamasya puro babhūva  || 13.68 ||


__________________________________________

prasthānam

tenopapāditamatho rathamutpātakamadhyāsya kārmukasanāthakaro narendraḥ  |
vajrāṅkuśaṃ prati sa vaṅkumuniprayuktaḥ prasthānamaṅgalavidhirmasṛṇaṃ pratasthe  || 13.69 ||

lagnenāṅge yugapaduṭajadvāradeśādadūre paśyantīnāṃ munimṛgadṛśāṃ locanāṃśūtkareṇa  |
uddāmājikratumakhavidherdīkṣayā saḥ kṣitīśo medhyāmeṇatvacamiva dadhan saḥ kṣaṇaṃ lakṣyate sma  || 13.70 ||
[var13.70 {॰vidher |lem |k̭ ॰mahā॰ |ed}]

calitayatisamādhi trastasāraṅgaśāvaṃ nibhṛtaśukabhṛtoccairnaḍimuḍḍīnabarhi  |
hariṇamithunamuktānyonyakaṇḍūyanaṃ tanmunivanamabhito'bhūtsainyakolāhalena  || 13.71 ||
[13.71 nalim?]

atha pathi navasāhasāṅkaḥ sa vidyādharairvanditaḥ pramuditamunikanyakāmuktanīvāralājāñjaliḥ  |
śriyamadhita ramāṅgadenānvitastūryaghoṣormibhiḥ kulagirikuharapratidhvānaṃ dīrghairnirundhan diśaḥ  || 13.72 ||

iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye vidyādharādhipasamāgamo nāma trayodaśaḥ sargaḥ

************************************************


caturdaśaḥ sargaḥ


__________________________________________

ākāśārohaṇam

athāśramopāntamahīṃ vihāya nemisvanotkaṇṭhitanīlakaṇṭhām  |
ratho'sya vidyādharamantraśaktyā rathāṅgapaṇeḥ padamāruroha  || 14.1 ||

sasaṃbhramottaṃbhitakarṇatālamākarṇito dikkaribhiḥ sakaṃpaiḥ  |
ādhmātaśaṅkhasvanamāṃsalo'gre tasyodagānmaṅgalatūryaghoṣaḥ  || 14.2 ||

udasya vaktrāṇi nabhasthalena yāntaṃ tamaikṣanta tapasvikanyāḥ  |
ākarṇavistāritamugdhanetrāḥ patrāntarairāśramapādapānām  || 14.3 ||

vrajan sa vidyādharavāhinīnāṃ madhye babhau madhyamalokapālaḥ  |
analpasaundaryasudhaikasūtiḥ śaśīva nakṣatraparamparāṇām  || 14.4 ||

kutūhalollolāsitapakṣalekhānyākṛṣṭakarṇotpalavibhramāṇi  |
tasminnamuñcyanta nitambinībhirapāṅgavalgīni vilocanāni  || 14.5 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadakṛtaṃ varṇanam

athāntikasthena saścābhyadhāyi ramāṅgadenetthamavantināthaḥ  |
sahasraśaḥ khe calitāni paśyan vidyādharāṇāṃ purato balāni  || 14.6 ||

haimaṃ nṛpa syandanamutpatākamākampi ratnāṅkitaketumete  |
sahasraraśmeriva vālakhilyā vidhyādharāste parivārayanti  || 14.7 ||

vrajannamartyapramadāvimukta mandāramālājaṭilāṃsakuṭaḥ  |
kundacchaṭāpāṇḍusaṭākalāpaḥ kātyāyanīsiṃha ivāvabhāsi  || 14.8 ||

prayāṇatūryadhvanireṣa kiṃ te pātālakukṣipratinādasāndraḥ  |
ravaḥ prasarpatyuta bhairavo'yamakālakalpāntapayodharāṇām  || 14.9 ||

mūle bhuvaḥ kajjaladhūlikalpamālakṣyate paśya nilīnametat |
nīrandhravidyādharamauliratna prabhāṅkuraprāśitamandhakāram  || 14.10 ||

āyāmimālāmaṇikāntidaṇḍairutsārayantaḥ paritastamāṃsi  |
amī marunnartitacāmarāste khamullikhantīva khuraisturaṅgāḥ  || 14.11 ||

vihasya vidyādharabālikābhiḥ samaṃ vimuktā nayanatribhāgaiḥ  |
prasthānalājāñjalayastavaite rathe patākāskhalitāḥ patanti  || 14.12 ||

narendra vidyādharapuṅgavānāmete puraḥ paśya kṛpāṇapaṭṭāḥ  |
kālāñjanaśyāmatayāśrayante taraṅgatāṃ vyomamahārṇavasya  || 14.13 ||

vācālayantyaḥ kakubhāṃ mukhāni nādena jāmbūnadakiṅkiṇīnām  |
imā vimānāvalayaḥ kathaṃ cidanyonyaruddhaprasarāḥ prayānti  || 14.14 ||


__________________________________________

vātavarṇanam

ādhūtakārtasvaraketuyaṣtirenānyaśokastabakāruṇāni  |
ānartayatyeṣa patākinīnāṃ vātaḥ patākāṃśukapallavāni  || 14.15 ||

nabhaścarāṇāṃ vrajatāmamīṣāmanyonyapīnāṃsavigharṣaṇena  |
ullāsitaḥ kuṅkumapāṃsupūraḥ piśaṅgayatyeṣa diśāṃ mukhāni  || 14.16 ||


__________________________________________

prekṣakastrīvarṇanam

saratnakāñcīvalayairvilāsa siṃhāsanairmanmathapārthivasya  |
ito nitambairasitekṣaṇānāṃ vyāptāntaraṃ vyoma samāptimeti  || 14.17 ||

anyonyasaṅghaṭṭavisūtritāni kumudvatīkāntakaropamāni  |
etāni paśyāmbarataḥ patanti vimānamuktāphalajālakāni  || 14.18 ||

etāḥ prayāntyaḥ purato vimānairvivartya bālā mukhapaṅkajāni  |
apāṅgaviśrāntavilolatāraistvāṃ nātha netrāñjalibhiḥ pibanti  || 14.19 ||

āsāmitaḥ satvaragāminīnāṃ gatāgatābhyāṃ maṇikuṇḍalāni  |
bhindanti vidyādharakāminīnāṃ kapolakālāgurupattrakāni  || 14.20 ||

ito mithaḥ pārśvavighaṭṭiteṣu javādvimāneṣu samāpatatsu  |
āsāmitaḥ preṅkhitamadhyaratna visūtritā hāralatāḥ sphuṭanti  || 14.21 ||

ete gatikṣobhavaśādvadhūnāṃ paśyonmayūkhā maṇikarṇapūrāḥ  |
itastale sañcaratāmahīnāṃ nipatya cūḍāmaṇiṣu svananti  || 14.22 ||

pātālametannayanotsavena vicintya śūnyaṃ śaśalāñchanena  |
ihāṅganābhiḥ svamukhachalena kṛto'mbare candramayīva sṛṣṭiḥ  || 14.23 ||

ito rasaṃ pallavayanti vīraṃ vidyādharāṇāṃ karavālavallyaḥ  |
etāśca śṛṅgāramito'ṅganānāṃ dṛśo navendīvaradāmadīrghās || 14.24 ||
[var14.24 {॰vallyaḥ|em̭ ॰vallayaḥ |ed |unmetrical}]

jhaṭityavāptapratibimbametatsainyaṃ vilokya sphaṭikāṅgaṇeṣu  |
savismayaṃ nāgapurāṅganābhiritaḥ kriyante nayanotpalāni  || 14.25 ||

narendra tasmādgaganāvahagāhi tvaddarśanavyagratapasvipaṅkteḥ  |
dūraṃ havirdhūmasugandhisīmnaḥ sthānādvayaṃ vaṅkumuneḥ pravṛttāḥ  || 14.26 ||


__________________________________________

vanavarṇanam

yathāyamabhyeti puro nabhasvānākṛṣṭanānāvidhapuṣpagandhaḥ  |
agre tathābhraṃlihabhūruhaṃ naḥ śaṅke vanaṃ dṛkpathameṣyatīti  || 14.27 ||

ebhirmahīpāla vimānaratnairagresaraiḥ sambhramamuktamārgaḥ  |
niṣkampaketurmahatāspadena rathastavāyaṃ viyati prayāti  || 14.28 ||
sitaprasūnastabakaistarūṇāmeṣā purastārikāntarikṣā  |
jhaṭityaśeṣaiva nimeṣamātrādunmīlitā paśya vanāntalekhā  || 14.29 ||

yadīśa vidyādharavāhinīyamākāśataḥ kiñcidadhaḥ pravṛttā  |
avaimi citte nihitaṃ tadasyāḥ padaṃ vanālokanakautukena  || 14.30 ||

eṣā tamālāvalinīlakānterupoḍharāmāmukhahemapadmā  |
senā vanasyābhimukhaṃ prayāti paśyāmburāseriva jahnukanyā  || 14.31 ||


asyāḥ kṣamāpāla vanāntarājervimānapaṅktiḥ purataḥsthiteyam  |
ālambate ratnakirīṭalakṣmīṃ maṇiprabhodgīrṇamahendracāpā  || 14.32 ||

ete khalīnakṣataśoṇitāktāḥ paśyendragopaśriyamudvahantaḥ  |
turaṅgalālājalabindavaste luṭhanti hemadrumapallaveṣu  || 14.33 ||

mandānilāndolitapallavāgrāḥ samagrapuṣpāharaṇodyatābhyaḥ  |
ito latāḥ pārthiva saṃrabhante lāvaṇyamādātumivāṅganābhyaḥ  || 14.34 ||

amūni puṣpāṇi mahīruhāṇāmābhānti līnabhramarodarāṇi  |
vidyādharīvibhramadarśanārthamuttānitānīva vilocanāni  || 14.35 ||

paśyāgravātāyanametadetya latāgrapuṣpāṇyacinvatīnām  |
nṛpātibhāreṇa nitambinīnāṃ vimānaratnāni puro namanti  || 14.36 ||

śākhāgralagnāsu mahātarūṇāṃ viṭaṅkaratnadyutiśrṅkhalāsu  |
vilambamānairiva cimbiteyamito vimānairmaṇipañjaraśrīḥ  || 14.37 ||

imāḥ samaṃ prāṇasamairvane'smin sarvaṛturlakṣmīnibiḍopagūḍhe  |
nabhaḥsthitā eva narendra paśya puṣpoccaye loladṛśaḥ pravṛttāḥ  || 14.38 ||

etāḥ karaiḥ kāñcanapadmagaurairvilāsavatyaḥ sanibandhanāni  |
haranti puṣpāṇi mahīruhāṇāṃ mṛdūni yūnāmiva mānasāni  || 14.39 ||

āsāṃ latāgrastabakotthitāni śikhaṇḍaratnadyuticumbitāni  |
paśyopariṣṭādalimaṇḍalāni nīlātapatrabhramamārabhante  || 14.40 ||

muñcatyaliḥ puṣpalatāmito'yamāghrātarāmāmukhapadmagandhaḥ  |
guṇaprakarṣe hi sadā manāṃsi guṇāntarajñāvatāṃ ramante  || 14.41 ||

lataitayā cūtataroḥ salīlamānīyamānā natimānatāṅgyā  |
paśyotpatatpuṣpaśilīmukheyamābhāti kandarpadhanurlateva  || 14.42 ||

bhramaddvirephāvaliloladṛṣṭirlīlāvataṃsakriyayonmukhāni  |
vicetumeṣā kusumāni nālaṃ tarostanāliṅganadohadasya  || 14.43 ||

priyārpitaḥ kāntanipītamukta purandhribimbādharakānticoraḥ  |
asyāḥ saratnatviṣi karṇapāśe kimapyaśokastabakaścakāsti  || 14.44 ||

asyā latākṣepavisūtritasya hārasya lagnastanakuṅkumasya  |
bhraṣṭāḥ kṣitau dāḍimabījamohādākṣipya muktāḥ kapayaḥ kṣipanti  || 14.45 ||

nidāghalakṣmīhasitachaṭeyaṃ paryāptakṛṣṇāgurudhūmagandhe  |
bandhe kacānā navamallikāsyāḥ paśyālinīle paribhāgameti  || 14.46 ||

asāvitaḥ pārthiva dantapatramāpāsya haṃsachadapāṇḍu bālā  |
karoti karṇe navakarṇikāramuttāpitāṣṭāpadaratnaśobhi  || 14.47 ||

sthito'yamanto navapallavānāṃ pravālatāmrāṅgulirāyatākṣyāḥ  |
vyaktiṃ śaraccandrakarojjvalābhirnakhāṃśurekhābhiritaḥ prayāti  || 14.48 ||

dhāvatkarā karṇaśirīṣalobhāditastataḥ sampatati dvirephe  |
krīḍāmiva vyākuladṛṣṭipātā bāleyamabhyasyati kandukasya  || 14.49 ||

prāvṛḍvilāsasmitamudvahantī karṇe navaṃ ketakabarhameṣā  |
priyāvataṃsīkṛtacandralekhā cakāsti kanyeva himācalasya  || 14.50 ||

vilokitālekhyakapolabhāgātplavaṅgaśāve'dhigate vimānam  |
itaḥ kadambādanivṛttahastamāste bhayādālikhiteva tanvī  || 14.51 ||

puṣpāṇi cinvatyatimuktakasya paśya ślathaṃ keśakalāpameṣā  |
aṃse bibhartīva tamālanīlaṃ vilāsavālavyajanaṃ smarasya  || 14.52 ||

vimuñcatī cakṣuṣi puṣpadhūlimeṣā gate taṃ prati vallabhasya  |
citraṃ jhaṭityaśrulavāvakīrṇe dṛśau sapatnyāḥ kaluṣīkaroti  || 14.53 ||

madhyacyute bibhrati kiṃśuke'sminnasrāruṇasyākṛtimaṅkuśasya  |
asyāḥ stanābhyāmadhunā dhṛteyamanaṅgamattadvipakumbhalakṣmīḥ  || 14.54 ||
[var14.54 {asyāḥ|lem |em̭ asyā॰ |ed}]

abhyāgatāyāḥ sahasāmunāsyāḥ kandarpasāmrājyadhurodvahena  |
sugandhibhiḥ pāṭalipādapena puṣpairitaḥ kalpitamātitheyam  || 14.55 ||

ārdrāgaseyaṃ gamitā prasādaṃ kṛtapraṇāmāñjalinā priyeṇa  |
pramṛṣṭabāṣpā sumukhī nakhābhyāṃ cūtāṅkuraṃ mānamivocchinatti  || 14.56 ||

adhyāsate kāmapi kāntimāsāmete sakundāḥ kabarīkalāpāḥ  |
kumudvatīkāntakarānuviddhāstamaḥpratānā iva yāminīnām  || 14.57 ||

puṣpāṇi nānāvidhavarṇabhāñji vimānavātāyanato vicitya  |
yuvāyamindrāyudhavarṇakāntamuttaṃsamasyāḥ sadṛśaḥ karoti  || 14.58 ||

paśyeyamudbāhulatāhitaśrīrmadhyena naśyattrivalī lateva  |
vimānataḥ komalamuccinoti vadhūrmadhūkaṃ svakapolakānti  || 14.59 ||

adaḥ sugandhīkuru matprasūnaṃ mukhābjasaugandhyakaṇārpaṇena  |
ityañcalāsaktalato na yāñcāmasyāḥ karotīva saḥ karṇikāraḥ  || 14.60 ||

tathā na cūte navamañjarīyamavāptavatyātmaguṇaprakarṣam  |
asyāḥ sakālāgarupatralekhe yathā kapole kamalekṣaṇāyāḥ  || 14.61 ||

vahatyaśoko'yamamartyakāntā padāhataḥ kāntamalaktakāṅgam  |
kṛtvā jagatyaskhalitāṃ nijājñāṃ skandhe'rpitaṃ tūṇāmiva smareṇa  || 14.62 ||

cireṇa manye bakuladrumo'yamavāptapāraḥspṛhaṇīyatāyāḥ  |
arthī karo'syāḥ sumanaḥsu yasya jāto jitāmartyatarupravālaḥ  || 14.63 ||

phalaṃ śirīṣeṇa cirādavāptamasyāḥ samagraṃ sukumāratāyāḥ  |
nirasya gīrvāṇataruprasūnāṃ yadetaduttaṃsitamāyātākṣyā  || 14.64 ||

asyāḥ śrutau campakamādadhatyāḥ kareṇa muktāvalayāṅkitena  |
eṣa dvijaḥ svastyayanaṃ virāvairnāsyāḥ karotīti na mañjukaṇṭhaḥ  || 14.65 ||

vinyastabandhūkadalī mukhe'syāḥ svedārdrakālāgarupatravallī  |
cakāsti saṃdhyātapaleśaśaṅki kalaṅkalekhā śaśalakṣmanīva  || 14.66 ||

etā mitho ruddhavimānamārgā latāgrajaṃ puṣpamanāpnuvantyaḥ  |
bhavanti bhārānamitāṅgakeṣu baddhābhyasūyāḥ stanamaṇḍaleṣu  || 14.67 ||

etāni kāntaiḥ pramadājanasya navapravālānyavataṃsitāni  |
paśyanti paśya svamivāptakāntiṃ kapolapālīmaṇidarpaṇeṣu  || 14.68 ||

yadyadvane'bhūtkusumaṃ sugandhi vadhūjanaistattadito gṛhītam  |
kṛtsnatrilokīvijayāvataṃsaṃ nirmātumiṣvāsamiva smarasya  || 14.69 ||

karṇāvataṃsīkṛtapallavānāṃ vicitrapuṣpābharaṇojjvaleṣu  |
paśyābhitaḥ pallaviteyamāsāmaṅgeṣu sāraṅgadṛśāmṛtuśrīḥ  || 14.70 ||

svedodabinduchalataḥ kapole vitīrṇamuktārgha iva smarasya  |
eṇīdṛśāṃ kānta iva pravṛttaḥ śramo'yamāliṅgitumaṅgalekhām  || 14.71 ||

āsāṃ pṛthusvedakaṇāṅkitāni vaktrāṇi netrotsavamarpayanti  |
pratyuptamuktāphaladanturāṇi hiraṇyamayānīva saroruhāṇi  || 14.72 ||

etāni paśya cyutapuṣpadhūli vyāluptapakṣmāvalisauṣṭhavāni  |
śrameṇa kiṃ cinmukulībhavanti sīmantinīnāṃ nayanotpalāni  || 14.73 ||
[var14.73 {sauṣṭhavāni|lem |em̭ sauṣṭavāni |ed}]

etāsu sīdanmaṇibandhamūla nilīnalīlāmaṇikaṅkaṇāsu  |
vilokyatāṃ vyaktimupaiti khedo vidyādharastrījanadorlatāsu  || 14.74 ||

anaṅgakalpadrumamañjarīṇāmamoghamantraṃ kusumāyudhasya  |
bhṛśaṃ yathā puṣpamayī vibhūṣā nāsāṃ tathā ratnamayī vibhāti  || 14.75 ||

vanāntametā vanitā na muktumanekapuṣpaṃ nṛpa śaknuvanti  |
vimucyate kena nāma sa deśo yatrāsti sāndraḥ sumanaḥpracāraḥ  || 14.76 ||

ityujjvale tasya vacaḥprasūne nīte'pi karṇātithitāṃ vihasya  |
nṛpo babhūvāstamanāḥ sacitraṃ smṛtāhirājendrasutāvilāsaḥ  || 14.77 ||

puṣpapravālāṅkuramaṇḍalaṃ tatkvaṇadvimānāvalihemaghaṇṭam  |
