vanaṃ vihāyātha samagramagre cacāla vidyādhararājasainyam  || 14.78 ||


__________________________________________

gaṅgāvarṇanam

atha pravālādharabimbacumbi parisphuratphenavilāsahāsā  |
vācālahaṃsāvalikāñcidāma sanāthatīrorunitambabimbā  || 14.79 ||

samīravellattaṭahemavalli lolaprabhāpiñjaritormilekhā  |
vilāsamajjatphaṇirājakanyā saṅkrāntakāntastanakuṅkumeva  || 14.80 ||

nimagnadiṅnāgakapolabhitti santānaniryanmadaveṇikṛṣṇā  |
tṛtīyanetrānaladhūmavalli kalaṅkiteva tripurāntakasya  || 14.81 ||

bhārānamadbhogiphaṇāsthitāyāḥ sthalocchaladvīcicayacchalena  |
uttambhayantīva tale satarkamarkopalastambhaśatāni bhūmeḥ  || 14.82 ||

taṭodgataprāṃśu tamālarāji chāyāghanaśyāmalitārdhabhāgā  |
mūrtistuṣārācalatulyakāntirumāpatiśrīdharayorivaikā  || 14.83 ||

kādambacañcūddhṛtacakravākī pāriplavavyaktamṛṇālakāṇḍā  |
caṇḍīdhavonnaddhajaṭāviṭaṅka kṛṣṭendulekhāṅkuradantureva  || 14.84 ||

taraṅgabhaṅgojjvalacāmaraśrīruddaṇḍahemāmburuhātapatrā  |
puṇyā puro dūrata eva tena trimārgagādṛśyata pārthivena  || 14.85 ||
[syntax kulaka]

tasyāstaṭe'tha kusumāvacayaśramārta sīmantinīnivahasasmitavīkṣitāyāḥ  |
vidyādhareṇa vidadhe dhavalormidhauta paryantahemasikate pṛtanāniveśaḥ  || 14.86 ||

tadanu puline sadyo vidyādharaiḥ parikalpitaṃ nrpatiraviśallīlāgāraṃ saḥ sāhasalāñchanaḥ  |
bhramamajanayannetrotkīrṇā iti pratibimbitāḥ sitamaṇimaye yasminnanto bahiśca mṛgīdṛśaḥ  || 14.87 ||


iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye pātālāvalokano nāma caturdaśaḥ sargaḥ



************************************************


pañcadaśaḥ sargaḥ

atha sāndragharmajalabindu lulitatilakaḥ pracakrame  |
hemakamalasubhage saritaḥ salile nimaṅktumasitekṣaṇājanaḥ  || 15.1 ||

śramametya muñcata madambu rabhasaparirambhalīlayā  |
evamavadadiva dīrghadṛśaḥ kalahaṃsayūthaninadena jāhnavī  || 15.2 ||

marutāvadhūtakaladhauta kamalamakarandagandhinā  |
ūrmidalanaśiśireṇa balādabalājanasya nunude pariśramaḥ  || 15.3 ||

parividdharekhamadhitīra viracitapade vadhūjane  |
kāntimudavahadamartyasarittaṭarūḍhakāñcanalateva kāṃ cana  || 15.4 ||

sitacakṣuṣāmasitaratna rucimuṣi mṛṇālinīdale  |
sthūlajalalavanibhena dadhe navamauktikārdha iva jāhnukanyayā  || 15.5 ||

uditatrapā iva vilāsa masṛṇagamanena subrhuvām  |
jagmuramarasaritaḥ pulinaṃ sahasā vihāya kalahaṃsapaṅktayaḥ  || 15.6 ||

dhavalābhrakachuritabhitti likhitalalitāṅganālipim  |
apsu kuvalayadṛśāṃ pratimāḥ sitaratnadarpaṇanibhāsu lebhire  || 15.7 ||

atha vallabhārpitakarāgra nihitanijapāṇipallavāḥ  |
vārivikacakanakābjarajaḥ kapiśormimālamabalā jagāhire  || 15.8 ||

sudṛśāṃ nimajjya surasindhu payasi niyamasthitairiva  |
ūrmitaralitamayūkhaśikhairmaṇinūpuraiḥ sapadi maunamādade  || 15.9 ||

kucaluptapatralatayātha vigalitavisūtrahārayā  |
prāpi ratiramaṇavilāsatulā lalitabhruvāṃ jalavihāralīlayā  || 15.10 ||

navakuṅkumāruṇapurandhri kucakalaśatāḍitaṃ payaḥ  |
rāgamabhajata na kasya bhavetsulabhāṅgakheladabalasya vikriyā  || 15.11 ||

vikiran vihasya maṇiśṛṅga dhṛtamudakametya pṛṣṭhataḥ  |
sācimukulitadṛśā dadṛśe dayito muhurvalitakaṇṭhamekayā  || 15.12 ||
[var15.12 {pṛṣṭhataḥ|lem |em̭ pṛṣṭataḥ |ed}]

pariṇāhavatkucaniṣiddha karaṇavalanotkadorlatam  |
lolamukharamaṇikaṅkaṇayā vidadhe jale murajavādyamanyayā  || 15.13 ||

hariṇīdṛśo hṛtapurandhri kucakalaśakuṅkumāṅkitā  |
kṣipramadhijaghanamūrmilatā śithileva hemaraśanā vididyute  || 15.14 ||

salilaṃ kirantamavalokya ramaṇamaparāṅganāmukhe  |
citramaviraladhṛtāśrulave nimimīla kā cidabalā vilocane  || 15.15 ||

drutamatruṭadvibudhasindhu nibiḍalaharīkarāhatam  |
hāravalayamaravindadṛśāṃ kuśalaṃ kuto'sti guṇināṃ jaḍāntare  || 15.16 ||

śuśubhe kayāpi karayantra nihitaśucivāridhārayā  |
muktasitakusumasāyakayā makarāṅkacāpalatayeva bālayā  || 15.17 ||

pariveṣavibhramaniveśi manasijatuṣāradīdhiteḥ  |
ko'pi karakisalaye kamitā vidadhe mṛṇālavalayaṃ mṛgīdṛśaḥ  || 15.18 ||

hṛdayeśamuktajaladhauta malayajavilepanairhriyā  |
lakṣyanavanakhapadāvaparā nalinīdalena pidadhe payodharau  || 15.19 ||

abhito'ṅganāmukhamṛgāṅkamuḍunikarakāntimādade  |
kelitaralataruṇīnibiḍa stanatāḍite payasi budbudotakaraḥ  || 15.20 ||

vidadhe vadhūrnijakarāgra galitamaṇikaṅkaṇāṅkite  |
meghaśakala iva sendradhanurvalayodare nayanamabjinīdale  || 15.21 ||

asitāñjanāṅkamabhijāta kuvalayadṛśāṃ dṛśo'ntarāt |
nīramahṛta na hi śuddhimatāṃ taṭavṛttirāśritakalaṅkamārjane  || 15.22 ||

taralormilaṅghitanitamba phalakalulitācchavāsaḥ  |
phenapaṭalamasitābjadṛśaḥ kṣaṇamekamaṃśukavilāsamādade  || 15.23 ||

sudṛśaḥ sphuṭaṃ navanakhāṅkamurasi jaladhatakuṅkume  |
kāntanihitanayanaṃ pidadhe pṛthuhāratārataralāṃśupallavaḥ  || 15.24 ||

lulitāñjanasya nayanasya sahabhuvamivekṣituṃ śriyam  |
lagnajalasaralitairalakairluluṭhe lalāṭataṭasīmni subhruvaḥ  || 15.25 ||

dadhatīva kāpi rucimāpa manasijavilāsavallakīm  |
aṃsavinihitamṛṇālalatā talavartyalābuparivartulastanī  || 15.26 ||

salilāhatitruṭitahāra parigalitamauktikabhramāt |
mugdhayuvatiraparā nidadhe dṛśamabjinīdalapuṭodabinduṣu  || 15.27 ||

dhavalodarairbhayavaśena taralavalitairvilocanaiḥ  |
nīlanalinadaladāmadṛśāṃ śapharaiḥ samaṃ suciramapsu babhrame  || 15.28 ||

galitāṅgadā gurutaraṅga dalitamaṇikarṇapūrakāḥ  |
srastakanakavalayāḥ sudṛśaḥ ślathabandharatnaraśanāścakāśire  || 15.29 ||

atikampamūrmikuṭilabhru bahalanavakuṅkumāruṇam  |
straiṇakaratalanirastamabhūdupajātakopamiva jāhnavījalam  || 15.30 ||

akarotpadaṃ pṛthunitamba vilulitajalārdraveṇiṣu  |
nīralavaśabalagaṇḍatalāsvabalāsu kuṇḍalitakārmukaḥ smaraḥ  || 15.31 ||

cyutaratnabhūṣaṇamarīci racitasuracāpamaṇḍalam  |
keśakusumaśabalaṃ śuśubhe sudṛśāmanaṅgamadadīpanaṃ payaḥ  || 15.32 ||

atha tābhirekatapanīya sarasijasanāthapāṇibhiḥ  |
śrībhiriva samudatāri tataḥ punaruktacāṭupaṭubhiḥ priyaiḥ saha  || 15.33 ||

sajalāṃśukāntiviśadāṅga janitanibiḍatrapākulāḥ  |
vyaktanavanakhapadābharaṇāḥ priyalocanānyaramayanmṛgīdṛśaḥ  || 15.34 ||

atha tā gṛhītaśaradindu dhavalasicayāḥ prasādhanām  |
cakruradhivapuramartyasaritpulinasthalīkanakavallidhāmasu  || 15.35 ||

tilakāṅkitāḥ pracurapuṣpa parimalamuco mṛgīdṛśaḥ  |
ūḍhanavalalitapatralatā dadhuraṅgajopavanarājivibhramam  || 15.36 ||

na cirādalaṅkṛtiviśeṣa vihitarucayo'tipeśalāḥ  |
lakṣyanavanavarasāḥ śucayaḥ kavipuṅgavoktaya ivāṅganā babhuḥ  || 15.37 ||

vahati sma nirmalakapola likhitamṛganābhipatrakam  |
kāpi nibhṛtamadhupāvalimatkaladhautakokanadakāntamānanam  || 15.38 ||

adhikarṇamudgatamayūkha marakatamanoharodaram  |
bālamiva madanakalpatarordalamekayā kanakapatramādade  || 15.39 ||

dhṛtayāvakāṅkamapi kānta vadanaparicumbanocite  |
rāgamadhurarucake nidadhe dayite ca kā cana cakoralocanā  || 15.40 ||

hariṇāṅkasundaramaneka viśadaguṇagumphitākṛtim  |
hāramavahadaparā na paraṃ hṛdi kāntamapyasamabāṇadīpanam  || 15.41 ||

itarā padāṅkamiva matta madanakariṇo'likodare  |
sāndramṛgamadanamaṣīracitaṃ tilakaṃ kuraṅgatilakānanā dadhe  || 15.42 ||

hṛtasuptahemaśatapatra rucimukulitāṅgulīdale  |
kā ciditarakarasaṅghaṭitaṃ nidadhe vilāsamaṇikaṅkaṇaṃ kare  || 15.43 ||

uditāsu kāñciguṇamadhya marakatamarīcisūciṣu  |
vyaktimalabhata na romalatā smaradīpakajjalaśikhā mṛgīdṛśām  || 15.44 ||

sarasāgaseva ramaṇena caraṇanalinānuṣaṅgiṇā  |
puṣpaśarataralitā dadhire maṇinūpureṇa mṛgalocanā rucim  || 15.45 ||

dadhatībhirindukarajāla śabalatimirormivibhramam  |
ūḍhadhavalakusumābhiralaṃ kabarīlatābhirabalāścakāśire  || 15.46 ||

priyapāṇipaṅkajadhṛteṣu sakalaśaśibimbabandhuṣu  |
vaktrakamalamaviloladṛśo maṇidarpaṇeṣu dadṛśuḥ purandhrayaḥ  || 15.47 ||

prakaṭaṃ dadhajjhaṭiti rāgamalaghumatha vāruṇīṃ prati  |
kāntinikara iva tigmarucervanitājano'bhimukhatāṃ samādade  || 15.48 ||

udiyāya mānatimiraugha vighaṭanapaṭurmano'mbare  |
bālakuvalayadṛśāṃ viśade śanakairmanobhuvakuraṅgalāñchanaḥ  || 15.49 ||

aruṇāṅgulīdalaniruddha navakanakakośakarṇikaiḥ  |
sāndramadhuparimalaī ruruce karapaṅkajairatha cakoracakṣuṣām  || 15.50 ||

maṇiśuktiṣu kṣaṇamupoḍha kuvalayasugandhisīdhuṣu  |
nyasyadasamaśarayantramiva bhramati sma bhṛṅgakulamutsukotsukam  || 15.51 ||
[15.51 śleṣa]

ruruce'limaṇḍalamudaṃśu kamalacaṣakopari sthitam  |
ekamiva ratipateruditaṃ tapanīyadaṇḍamasitoṣṇavāraṇam  || 15.52 ||
[var15.52 {kamala॰|lem |em̭ kamaka॰ |ed}]

vidhṛtā kare priyatamena katipayaśilīmukhānvitā  |
cittamakṛta vivaśaṃ sudṛśāṃ kusumeṣukārmukalateva vāruṇī  || 15.53 ||
[15.53 śleṣa]

atirāgiṇi praṇayiṇīva madhuni paricumbitānane  |
vyaktimalabhata mado hṛdaye pulakaḥ kapolaphalake ca subhruvām  || 15.54 ||

pratimāgatendukarajāla dhavalitamiva śriyaṃ dadhe  |
pānanamitamukhamugdhavadhū vigaladvilāsahasitachaṭaṃ madhu  || 15.55 ||

madhu kāpi pāṭalakapola taralakaladhautakuṇḍalā  |
lolanijamukhatuṣārakara pratibimbagarbhamabhikārpitaṃ papau  || 15.56 ||

atiraktamāyatatareṇa vilasadasitotpalaśriyā  |
kāmamapibadaparendumukhī caṣakeṇa sīdhu, nayanena vallabham  || 15.57 ||

agamattrayasya samameva parimalarasajñatāmaliḥ  |
kalpaviṭapimadhunaḥ pramadā mukhamārutasya vikacotpalasya  || 15.58 ||

adharāgracumbanamavāpya kamaladaladīrghacakṣuṣām  |
śvāsataralamajaniṣṭa mudā muhurāttalāsyamiva kāpiśāyanam  || 15.59 ||

madamāsavena rameṇa nakhapadamivārpitaṃ hṛdi  |
nātiviśadapadabandhamatho navasāhasāṅkacaritaṃ jaguḥ striyaḥ  || 15.60 ||

madirārpitādharadalena yuvatiramṛtachaṭopamā  |
premarasamṛduṣu dīrghadṛśāṃ hṛdayeṣu pallavayati sma manmatham  || 15.61 ||

dayitāhite yuvajanena vilasadatipāṭaladyutau  |
kāmasuhṛdi vihitaḥ praṇayaḥ prathamaṃ madhunyadharapallave tataḥ  || 15.62 ||

avadhūtamānamadhupāna rasarabhasataḥ prasannatām  |
prāpuratipṛthudṛśāṃ sahasā hṛdayāni cācchamaṇibhājanāni ca  || 15.63 ||

dayitāmanaṅgamadasūtimasitanalināvataṃsakām  |
sāndraruciyuvajano madirāṃ paricumbati sma na paraṃ vadhūmapi  || 15.64 ||

anayan sahaiva śithilatvamubhayamubhayena yoṣitām  |
vrīḍamaruṇarucinā madhunā maṇikāñcidāma ca kareṇa kāminaḥ  || 15.65 ||

sudṛśāṃ madādatha parisrudaruṇitasitodarāḥ karāt |
peturamarasaritaḥ puline maṇiśuktayaḥ priyatame ca dṛṣṭayaḥ  || 15.66 ||

navapallavāruṇamuvāha dhṛtataruṇavāruṇīmadaḥ  |
rāgamatha śucini gaṇḍatale nayanodare ca madirekṣanājanaḥ  || 15.67 ||

aparisphuṭoktilalitāsu madamukuladīrghadṛṣṭiṣu  |
tāsu śabalakusumāṃsalasatkabarīlatāsu padamādadhe smaraḥ  || 15.68 ||

āpānabhūravasitāsavarāgadīrgha sendīvarasphaṭikaśuktiśatā cakāse  |
kautūhalānmadavilolavadhūvilāsa vyālokanonmiṣitavaktraparampareva  || 15.69 ||

aviralamaṇidīpoddyotadūrāpasarpattimiramamarasindhoḥ kūlakacchaṃ praviśya  |
atha samamasunāthaistāḥ salīlaṃ niṣeduḥ sarasakanakajambūpallavaprastareṣu  || 15.70 ||

sāndronmīlatsaurabhāṇyudvahantyo vidyullekhapiṅgalānyaṅkāni  |
lakṣmīṃ hemāmbhojamālā ivāpuḥ sāraṅgākṣyaḥ preyasāṃ kaṇṭhalagnāḥ  || 15.71 ||

tato visūtracyutahārayaṣṭiḥ śīrṇāṅgado bhaṅgurakarṇapūraḥ  |
madena sadyo madirekṣaṇānāṃ ratotsavaḥ pallavito babhūva  || 15.72 ||

nānāṅgarāgaśabale ślathabandhalola dhammillamālyakusumaprakarāvakīrṇe  |
puṣpeṣutalpe iva vakṣasi vallabhānāṃ nidrāmavāpuratha tāḥ surataśrameṇa  || 15.73 ||

kandarpasya trilokīhaṭhavijayamahāsāhasotsāhaheturdhunvaṃstatpakṣmadhūlivyatikarakapiśaḥ kāñcanāmbhoruhāṇi  |
tanvānastīrarūḍhatridaśatarulatālāsyamālasyabhājāṃ tāsāṃ saṃbhogakeliklamabharamaharajjāhnavīvīcivātaḥ  || 15.74 ||


iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye pātālagaṅgāvagāhano nāma pañcadaśaḥ sargaḥ



************************************************


ṣoḍaśaḥ sargaḥ

atrāntare nabhaḥsindhu pulinaṃ prāpa pāṭalā  |
dadhatyanaṅgalekhena lāñchitaṃ pāṇipallavam  || 16.1 ||

maṇipragrīvakodgīrṇa śunāsīraśarāsanam  |
apaśyadgaganollekhi tatra sphaṭikaveśma sā  || 16.2 ||

tadindranīladvāre sā hemavetrākule babhau  |
śliṣyantī sataḍillekhe lekhevendoḥ payomuci  || 16.3 ||

ratnavātāyanasthasya tiṣṭhan pārśve mahīpateḥ  |
dvāḥsthaṃ kimapi pṛcchantīṃ tāmaikṣata ramāṅgadaḥ  || 16.4 ||

antaḥ praveśayāmāsa saśca tāmāttasaṃbhramaḥ  |
yayau sā ca viśāṃpatyurlocanāmṛtavartitām  || 16.5 ||

sitāśmaharmyamuttuṅgaṃ jhaṭityadhiruroha sā  |
ānandakandalīcittamatyacchaṃ pārthivasya ca  || 16.6 ||

kṣiticumbitahāraṃ sā praṇanāma mahībhuje  |
ramāṅgadaṃ karagrāha sauhārdye pātratāṃ nayat || 16.7 ||

nṛpeṇa svayamuktāpi ita ehyehi pāṭale  |
tallīlāmaṇiparyaṅka savidhe niṣasāda sā  || 16.8 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalāvākyam

kṛtasambhāṣaṇā sātha tenāvanimanobhuvā  |
ityavocata dantāṃśu śabalādharapallavā  || 16.9 ||

mudritasmarasaundarya vāntā netrāmṛtachaṭā  |
iyaṃ mūrtirmahārāja kaccitkuśalinī tava ?  || 16.10 ||

kathametāṃ praviṣṭāstvaṃ bhūmimatyantadurgamām  |
na kasya vismayāyeyaṃ śaktiratyadbhutā tava  || 16.11 ||

deva vidyādharaḥ ko'pi śāpānnirmocitastvayā  |
ityamandaḥ pravādo'tra tatko'pyasi namo'stu te  || 16.12 ||

aho guṇena rājendra yena kenāpi gṛhyase  |
yadevaṃ kliśyase tasyāḥ phaṇīndraduhituḥ kṛte  || 16.13 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavākyam

atha vidrumatāmrauṣṭha luṭhaddaśanadīdhitiḥ  |
smitvā vasumatīnāthastāmiti pratyabhāṣata  || 16.14 ||

kadācitpāṭale kaccidbhujaṅgapatikanyakā  |
smaratyasmān sakhīsvaira saṅkathāsvantarāntarā  || 16.15 ||

kimanaṅgavatī vakti brūte mālyavatī ca kim  |
apyastyahipure'smākaṃ kiṃvadantī kiyatyapi  || 16.16 ||

phaṇirājasutākarṇaṃ kaccitprāpayya madvacaḥ  |
vihito ratnacūḍena śukarūpaviparyayaḥ  || 16.17 ||

kimāgatāsi kiṃ caivaṃ parimlāneva lakṣyase  |
nivedaya drutaṃ yanme kimapyāśaṅkate manaḥ  || 16.18 ||


__________________________________________

lekhārpaṇam

iti pṛṣṭavate tasmai sāndrapremārdracetase  |
kandarpadīpanaṃ lekhamarpayāmāsa pāṭalā  || 16.19 ||

tamāttaṃ vācayetyuktvā paramārakulodvahaḥ  |
ramāṅgadasya cikṣepa sambhramottānite kare  || 16.20 ||

taṃ hemakadalīpatra kastūrilikhitākṣaram  |
so'vadhāryātha tasyāgre vyaktavāgityavāacayat || 16.21 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhālekhaḥ

svasti sthitasya svaḥsindhu tīre phaṇipurasthayā  |
idaṃ madhyamalokendormālyavatyā nivedyate  || 16.22 ||

yadaivāsmatsakhī vindhye tvayā rājan vyayujyata  |
tadaiveyaṃ kuraṅgīva viddhā hṛdi manobhuvā  || 16.23 ||

dṛṣṭiḥ sarvatra rājendoḥ sudhāniṣyandinī tava  |
jātā śaśiprabhāyāṃ tu saiva hālāhalachaṭā  || 16.24 ||

na vinodayituṃ śakyameṣā kenāpi vastunā  |
vineśa tava karpūra śītayā saṅgamāśayā  || 16.25 ||

tvaddarśanopakāriṇyāḥ karotyekāvalīmiva  |
iyaṃ pakṣmāgravartyaśru pṛṣatachadmanā dṛśaḥ  || 16.26 ||

vakti vyaktāśrulekhena nihitena karodare  |
iyamāpāṇḍugaṇḍena smaratāpamukhendunā  || 16.27 ||

śirīṣādapi mṛdvaṅgī kveyamāyātalocanā  |
eṣa kva ca kukūlāgni karkaśo madanajvaraḥ  || 16.28 ||

neyaṃ pravālaśayyāyāṃ nāpi prāleyaveśmani  |
na cendumaṇiparyaṅke sakhī nirvṛtimeti naḥ  || 16.29 ||

nisargaraktametasyāḥ sakhījanamivādharam  |
nayanti kimapi mlānimuṣṇā niḥśvasitormayaḥ  || 16.30 ||

aratitvamavāpyāmbhaḥ kaṇikeva vighūrṇate  |
eṣā kamalinīpatra śayyāyāmāyatekṣaṇā  || 16.31 ||

asyāḥ smarāgnisantaptaṃ vapuḥ śaśikalāmṛdu  |
nīrandhragharmasalila chaleneva vilīyate  || 16.32 ||

kṛśatāmaṅgake gāḍhamasyāḥ kusumakomale  |
āropayati puṣpeṣu maurvīmiva śarāsane  || 16.33 ||

dhṛtayā hṛdi bāleyaṃ vitīrṇaharicandane  |
nirvāṇameti bhavataḥ kathayā na jalārdrayā  || 16.34 ||

ākarṇakṛṣṭakodaṇḍastvāṃ vinā niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ  |
bhinattyaṅgamanaṅgo'syāḥ kṣitiścetasi kāpi me  || 16.35 ||

iyamatyacchahṛdaye dadhatyatisuvṛttatām  |
bālā māṇikyamukure vimukhī saṃmukhī tvayi  || 16.36 ||

eṣā śikheva dīpasya mugdhā dagdhadaśāśrayā  |
smarānilaparāmarśāditaścetaśca vepate  || 16.37 ||

iyamindudyutiharaṃ yuktamatyāyatairguṇaiḥ  |
mṛṇālavalayaṃ haste vahati tvāṃ ca cetasi  || 16.38 ||

anaṅgatāpavatyasyā nikāmasarasaṃ hṛdi  |
saṅkucatyabjinīpatraṃ na tu tvatpremapallavaḥ  || 16.39 ||

kriyate valayenāsyā maṇibandhe gatāgatam  |
kārśyāgrabhūmimāptāyāstvayyantaḥkaraṇena ca  || 16.40 ||

jāyate peśalamapi prāyo vastvanyathāpadi  |
prāpto mṛṇālahāro'pi yadasyā dāhahetutām  || 16.41 ||

nihitāḥ sāsramālībhirlavalīpākapāṇḍunī  |
asyāḥ stanataṭe bhāraḥ paraṃ vānīrapallavāḥ  || 16.42 ||

na candanena nośīra vāriṇā na jalārdrayā  |
nāsyāḥ puṭakinīpatraiḥ śamameti smarajvaraḥ  || 16.43 ||

kiṃ cāparaṃ tvametasyā hṛdayasyādhidevatā  |
yatastvanmayamevaiṣā viśvaṃ viśveśa paśyati  || 16.44 ||

ratnacūḍopanītena sandeśena tavādhunā  |
iyamucchvasitā kiṃ citsudhāgaṇḍūṣabandhunā  || 16.45 ||

tāvadāgaccha vegena gṛhītvā hemapaṅkajam  |
anaṅgavidhunā yāvadiyaṃ śvasiti naḥ sakhī  || 16.46 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakasya pratisandeśaḥ

evamākarṇya lalitaṃ lekhārthamatha pārthivaḥ  |
udbhinnasāndrapulakaḥ pāṭalāmityavocata  || 16.47 ||


yathā sakhī vaḥ kimapi prapannā vidhurāṃ daśām  |
tathā tvamapi māmevaṃ pāṭale kiṃ na paśyasi ?  || 16.48 ||

tadgaccha tāṃ śaśimukhīmāśvāsayitumarhasi  |
vayamete ca hemābjamānetuṃ prayatāmahe  || 16.49 ||

tathā kāryaṃ na vandhyaḥ syādyathā mama manorathaḥ  |
vaktavyā mālyavatyaivaṃ madgirā valguvādini  || 16.50 ||


__________________________________________

pāṭalāprayāṇam

āvṛttidattasandeśā sā tataḥ pṛthivībhujā  |
phaṇirājendranagarīṃ tvaritaṃ pāṭalā yayau  || 16.51 ||

prasthānaśaṃsī sahasā tasyātha pṛthivīpateḥ  |
pralayāmbudharadhvāna dhīraṃ dadhvāna dundubhiḥ  || 16.52 ||


__________________________________________

vidyādharastrīpuruṣavarṇanam

nadannutkampimanasāṃ sa vidyādharayoṣitām  |
ninye priyapariṣvaṅga ślathatāmatha dorlatāḥ  || 16.53 ||

kathaṃ citprāṇanāthasya mukhādharapallavam  |
bālā vyaghaṭayatkā citkācidurasaśca payodharau  || 16.54 ||

śanairbabandha jaghane nakhāṅkavati mekhalām  |
kāpi smitamukhī kānte tiryagarpitalocanā  || 16.55 ||

dṛṣṭiṃ dāsyanti me sakhyaḥ khaṇḍite dantavāsasi  |
kimatra kṛtyamityanyā kimapi vyākulābhavat || 16.56 ||

kā citparyaṅkamālokya sālaktakapadāṅkitam  |
anyonyārpitanetrābhirvayasyābhirahasyata  || 16.57 ||

ekā māṇikyakaṭakaṃ karāgracyutamādade  |
hṛdayaṃ puṣpacāpena madhye viddhamivātmanaḥ  || 16.58 ||

cumbanakliṣṭabimbauṣṭhaṃ jāgarāruṇalocanam  |
mukhāmbhojaṃ dṛśānyonaṃ vilāsibhirapīyata  || 16.59 ||


__________________________________________

ratnavatīṃ prati gamanam

so'tha pravavṛte gantuṃ vidyādharabalānvitaḥ  |
rathena rathināmagryaḥ purīṃ ratnavatīṃ prati  || 16.60 ||


__________________________________________

ratnacūḍasamāgamaḥ

tataḥ phaṇikumārena ratnacūḍena sa adhvani  |
samagaṃsta jagatpūjyaḥ pūṣā darśa ivendunā  || 16.61 ||
[16.16 ādarśe?]

sastasyopayānīcakre dikcakraskhalitadhvanim  |
acchinnadānaniṣyandamātmānamiva vāraṇam  || 16.62 ||

nṛpasya dīpikākṛtyaṃ cakrire timiracchidaḥ  |
puraḥ prasarpattatsainya phaṇāratnāṃśusūcayaḥ  || 16.63 ||

kiyatāpyatha kālena santataiḥ saḥ prayāṇakaiḥ  |
prapede kusumāśleṣa sugandhipavanaṃ vanam  || 16.64 ||


__________________________________________

dūtaprasthāpanam

asurādhipatiṃ sāmnā yācituṃ hemapaṅkajam  |
sthitvā sastatra purato visasarja ramāṅgadam  || 16.65 ||

saśca trijagataḥ sāramādāyaiva vinirmitām  |
uttuṅgaratnaprāsādāṃ prāpa ratnavatīṃ purīm  || 16.66 ||

rāmārthabaddhakakṣeṇa preṣitaḥ prabhuṇāviśat |
parikhārṇavamullaṅghya sastāṃ laṅkāmivāṅgadaḥ  || 16.67 ||

ko'yaṃ ko'pyayamanyonyamevaṃ pallavitoktibhiḥ  |
kautukastimitākṣaiḥ saḥ pauraiściramadṛśyata  || 16.68 ||

sa candranīlaharmyeṣu dadarśāsurakanyakāḥ  |
lolāstamālaśyāmeṣu megheṣviva śatahradāḥ  || 16.69 ||

nipīyamānaḥ paurastrī netrasphaṭikaśuktibhiḥ  |
vairidvipaghaṭāsiṃhaḥ siṃhadvāramavāpa sa  || 16.70 ||

asurendrasya doḥkaṇḍu duḥsthānaikabhaṭākulam  |
vīraḥ sa aviśadāsthānamatha dvāḥsthaniveditaḥ  || 16.71 ||


__________________________________________

asurendravarṇanam

āsīnamañjanaśyāmamuccaiḥ sphaṭikaviṣṭare  |
himācalendraśikhare navīnamiva nīradam  || 16.72 ||

keyūrapadmarāgāṃśu mañjarījaṭilaūbhau  |
bhujau bibhrāṇamujjvāla pratāpajvalanāviva  || 16.73 ||

urasā ruddhagīrvāṇa dvipendraradakoṭinā  |
hāravallīṃ dadhallolāṃ dolāmiva jayaśriyaḥ  || 16.74 ||

dadhānaṃ dīptiparyasta timirau maṇikuṇḍalau  |
bandīkṛtau sahaivobhau sūryācandramasāviva  || 16.75 ||

vīravratasyālaṅkāramahaṅkārasya jīvitam  |
jagatāmaṅkuśaṃ tatra sa vajrāṅkuśamaikṣata  || 16.76 ||

tannideśitamadhyāsta sa hiraṇyamayamāsanam  |
nānāratnāṃśuśabalaṃ śṛṅgaṃ merorivāryamā  || 16.77 ||


__________________________________________

dūtaṃ prati praśnaḥ

kurvanmukhāni smerāṇi daśanajyotsnayā diśām  |
vidhāya satkriyāmevamasurendrastamabravīt || 16.78 ||

aho kimapi kalyāṇamāsannaphalamadya naḥ  |
anyathā hi gṛhaṃ santaḥ kimāyānti bhavadvidhāḥ  || 16.79 ||

yadāśrame vaṅkumuneryuvayorvṛttamadbhutam  |
tatkarṇātithitāṃ nītametya praṇidhibhirmama  || 16.80 ||

etayā sāṃprataṃ brūhi yuto vidyādharairayam  |
kimākaṅkṣati vaḥ svāmī phalaṃ pāṭālayātrayā  || 16.81 ||

niyujyante nṛpeṇārthe nālpīyasi bhavaddṛśāḥ  |
śeṣo dhṛterbhuvo'nyatra vyāpārayati kiṃ phaṇān  || 16.82 ||

tadatra prahito rājñā kimarthamasi kathyatām  |
arthināṃ vyarthatāmeti na jātu prārthanā mama  || 16.83 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadoktiḥ

mārgairivāyatairvācāṃ śucibhirdaśanāṃśubhiḥ  |
sīmantitādharaḥ smitvā tamityūce ramāṅgadaḥ  || 16.84 ||

āsāṃ sudhārasārdrāṇāṃ śuddhānāmasurādhipa  |
girāṃ tvameko yatsatyamindurbhāsāmivākaraḥ  || 16.85 ||

kaccittvayāyamajñāyi devaḥ sāhasalāñchanaḥ  |
jagatpradīpamathavā ko na vetti virocanam  || 16.86 ||

phaṇirājasutāmeṣa pariṇetuṃ śaśiprabhām  |
pathānena rathakṣuṇṇa ratnaśailena gacchati  || 16.87 ||

tvadvāpihemapadmena śulkasaṃsthā kṛtā kila  |
asyāḥ pitreti devena tadarthaṃ prahitā vayam  || 16.88 ||

anenecchasi cetkartuṃ sakhyaṃ sāhasalakṣmaṇā  |
tatkārtasvararājīvamānīya svayamarpyatām  || 16.89 ||

lumpanti tvanmukhacchāyāṃ yāvannāsya camūrajaḥ  |
kuruṣva tāvadātithyaṃ hemābjena mahībhuje  || 16.90 ||

kimanyan ’’nārthināṃ matto vighaṭante manorathāḥ" |
iti tvayā svamevāśu pramāṇīkriyatāṃ vacaḥ  || 16.91 ||


__________________________________________

vajrāṅkuśavākyam

ityuktavati sāmarṣa bhaṭadṛṣṭe yaśobhaṭe  |
pratyabhāṣata sāvajñaṃ vihasyāsurakuñjaraḥ  || 16.92 ||

aho bata vidagdho'pi dhiṅmugdha iva lakṣyase  |
viduṣāpi tvayā kiṃcidyaduktamasamañjasam  || 16.93 ||


yūyaṃ kva māṇuṣāḥ pṛthvī saṅkaṭasvāmyaduḥsthitāḥ  |
sā ca trijagatāṃ bharturucitā kva śaśiprabhā  || 16.94 ||

tādṛṅmadaṅkamevātra strīratnamadhirohati  |
ramate hi harasyaiva maulāvindukalāṅkuraḥ  || 16.95 ||

yadyadastīha pātāle ratnaṃ kva cana kiṃ cana  |
bhājanaṃ tasya tasyāhamekaḥ ke yūyamucyatām ?  || 16.96 ||

sahate nṛpatau naiva hematāmarasārpaṇam  |
mamaiṣa śakravijaya krīḍādurlalito bhujaḥ  || 16.97 ||

prabhustava nayajño'pi kimanarthāya kevalam  |
dīrghendīvaramāleti vikarṣatyasitoragam  || 16.98 ||

ita eva nivartadhvaṃ vartadhvaṃ vacane mama  |
avaṭe kimiti kṣeptumātmānaṃ yūyamudyatāḥ  || 16.99 ||

yāvadete na muñcanti maryādāmasurābdhayaḥ  |
tāvatdūrāpasāreṇa svāminaṃ trātumarhatha  || 16.100 ||

narendraṃ durjigīṣātastadgaccha vinivāraya  |
tvādṛśāḥ kimupekṣante patimutpathagāminam  || 16.101 ||

athavāsyāsti śaktiścetkimadyāpi vilambate  |
āyātu svayamādātumitaḥ kāñcanapaṅkajam  || 16.102 ||

kimanyajjāyatāmeṣa khaḍgadhārātithirmama  |
ityuktvā kopataralaṃ virarāmāsureśvaraḥ  || 16.103 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadasya prativacanam

dhairyaṃ saṃvṛtakopo'tha smayamāno ramāṅgadaḥ  |
tamityamuktaparyaṅkaḥ prativaktuṃ pracakrame  || 16.104 ||

nirvāṇavīryavāte'smin rasātalabilodare  |
abhugnabhujakaṇḍūtiraho kimapi dṛpyase  || 16.105 ||

manye tavaitannīrandhramajñātasvaparāntare  |
pātālacirasaṃvāsāccitte pariṇataṃ tamaḥ  || 16.106 ||

yāṃ harasyāṣṭamīmāhurmūrtimāhutilehinīm  |
tatsūtiḥ prāgabhūdbhartā paramāra iti kṣiteḥ  || 16.107 ||

kṛtāvatāraṃ tadvaṃśe vadhāya vibudhadviṣām  |
kimādidevaṃ kaṃsāriṃ dhiṅmartya iti manyase  || 16.108 ||
[16.108 sandānitakam]

kamaleva mukundasya pārvatīva pinākinaḥ  |
phaṇikanyocitā patnī sā mahīmīnalakṣmaṇaḥ  || 16.109 ||

tāṃ haṭhenātmasātkartumasurendra na śakyase  |
na ratnasūcimākraṣṭumayaskāntasya yogyatā  || 16.110 ||

padaṃ pathi nidhatse'tra kimanyāyamalīyase  |
apathe caratāṃ yānti dūrāddūraṃ vibhūtayaḥ  || 16.111 ||

anarpaṇaṃ mahīpāle hemapaṅkeruhasya yat |
tavāparighamutprekṣe tadeva dvāramāpadām  || 16.112 ||

rājendradīpake tasmin samarāṅgaṇavartini  |
ete bhaṭāste na cirātāyāsyanti pataṅgatām  || 16.113 ||

tathā vidhehi na yathā tvandataḥpurayoṣitām  |
karapallavaśayyāsu śerate vadanendavaḥ  || 16.114 ||

pravṛttapatiśokārta paurastrīparidevanā  |
iyamutsannasaṅgītā mā bhūdratnavatī purī  || 16.115 ||

devasyārpitahemābjastadehi pata pādayoḥ  |
śiraḥ punantu te kāmaṃ puṇyāstatpādapāṃsavaḥ  || 16.116 ||

athavā subhaṭaiḥ sārdhamuttiṣṭha purato bhava  |
samaṃ vidyādharānīkairayamāyāti bhūpatiḥ  || 16.117 ||

kimanyatsamametena śirastāmarasena te  |
hemakokanadaṃ devaḥ svayameva grahīṣyati  || 16.118 ||


__________________________________________

ramāṅgadāgamanam

tamityuktvā sabhāmadhyānnirjagāma ramāṅgadaḥ  |
nikaṭaṃ ca raṇotkasya jagāma jagatīpateḥ  || 16.119 ||

atha vadati śanaiḥ sametya tasminnamarariporvacanāni tānitāni  |
asurapativināśakālarātriṃ bhṛkuṭīmuvāha mukhena mālavendraḥ  || 16.120 ||

gatvā vidyādharabhaṭacamūcakravālaiḥ sabhaṃ saḥ kṣmāpālo'tha vyadhita paritastatpuro ratnavatyāḥ  |
yena vyaktāmarajayamahāsāhasasyāsurāṇāṃ nāthasyāsīnnavaparibhavaśyāmalāvaktralakṣmīḥ  || 16.121 ||

iti śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye kanakāravindaprārthano nāma ṣoḍaśaḥ sargaḥ



************************************************


saptadaśaḥ sargaḥ


yuddhavarṇanaṃ

athāhividyādhararājasainyairnipīḍitāyāṃ puri ratnavatyām  |
bhaṭāḥ sakopaṃ bhujadarpabhājo nirjagmurākṛṣṭakṛpāṇapaṭṭāḥ  || 17.1 ||

anyonyasaṅghaṭṭavaśena teṣāmudañcitāḥ kāñcanakaṅkaṭebhyaḥ  |
yugāntakālānalabījaśaṅkāṃ na kasya cakruḥ śikhinaḥ sphulliṅgāḥ  || 17.2 ||

bhāleṣu bhīmā bhṛkuṭīrvahanto bhṛṅgatviṣaścāsilatāḥ kareṣu  |
khurāgrarugṇāvanibhiḥ saroṣaṃ nirīyuranye caturaisturaṅgaiḥ  || 17.3 ||

nyabhādanīkaṃ kariṇāṃ ca sāndra sindūrapūrāruṇagaṇḍabhitti  |
pratyagradāvānalalīḍhakoṭi kulācalendrapracayopameyam  || 17.4 ||

puro raṇotsekabhṛtāṃ niruddhamoghena vidyādharavāhinīnām  |
tatpuñjitaṃ sainyamamartyaśatrorbabhūva mūle maṇitoraṇasya  || 17.5 ||

sāṭopamāropitacāpayāṣṭirnibaddhatūṇīrayugo gajasthaḥ  |
so nirjagāmātha mahāsurendro vegādahaṃkāra ivāttadehaḥ  || 17.6 ||

yugātyayāmbhodharanādadhīrastasyodgataḥ saṅgaratūryaghoṣaḥ  |
baddhapratiśrunti nitāntabhīmaścakāra pātālabilodarāṇi  || 17.7 ||

satpuṣkarānañjanapuñjabhāsaspatho jayasyātha gajānasīṃśca  |
vyāpārayanti sma rayeṇa vīrāstatpreritā vairivarūthinīṣu  || 17.8 ||

parasparāpātikṛpāṇaniryatjvālāvalīpallavitāntarikṣaḥ  |
abhūtpravṛttaḥ samaro mahāhi vidyādharendrāsurapuṅgavānām  || 17.9 ||

vīreṣu dhāvatsu caratsvamandaṃ gajeṣu valgatsu turaṅgameṣu  |
akālakalpātyayayaśāṅkitāni cakampire sapta rasātalān  || 17.10 ||

prakāśayantaḥ karaṇaprapanncaṃ surāṅganābhiḥ spṛhayekṣyamāṇāḥ  |
atyadbhutaṃ nṛttamivārabhanta bhaṭā raṇaprāṅgaṇaraṅgamadhye  || 17.11 ||

utplutya vegātpavamānamārgaṃ vidyādharairdānajalāvilāni  |
paṭu vyapāṭyanta mataṅgajānāṃ tīkṣṇāsipatrakrakacaiḥ śirāṃsi  || 17.12 ||

nipatya kumbheṣu mahāgajānāṃ nīrandhramukteṣu paṭu kvaṇantaḥ  |
bhānti sma vidyādharapuṅgavānāṃ muktāṭṭahāsā iva khaḍgapaṭṭāḥ  || 17.13 ||

āvartatāmūrmilatāyamāna nistriṃśavallīvalayākulāsu  |
prapedire pūrvasurojjhitāni cakrāṇi vidyādharavāhinīṣu  || 17.14 ||

phaṇāvalīṣvāpatitoragāṇāmaphalguratnopalakarkaśāsu  |
kṛpāṇadharā masṛṇībabhūva mahāsurāṇāṃ na tu saṅgarecchā  || 17.15 ||

amānti pātālatale prasasruḥ kṣitau biladvāravinirgatāni  |
jyāśabdahṛṣṭāsurasiṃhanāda turaṅgaheṣāgajabṛṃhitāni  || 17.16 ||

pradhāvadaśvīyakhurāhatānāmabhyudgato ratnabhuvāṃ parāgaḥ  |
gīrvāṇacāpacchavilāñchitāni kṛtsnāni cakre kakubhāṃ mukhāni  || 17.17 ||

bhaṭāstrapūrṇaiḥ parito gajānāṃ svadānapaṅkopacitaiḥ padāṅkaiḥ  |
bhayaṅkarābhūttaruṇārkabimba sahasrakīrṇeva raṇāṅgaṇorvī  || 17.18 ||

lūnāḥ samūlaṃ subhaṭāsipatraiḥ sahasraśaḥ śoṇitaśīkarārdrāḥ  |
uttālavaivasvatatālavṛnta vicchittimūhuḥ karikarṇatālāḥ  || 17.19 ||

anyonyakṛttāstarasā bhaṭānāṃ navātapātāmranakhatviṣo'gre  |
bhujā nipetuḥ samamīkṣaṇena samauliratnā iva pannagendrāḥ  || 17.20 ||

aṅgādasiprāsapṛṣatkabhinnātasṛkpravāheṣu bhṛśaṃ vahatsu  |
līlāgaladgairikanirjharāṇāṃ gajairjagāhe kulaparvatānām  || 17.21 ||

kṣmāyāṃ babhuḥkhaḍgapṛthakkṛtāni sahasraśaḥ śastrabhṛtāṃ śirāṃsi  |
kālena saṅgrāmasarontarālādutkhaṇḍitānīva saroruhāṇi  || 17.22 ||

kabandhakaṇṭhocchaladasravantaḥ samīpametyopari kaṅkayūtham  |
muhūrtamiddhāruṇaratnadaṇḍamadṛśyata cchatramivāntakasya  || 17.23 ||

muktaiḥ samūhena nabhaścarāṇāṃ kumbheṣu cakraiḥ kariṇāṃ patadbhiḥ  |
astācalavyāyatavaprapāti pataṅgabimbānukṛtirvitene  || 17.24 ||

patadbhaṭaṃ nirdalitāśvavāraṃ nikṛttamattebhakaraṃ kṣaṇena  |
vyadhāyi vidyādharasainikaistatsanāgavīrairasurendrasainyam  || 17.25 ||

haṃsairiva smerataṭāḥsamantātparicyutaiḥ kuñjarakarṇaśaṅkhaiḥ  |
voḍhuṃ pravṛttā bhavaduttaraṅgā jhaṭityagādhā rudhirasravantī  || 17.26 ||
vidyādharavyālabhaṭāvalupta dhairyeṣu naśyatsu mahāsureṣu  |
viśvāṅkuśo nāma surārisūnuratho rathenājimahīṃ viveśa  || 17.27 ||

ākarṇakṛṣtāddhanuṣaḥ patadbhirbālendulekhākṛtibhiḥ pṛṣatkaiḥ  |
arātisainye raṇadhīravīrān bhīrūnivaiko vimukhīcakāra  || 17.28 ||

tadvīryanirvāsitasauṣṭhavānāṃ vidyādharāṇāmapatan karebhyaḥ  |
dhārāgralagnadvipakumbhamuktāḥ sabāṣpaleśā iva khaḍgalekhāḥ  || 17.29 ||

kṣaṇādvalanti sma tadā hatāni balāni vidyādharapannagānām  |
saritpayāṃsīva niśakarāṃśu pūrapravṛddhārṇavapīḍitāni  || 17.30 ||

na śekatustasya gatiṃ niroddhuṃ vidyādharendroragarājaputrau  |
ratnākarasyeva mahendrasahyā vasahyavegaṃ pralayotthitasya  || 17.31 ||

dordaṇḍakaṇḍūtimathāsya hartumudbhrūlataṃ bhūpatinā niyuktaḥ  |
javājjagāmājipathaṃ rathena ramāṅgadaḥkuṇḍalito'gracāpaḥ  || 17.32 ||

tathopalebhe samaronmukhasya nādena vṛddhiḥ śarajanmanāsya  |
cakre padaṃ bāṣpakaṇotkareṇa yathā kapoleṣvasurāṅganānām  || 17.33 ||

dūrātsuparvārisutaṃ rathena sa raṃhasā saṃmukhamāpatantam  |
rurodha taṃ bāṇaparaṃparābhiryaśobhaṭaḥ karṇamivendrasūnus || 17.34 ||

viśvāṅkuśaḥ satkavace mumoca vakṣaḥsthale haimamathāsya bāṇam  |
taṭe'ñjanaśyāmatanau mahādreḥ śātahradaṃ jyotirivāmbuvāhaḥ  || 17.35 ||

alakṣyasaṃdhānavimokṣapātān yaśobhaṭasyāttaruṣo'pi ropān  |
mūrcchāluṭhatsārathirāhatāśvo rathaḥ śaśaṃsāsuranandanasya  || 17.36 ||

vilāsakāñcīmatha kālarātrerudyatkrudhaḥ paddhatimantakasya  |
maurvīṃ pṛṣatkeṇa ramāṅgadasya ciccheda gīrvāṇaripostanūjaḥ  || 17.37 ||

sannābhibimbena mahājilakṣmyā vīraśriyo vibhramanūpureṇa  |
saṃrabhya rāhoriva cakrapāṇiḥ sa apyasya cakreṇa śiraścakarta  || 17.38 ||

nanarta vidyādharasundarīṇāṃ gaṇo nadannūpuramambare'tha  |
maulau śarakṣuṇṇaśirastraratne cikṣepa cāsyāmarapuṣpavṛṣṭim  || 17.39 ||

dṛṣṭe śirasyutphalite svasūnordiṅmulalīnāsu patākinīṣu  |
vajrāṅkuśāḥ saṃmukhamāpapāta patyurviśāmastagirerivārkaḥ  || 17.40 ||

madāmbuvarṣī samare'bhidhāvan reje gajastasya sahemakakṣyaḥ  |
bhinne'ntarāle pavamānanunnastamālanīlastaḍiteva meghaḥ  || 17.41 ||

patyuḥ prasādasmitarajjukṛṣṭā bhaṭā vivṛtyāsya puro babhūvuḥ  |
svajīvitānyājimukhe vihātumatyutsukā bhaṅgamalīmasāni  || 17.42 ||

āruhya candraḥ khamivābhirāmaṃ nakṣatramālābharaṇaṃ gajendram  |
devo'pi tadvairitamo niyantumatha pratasthe navasāhasāṅkaḥ  || 17.43 ||

ekatra pārśve śaśikhaṇḍanāmā vidyādharendro nṛpaterbabhūva  |
arātisenānalinīvanaika śītāṃśuranyatra yaśobhaṭaśca  || 17.44 ||

sphuratphaṇachatramaṇipratāna tejaśchaṭājarjaritāndhakāraḥ  |
aphalgurvīryaḥ phaṇabhṛtkumāro'pyagre'bhavattasya sa ratnacūḍaḥ  || 17.45 ||

vidhūtanistriṃśataraṅgitāni sabāṇacakrīkṛtakārmukāni  |
hatāvaśeṣāṇi puro'sya celurbalāni vidyādharapannagānām  || 17.46 ||

raktāsavakṣībasahastatāla vetālatālocchalitāṭṭahāsaḥ  |
mahābhaṭānāmasunirvyapekṣamanyonyamāvartata saṃparāyaḥ  || 17.47 ||

cakāśire śastrabhṛtāṃ śiraḥsu mithaḥ patantyaḥ karavālavallyaḥ  |
muktāḥ salīlāṃ tridaśāṅganābhirmālā ivendīvarapatramayyaḥ  || 17.48 ||

keṣāṃ cidūhuḥ kavacāni śobhāṃ kva citkva cillohitapāṭalāni  |
khelajjayaśrīcaraṇāravinda lākṣāraseneva navāṅkitāni  || 17.49 ||

hṛdipraviṣṭairaviśuddhimadbhirabhūdvyathā kāpi śaraiḥ pareṣām  |
durātmanāṃ sādhuguṇairivāgre phalena saṃyogamupeyivadbhiḥ  || 17.50 ||

parasparāpātajuṣāmasīnāṃ dhārācyutaḥ saṃyaticūrṇareṇuḥ  |
avāpa tāpiccharucirjayaśrī vilāsakālāñjanadhūlilīlām  || 17.51 ||

paryāyajātobhayasainyabhaṅga karālakolāhalakātarāṇām  |
surārividyādharasundarīṇāṃ doleva śokapramādāvabhūtām  || 17.52 ||

athāsurendradviradena vegādabhyutthitenodgatadānadhāram  |
madhyeraṇaṃ madhyamalokabharturjavānmadāndho jaghaṭe gajendraḥ  || 17.53 ||

mahebhayostatra śikhāchalena pratiprahāraṃ radajaḥ kṛṣānuḥ  |
kośeṣu vidutkapiśā muhūrtaṃ vyadhādivāṣṭāpadapatravallīḥ  || 17.54 ||

muhuḥ prajānāmadhipena gāḍhamākṛṣyamāṇasya śarāsanasya  |
dviṣadvadhārambhavidhau gabhīraḥ kreṅkārahuṅkāra ivoccacāra  || 17.55 ||

parisphuratkuṇḍalaghṛṣṭapuṅkhāstena prayuktāspṛthuvikrameṇa  |
pramṛṣṭakāntākucapatralekhe lekhārivakṣasyapatan pṛṣatkāḥ  || 17.56 ||

arātimukteṣu tatastanutrādvahnisphuliṅgeṣu samullasatsu  |
mūrtirbabhāse vasudhādhipasya niryatpratāpāgnikaṇachaṭeva  || 17.57 ||

hiraṇmayī pārthivabāṇapaṅktiratyunnate mūrdhni mahāsurasya  |
reje tarāmañjanaparvatasya lagneva tigmāṃśumayūkhamālā  || 17.58 ||

abhyudgatā bharturarātibāṇa kṣuṇṇendranīlāṅgadareṇurājiḥ  |
adṛśyatoddāmabhujāspadasya parākramāgneriva dhūmalekhā  || 17.59 ||

ramāṅgado'pyudbhrukuṭiḥ kṛtāstraṃ vīraṃ dviṣaḥ pārśvagataṃ nihatya  |
śarairalāvījjayavaijayantīṃ jyotsnāsitaṃ kīrtimivāsurasya  || 17.60 ||

adhaḥsthitoḍḍāmaravairipatti mukteṣu nirlūnaśarāsanajyaḥ  |
cikṣepa cakrāṇyatidīrghabāhuḥ saḥ kālarātreriva kaṅkaṇāni  || 17.61 ||

utplutya helāhatasaṃmukhārirvidyādharendro'pyasinā cakarta  |
jagajjayastambhamivoddhurasya suradviṣaḥ kāñcanaketudaṇḍam  || 17.62 ||

so ratnacūḍo'pi tathā bhuśuṇḍyā pipeṣa vairidviṣakumbhapīṭham  |
sitātapatratvamudañcadāpa yathāsya muktāphaladhūlijālam  || 17.63 ||

tuṅgaṃ dadhatkarkaśatāmabhīkaḥ śrīsindhurājadvipakumbhayugmam  |
payodharadvandvamivājilakṣmyāścakre'rdhacandrāṅkitamindraśatruḥ  || 17.64 ||

parasparāghaṭṭitadantakoṭi bhraṣṭāgniveśabhramamādadhanti  |
raṇājire lohitarañjitāni virejire kuñjaramauktikāni  || 17.65 ||

cikṣepa pṛthītilake surāriryāṃ yāmiṣuṃ kopakaṣāyitākṣaḥ  |
tāṃ tāṃ jayāśāmiva bāhuśālī śaraiḥ sastasyārdhapathe lulova  || 17.66 ||

tayostatheṣvāsaprakarṣa pratyuktakarṇārjunayorjayaśrīḥ  |
suvelaratnākarayorudagrā cakāra veleva gatāgatāni  || 17.67 ||

patyuḥ prajānāmasureśvaro'tha kirīṭamāṇikyacayaṃ jahāra  |
maṇiprasūnastabakapratānaṃ kalpadrumasyeva yugāntavātaḥ  || 17.68 ||

krodhādathārdhaśaśalāñchanasodareṇa bāṇena vāsavaripornavasāhasāṅkaḥ  |
ciccheda rāma iva viśravasaḥ sutasya pīnāṃsalolamaṇikuṇḍalamuttamāṅgam  || 17.69 ||

āsanmukhāni kakubhāmabhito'tha citra vāditranādalaharīmukharodarāṇi  |
devasya ca tridivapuṣpamayaṃ papāta mālyaṃ śirasyasuravairipurandhrimuktam  || 17.70 ||

lakṣmīpateḥ pṛthubhujadvayamārdrasāndra jyāghātalāñchitamalāñchitavikramasya  |
atyādarāgatajitorjitavairilakṣmī pādābjayāvakaniṣaktamivācakāśe  || 17.71 ||

tasyāgrataḥ kanakakuṇḍalatāḍyamāna gaṇḍasthalīlulitakuṅkumapatralekhāḥ  |
vidyādharoragakuraṅgadṛśaḥ pramoda sāndrocchaladhvani jagurjayamaṅgalāni  || 17.72 ||

smitvā yaśobhaṭakarārpitacāpayaṣṭirunmuktaratnakavacaḥ khacareśvareṇa  |
udbhinnamauktikanibhaśramavāribindurdevo mamārja mukhamaṃśukapallavena  || 17.73 ||

dattābhayopanatapauraśatārpyamāṇa ratnopadhāmatha sa ratnavatīṃ praviśya  |
taṃ saṃyugasphuṭaparīkṣitaśauryasāraṃ rājye ripoḥ phaṇikumārakamabhyaṣiñcat || 17.74 ||

mūrtaṃ manorathamivopavanātsakandamādāya tatkanakakokanadaṃ narendraḥ  |
ādātumīpsitamahīndrasuteti ratnamabhyutsukastadanu bhogavatīṃ pratasthe  || 17.75 ||

devaḥ sāhasiko'pyamandamurajadhvānānumeyotsavāmunnamraiḥ parito mahīṃ maṇigṛhairuttambhayantīmiva  |
tāmatyunnataratnatoraṇaśikhāpreṅkholamuktāphala prālambocchaladacchakāntinikarasmerāmavāpatpurīm  || 17.76 ||


iti  śrīmṛgāṅkadattasūnoḥ parimalāparanāmnaḥ padmaguptasya kṛtau navasāhasāṅkacarite mahākāvye hemakamalaharaṇo nāma saptadaśaḥ sargaḥ




************************************************


aṣṭādaśaḥ sargaḥ


phaṇirājadarśanam

taṃ viṣṭapatritayakaṇṭakadṛṣṭasāramabhyāgataṃ nṛpatimudgatagāḍhaharṣaḥ  |
pratyudyayāvadhipatiḥ phaṇināmanargha ratnārghapāṇiraśanairatha śaṅkhapālaḥ  || 18.1 ||


__________________________________________

purapraveśaḥ

ādāya sādaraphaṇīśvaradattamarghyamarghyaḥ satāṃ sa bahireva niveśya sainyam  |
devo'viśadvinayavān puramagrayāyi vidyādharādhiparamāṅgadaratnacūḍaḥ  || 18.2 ||

utsṛjya gītamasamāpya vilāsalāsyamaṅkādapāsya sahasā maṇivallakīṃ ca  |
atyunmanāstadavalokanakautukena vātāyanānyadhiruroha purandhrilokaḥ  || 18.3 ||

utkṣipya vepathumatā karapallavena vātāyanāgramaṇimauktikalājakāni  |
smitvaikayā sa vilasanmakarāvacūla līlālavāñcitavilocanamāluloke  || 18.4 ||

udyadvivṛttakaraveṇikayā nṛpendau tasmin smarollasitajṛmbhikayā kayā cit |
muktā muhurvibudhasindhukalindakanyā kirmīravārilaharīsuhṛdaḥ kaṭākṣāḥ  || 18.5 ||

vācālaratnavalayā savilāsamasminnikṣipya kāpi navamauktikalājamuṣṭim  |
tattāḍitāṃsataṭapārthivadattadṛṣṭirdīrghekṣaṇā kimiva na trapayā cakāra  || 18.6 ||

vakṣo dadhānamamarādriśilāviśālamājānubāhumavalokya narendracandram  |
cittopanītaparirambhasukhātisāndramanyā payodharabhare pulakaṃ babhāra  || 18.7 ||

ālokya darpaṇatale pratimāgataṃ tam ’ātto mayaiṣa' iti kāpi kṛtotsavābhūt |
mugdhā gate'tha purato'tra tadīyabimbe śūnyātmadarśavidhurendumukhī babhūva  || 18.8 ||

ityāpatanmadanabāṇaparamparāṇāmunmīlitāṅgavalanaślathamekhalānām  |
eṇīdṛśāṃ vicarati sma sa rājahaṃsaḥ pārollasannavarasormiṣu mānaseṣu  || 18.9 ||


__________________________________________

nāyakavarṇanam

saṅgītaveśmani phaṇīśvaracāraṇānāṃ gīteṣvajasramiha śuśruma yadyaśāṃsi  |
yātaḥ sa eva nayanātithitāmayaṃ naḥ puṇyairaho bata nṛpo navasāhasāṅkaḥ  || 18.10 ||

kāntichaṭāchuritadiktaṭa eṣa devo jīyājjaganti paramārakulapradīpaḥ  |
unmūlya saṃprati surāritamaḥ samūlaṃ yenāhiviṣṭapatale vihitaḥ prakāśaḥ  || 18.11 ||

hantaiṣa pannagapateratulapratijñā prāgbhārasāgarasamuttaraṇaikapotaḥ  |
utpākameṣa ca phalaṃ phaṇirājakanyā citte ciraṃ kṛtapadasya manorathasya  || 18.12 ||

etadyaśobhaṭakare kanakāmbujaṃ tallīlāvataṃsamacirādviracayya yena  |
pratyuptakalpatarupallavameṣa pāṇimādāsyate nṛpatiradya śaśiprabhāyāḥ  || 18.13 ||


__________________________________________

hāṭakeśvaradarśanam


sāndrānurāgapiśunāḥ paraśuḥ pareṣāmākarṇayanniti saḥ paurajanasya vācaḥ  |
śrīhāṭakeśvara iti prathitasya tuṅgamagre dadarśa maṇimandiramindumauleḥ  || 18.14 ||
[syntax kulakam]

tatra praviśya sakṛtānatirādidevamānarca kalpaviṭapiprabhavaiḥ prasūnaiḥ  |
stotuṃ kṛtāñjalipuṭaḥ kuṭajāvadāta dantāṃśupallavitavāgupacakrame ca  || 18.15 ||


__________________________________________

hāṭakeśvarastutiḥ

antarjatāpihitasomasurāpagāya pracchannaśaraśāsanalocanāya  |
tīvravrataglapitaśailasutāsvarūpa vijñānanarmapaṭave baṭave namaste  || 18.16 ||


atyādarānatasurāsuramauliratna nānāmarīcikhacitāṅghrisaroruhāya  |
dehārdhavartigirijāvihitābhyasūya sandhyāpraṇāmaviṣamāñjalaye namaste  || 18.17 ||

nīrandhrasindhujalasiktakapālamukta ratnāṅkurasya karaṇīṃ vidhurātanoti  |
maulau sadaiva bhavato bhavabhedakarturnirdagdhabhāskaramahāya namo'stu tasmai  || 18.18 ||

kandarpadarpaśamanāya kṛtāntahartre kartre śubhasya bhujagādhipaveṣṭanāya  |
urvīmarudraviniśākaravahnitoya yājyāmbaroccavapuṣe supuṣe namaste  || 18.19 ||

nīrandhrabhūtidhavalāya gajendrakṛtti saṃvītadehakavalīkṛtapannagāya  |
nirdagdhadānavakulāya vipatkṣayaika kāryāya kāraṇanutāya namo'stu tubhyam  || 18.20 ||

te te yameva kila vāṅmayasāgarasya pāraṃ gatāḥ praṇavamātmavido vadanti  |
tasmai samāhitamaharṣivinidrahṛdya hṛtpuṇḍarīkavihitasthitaye namaste  || 18.21 ||

uttaṃsitenduśakalāya kapālajūṭa saṅghaṭṭitormimukharāmbaranirjharāya  |
bhasmāṅgarāgaśucaye vikacopavīta vyālendumaulimaṇidīdhitaye namaste  || 18.22 ||

nāstraṃ na bhasma na jaṭā na kapāladāma nenduḥ siddhataṭinī na phaṇīndrahāraḥ  |
nokṣā viṣaṃ na dayitāpi na yatra rūpamavyaktamīśa kila taddadhate namaste  || 18.23 ||


__________________________________________

nāgarājagamanam


stutvetyavantipatirindukulāvataṃsaṃ tanmandirātsahacaraiḥ saha nirjagāma  |
antarniveśitaharinmaṇivedi valgannāgāṅganaṃ saḥ phaṇirājagṛhaṃ jagāma  || 18.24 ||

tatrāvatīrya rathataḥ sa ramāṅgadātta pāṇiḥ samucchalitamaṅgalatūryaghoṣe  |
unnidrasāndrakusumaprakarāvakīrṇa māṇikyakuṭṭimatale masṛṇaṃ viveśa  || 18.25 ||

anyonyapallavitatadvijayapraśaṃsaḥ prāptasthitirvikaṭakāñcanaviṣṭareṣu  |
padmachadāyatadṛśā dadṛśe'tha tasminnekatra tena militaḥ phaṇirājalokaḥ  || 18.26 ||


__________________________________________

tatkṛtaḥ satkāraḥ

tasmin gate nayanagocaramuddhṛtārau baddhāñjalirjhaṭiti pannagarājasaṃsat |
mandākinīva parito hariṇāvacūḍa vyālokakuḍmalitakāñcanapaṅkajābhūt || 18.27 ||

nyañcacchikhābharaṇabhāsurapadmarāga rocichaṭāghaṭitatatphaṇaratnakāntiḥ  |
rājanyamaulimaṇicumbitapādapīṭhastasmai cakāra sa mahābhijanaḥ praṇāmam  || 18.28 ||

pratyuptaratnamabhitaḥ pramadāvakīrṇaṃ muktvā catuṣkamuragendranideśitaṃ sa  |
adhyāsta sādarajaratphaṇikalpitāśīstanmadhyavartikanakāsanamunnatāṃsaḥ  || 18.29 ||

vatsāṃ vrajanāya mameti śanairvisṛjya nepathyanīlamaṇiveśmani ratnacūḍam  |
tatrāsanadvayamadāpayadasya pārśve vidyādharādhipayaśobhaṭayoḥ phaṇīndraḥ  || 18.30 ||

svarṇāsane svayamathācchaphaṇātapatra ratnapradīpaśatajarjaritāndhakāraḥ  |
lokatrayaikatilakasya sa nātidūre devasya dāritamahendraripornyaṣīdat || 18.31 ||

sthitvaikato yuvatimaṅgalagītimatra śṛṇvan sa vindhyataṭadṛṣṭacaraḥ kuraṅgaḥ  |
citre niveśita ivātha yaśobhaṭena smitvā savismayamasūcyata pārthivāya  || 18.32 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhādarśanam

atrāntare pramadaloladṛśā nṛpeṇa dūrādadarśi phaṇirājasutābhiyāntī  |
tanvī śirīśasumanaḥsukumāramūrtirdevasya kārmukalateva manobhavasya  || 18.33 ||

jyotsnāsitāmbararucisnapitānanendurmātrācirodgatayavāṅkurakarṇapūram  |
muktvojjvalaṃ lalitakautukakaṅkaṇaṃ ca veṣaṃ vivāhasamayocitamudvahantī  || 18.34 ||

sakhyā kayāpi likhitaṃ madanānalaika dhūmāvalīvalayasaṃśayamarpayantam  |
ekāntakāntamasitāgarupatrabhaṅgamābibhratī lavalipāṇḍutale kapole  || 18.35 ||

āttaprasādhanamanaṅgavilāsaveśma līlāvidhānamavadhirnayanotsavasya  |
lāvaṇyasaṃvalitamaṅgakamudvahantī śṛṅgāradugdhajaladheradhidevateva  || 18.36 ||

sā pāṭalāvidhutacāmaramāruteṣatvyānartitālakalatā sahitā sakhībhiḥ  |
nātisphuṭakvaṇitanūpuramākulāni kiṃcidvilambya dadhatī trapayā padāni  || 18.37 ||


__________________________________________

nāyikayā nāyakadarśanam

utpakṣmaṇā nirupamollasitapramoda vistāralaṅghitavilāsasaroruheṇa  |
sāndrasmarajvarapipāsitayā tayāpi dūrādapāyi nayanāñjalinā narendraḥ  || 18.38 ||


__________________________________________

mālyavatīvākyam

vrīḍāvanamramukhapadmamupāgatāyāṃ tasyāṃ pituḥ kanakaviṣṭarabhāgabhāji  |
mālyādikalpitayathocitasatkriyānte taṃ mālavendramiti mālyavatī jagāda  || 18.39 ||

rājan! mahītalamṛgāṅga! vilambase kim? adyāpi tūrṇamamunā svabhujārjitena  |
hemāmbujena viracayya vataṃsamasyāḥ pūrṇapratijñamuragādhipatiṃ vidhehi  || 18.40 ||


__________________________________________

kamalāvataṃsaḥ

ukte tayetyakṛta kāñcanapuṣkaraṃ tadyāvatsaḥ karṇaśikhare phaṇirājaputryāḥ  |
tāvadvihāya mṛgarūpamudāramūrtiragre babhūva puruṣo'sya sahemavetraḥ  || 18.41 ||


__________________________________________

puruṣaṃ prati praśnaḥ

kastvaṃ mṛgaḥ kathamabhūriti pārthivena pṛṣṭaḥ sa vismayasamutsukamānasena  |
ityabravīduraganetraparamparābhirāpīyamānavapuruktimavantinātham  || 18.42 ||


__________________________________________

pratīhārasya vṛttāntaḥ

kailāsaśailavasatergiriśoparodhāddvārapraveśaviniṣedhakaṣāyitena  |
śapto'smi kaṇvamunināyamahaṃ pituste śrīharṣadevanṛpateḥ pratihārapālas || 18.43 ||

rājā phaṇīndraduhituḥ kanakāravindaṃ karṇe kariṣyati yadā navasāhasāṅkaḥ  |
svaṃ rūpamāpsyasi tadeti samādideśa śāpāntameṣa vihitānunayo maharṣiḥ  || 18.44 ||

tadvāsavārivijayotthamidaṃ yaśaste gatvaikapiṅgalagireravataṃsayāmi  |
uktveti divyakusumairavakīrya maulau pātālamallamanilasya pathā jagāma  || 18.45 ||


__________________________________________

vivāhavidhiḥ

tūryasvaneṣu vilasatsu paṭhatsvamandaṃ bandiṣvanīyata phaṇīndrapurodhasā ca  |
koṇāvasaktajalapūritaratnakumbhāṃ vediṃ tayā saha sa madhyamalokapālaḥ  || 18.46 ||

abhyudgatārciranalojjhitadhūmarāji śyāmībhavatkanakatāmarasāvataṃsām  |
tasyāṃ yathāvidhi sa mālavapuṣpaketuḥ kanyāmaheḥ kuvalayāśva iva upayeme  || 18.47 ||

ānītayā jhaṭiti rūpamadṛṣṭapūrvamaṅgena puṣpaśarabhaṅgitaraṅgitena  |
bhāti sma śantanuriva tridivasravantyā pātālacandrakalayā sastayā sametya  || 18.48 ||


__________________________________________

phaṇipativākyam

nirgacchadaccharucinirbharamaṃśukena saṃchāditaṃ kimapi pāṇitale dadhānaḥ  |
ūce tamityadhipatiḥ phaṇināmudañcatdantāṃśuśāritaradachadaratnakāntiḥ  || 18.49 ||

yaddīyate tava na tādṛśamasti kiṃcidgehe mamātra nṛpate navasāhasāṅka !  |
kośapratiṣṭhitanidhānaśataṃ yatastvāmaiśvaryanirjitapurandaramāmananti  || 18.50 ||

tatsphāṭikaṃ svamiva śuddhamidaṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṣṭṛprayatnaghaṭitaṃ śivaliṅgamekam  |
ākāramardhavanitāvapuṣaḥ purāreryasyāntare sukṛtino hi vilokayanti  || 18.51 ||

vyāsaḥ purā kila purāṇamuneḥ prapede tasmātkilādikavipāṇitalaṃ jagāma  |
lebhe tato'pi bhagavān kapilo maharṣiḥ sānugraheṇa mama cedamadāyi tena  || 18.52 ||


__________________________________________

śivaliṅgārpaṇam

uktvetyanarghamatipāvanamarpitaṃ tadantaḥsphuṭaikaśivarūpamahīśvareṇa  |
pūrṇendukānti sahasā nigṛhītaśatrurjagrāha piṇḍitamiva svayaśo narendraḥ  || 18.53 ||

tatrātha diktaṭapariskhalitapravṛtta sīmantinīcaṭulanūpurakāñcinādaḥ  |
ko'pyucchalatpaṭahavaṃśahuḍḍukkaśaṅkha vīṇāmṛdaṅgamurajadhvanirutsavo'bhūt || 18.54 ||


__________________________________________

svanagarīṃ prati prasthāpanam

vṛtte vadhūmatha vivāhamahotsave tāmādāya niṣpratimapauruṣavaijayantīm  |
anvāgatādaranivartitapannagendraḥ paryutsukaḥ svanagarīṃ sa nṛpaḥ pratasthe  || 18.55 ||

gatvātha dūramahiviṣṭapataḥ sahelamaṃśaḥ purāṇapuruṣasya sa nirjagāma  |
śiprārpitena sahasā purataḥ prabhāva sīmantitāmbupaṭalena pathā sasainyaḥ  || 18.56 ||

tasyāḥ svahastamunisaṃhatikalpitārghaḥ sindhostaṭe saḥ padamekapade cakāra  |
śṛṅge tadā ca bhagavānaravindabandhurbandhūkapāṭalaruciḥ kanakācalasya  || 18.57 ||


__________________________________________

ujjayinīpraveśaḥ

bālātapachuritaharmyaviṭaṅkavarti pārāvatātimadhuradhvanitachalena  |
sambhāṣaṇaṃ vidadhatīmiva pauramukta puṣpāñjaliḥ saḥ puramujjayinīṃ viveśa  || 18.58 ||

kāntāyaśobhaṭayutaṃ kṛśatāmavāptāstaccintayaiva sacivāstamatha praṇemuḥ  |
kākutsthamāhatasurārimivānuyāntaṃ saumitriṇā janakarājatanūjayā ca  || 18.59 ||


__________________________________________

mahākāleśvaradarśanam

ānandabāṣpasalilārdradṛśo'rdhamārge sambhāṣya tān smitamukhaḥ saha tairjagāma  |
vidyādharoragakarāhatahemaghaṇṭā ṭāṅkārahāri bhavanaṃ tripurāntakasya  || 18.60 ||

tasmiṃścarācaragurorhariṇāvacūla cūḍāmaṇerapacitiṃ vidhivadvidhāya  |
sākaṃ phaṇīndrasutayāmbararodhikambu tūryasvanormi saśca rājakulaṃ viveśa  || 18.61 ||


__________________________________________

dhārāgamanam

tatrārṇavadhvanighanotsavatūryaghoṣe sthitvā dināni katicitsa narendracandraḥ  |
yāti sma bhūṣitakulaḥ kularājadhānīṃ dhārāmamātyakathitāmṛgayetivṛttaḥ  || 18.62 ||

udghāṭiteṣvatha vilokanakautukena vātāyaneṣu paritaḥ purasundarībhiḥ  |
tasmiṃścirādviśati jīva iveśvare sā pronmīlitorunayaneva purī babhūva  || 18.63 ||


__________________________________________

śivaliṅgapratiṣṭhā

tatsādhvakārayadathādhigatapratiṣṭhaṃ tatrāccharatnaśivaliṅgamanarghaśīlaḥ  |
tasya prabhāvaghaṭitairvyadhurarhaṇāṃ ca vidyādharā vikacakalpataruprasūnaiḥ  || 18.64 ||


__________________________________________

anuyāyiprasthānam

kṛtvā yathocitamakṛtrimamutsavānte satkāramāyatananiślathamauliratnau  |
dattāṅkapāṇirubhayoḥ prajighāya sa atha vidyādharādhipaphaṇīndrasūtau svadeśam  || 18.65 ||

ekastayoragamadambaragāmisainya sīmantitābhrapaṭalaḥ śaśikāntaśailam  |
anyo'pyagādhajalamālavajahnukanyā viśrāṇitorusaraṇirnijarājadhānīm  || 18.66 ||


__________________________________________

śaśiprabhāsakhīgamanam

mābhūḥ kadāpi vimukhī rameṇa yadasya chandānuvṛttiratisaṃvananaṃ madasya  |
uktveti tāmahisutāmagaman gṛhāṇi gandharvakinnaramahoragasiddhakanyāḥ  || 18.67 ||


__________________________________________

sāmrājyalakṣmīsvīkāraḥ

nīlachatrāvataṃsā bhujagapatisutāpāṇḍugaṇḍasthalāntaḥ kastūrīpaṅkapatravyatikaraśabalavyāyatāṃse salīlam  |
devenātha svamantripravaraciradhṛtā sāhasāṅkena dīrghe rohajjyāghātarekhe puno'pi nidadhe doṣṇi sāmrājyalakṣmīḥ  || 18.68 ||


__________________________________________

atha granthapraśastiḥ

śrīmatkavipriyasuhṛcchaladaṅkarāma rājendubhaktyadhigatapratibhāviśeṣaḥ  |
etadvinidrakusumadadyuti padmaguptaḥ śrīsindhurājanṛpateścaritaṃ babandha  || gp.1 ||

lakṣmīlatānavavasanta mahītalendra vidyāvilāsamaṇidarpaṇa sindhurāja  |
etanmayā ghaṭitamujjvalakānti kāvya māṇikyakuṇḍalamiha śravaṇe videhi  || gp.2 ||

nyastāni yāni mayi sūktisudhāpṛṣanti devena tena kati citkavibāndhavena  |
candrātapasnapitamauktikasodarāṇāṃ teṣāmidaṃ vilasitaṃ navasāhasāṅka  || gp.3 ||

yaccāpalaṃ kimapi mandhadhiyā mayaivamāsūtritaṃ narapate navasāhasāṅka  |
ājñaiva heturiha te śayanīkṛtogra rājanyamaulikusumā na kavitvadarpaḥ  || gp.4 ||

iti navasāhasāṅkacaritaṃ saṃpūrṇam

